FILE: scripts\traceFunction2479.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 2479;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next + mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next + name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;
      functions++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

function decode(func) {

  let p = func.start;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  while (p < func.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = null;

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {

        let value = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {

          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);

          value |=
            (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if ((b & 0x80n) === 0n) break;

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        text = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x77:
        text = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x78:
        text = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {

        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        const name =
          op === 0x02
            ? "block"
            : op === 0x03
              ? "loop"
              : "if";

        text =
          `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;

          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);

        text =
          `br_table ${labels.join(",")}`;

        break;
      }

      default:

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode ${hex(op)}`
        );

        console.log(
          "RAW REMAINING : " +
          Buffer.from(
            bytes.subarray(at, func.end)
          ).toString("hex")
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
  }
}

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

console.log("FUNCTION 2479 TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

console.log(
  `WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`
);

console.log("");

const sections = readSections();

const importSec =
  sections.find(x => x.id === 2);

const functionSec =
  sections.find(x => x.id === 3);

const codeSec =
  sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!importSec || !functionSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error(
    "Required WASM sections not found"
  );
}

const importedCount =
  parseImports(importSec);

const functionTypes =
  parseFunctionTypes(functionSec);

const funcs =
  parseCode(
    codeSec,
    functionTypes,
    importedCount
  );

const target =
  funcs.find(
    x => x.funcIndex === TARGET
  );

if (!target) {
  throw new Error(
    `Function ${TARGET} not found`
  );
}

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET}`);

decode(target);

console.log("");

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

console.log("END");

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 2499;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB too long");
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB too long");
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];
    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;
  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = bytes[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(`Unsupported type form at ${hex(p - 1)}`);
    }

    const pc = uleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(bytes[p++]);
    }

    const rc = uleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(bytes[p++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }

  return types;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;
  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next;

    p += mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next;

    p += name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      functions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const lim = bytes[p++];

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (lim & 1) {
        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const lim = bytes[p++];

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (lim & 1) {
        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

function decode(func) {
  let p = func.start;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  while (p < func.end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = null;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text = `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {
        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        let name =
          op === 0x02 ? "block" :
          op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
          "if";

        text = `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);

        text = `br_table ${labels.join(",")}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        let v = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {
          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);
          v |= (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if ((b & 0x80n) === 0n) break;

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${v}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text = `${names[op]} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const b = uleb(p);
        p = b.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text = `${names[op]} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
        break;
      }

      default: {
        const simple = {
          0x45: "i32.eqz",
          0x46: "i32.eq",
          0x47: "i32.ne",
          0x48: "i32.lt_s",
          0x49: "i32.lt_u",
          0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
          0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
          0x4c: "i32.le_s",
          0x4d: "i32.le_u",
          0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
          0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
          0x6a: "i32.add",
          0x6b: "i32.sub",
          0x6c: "i32.mul",
          0x71: "i32.and",
          0x72: "i32.or",
          0x73: "i32.xor",
          0x74: "i32.shl",
          0x75: "i32.shr_s",
          0x76: "i32.shr_u",
          0x77: "i32.rotl",
          0x78: "i32.rotr"
        };

        text = simple[op];

        if (!text) {
          console.log(`${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode ${hex(op)}`);
          console.log(
            "RAW REMAINING : " +
            Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(at, func.end)).toString("hex")
          );
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");

const sections = readSections();

const typeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const importSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
const functionSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!typeSec || !importSec || !functionSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

const types = parseTypes(typeSec);
const importedCount = parseImports(importSec);
const functionTypes = parseFunctionTypes(functionSec);
const funcs = parseCode(codeSec, functionTypes, importedCount);

const target = funcs.find(x => x.funcIndex === TARGET);

if (!target) {
  throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET} not found`);
}

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET}`);
decode(target);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction259.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 259;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB too long at 0x" + pos.toString(16));
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readSLEB(pos, bits = 32) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < bits && (b & 0x40))
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > bits + 7)
            throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections() {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        p = sizeR.next;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start: p,
            end: p + sizeR.value
        });

        p += sizeR.value;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseFunctionSection(section) {
    const types = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    p = countR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        types.push(r.value);
        p = r.next;
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCodeSection(section, functionTypes) {
    const bodies = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    p = countR.next;

    const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = sizeR.value;
        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localsR = readULEB(p);
        p = localsR.next;

        for (let g = 0; g < localsR.value; g++) {
            const nR = readULEB(p);
            p = nR.next;
            p++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function memarg(p) {
    const align = buf[p++];

    const off = readULEB(p);

    return {
        align,
        offset: off.value,
        next: off.next
    };
}

function decode(body) {

    let p = body.codeStart;

    while (p < body.codeEnd) {

        const offset = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let text;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x00:
                text = "unreachable";
                break;

            case 0x01:
                text = "nop";
                break;

            case 0x02: {
                const t = buf[p++];
                text = `block type=0x${t.toString(16)}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const t = buf[p++];
                text = `loop type=0x${t.toString(16)}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const t = buf[p++];
                text = `if type=0x${t.toString(16)}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;

                const labels = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    labels.push(r.value);
                }

                const d = readULEB(p);
                p = d.next;

                text =
                    `br_table [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${d.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                text = "return";
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `call ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const a = readULEB(p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(p);
                p = b.next;

                text =
                    `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x1b:
                text = "select";
                break;

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2c: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2e: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2f: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x38: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `f32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x39: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `f64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x3a: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x3b: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;
                text =
                    `i32.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const r = readSLEB(p, 32);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = readSLEB(p, 64);
                p = r.next;
                text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x48:
                text = "i32.lt_s";
                break;

            case 0x49:
                text = "i32.lt_u";
                break;

            case 0x4a:
                text = "i32.gt_s";
                break;

            case 0x4b:
                text = "i32.gt_u";
                break;

            case 0x4c:
                text = "i32.le_s";
                break;

            case 0x4d:
                text = "i32.le_u";
                break;

            case 0x4e:
                text = "i32.ge_s";
                break;

            case 0x4f:
                text = "i32.ge_u";
                break;

            case 0x50:
                text = "i64.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x51:
                text = "i64.eq";
                break;

            case 0x52:
                text = "i64.ne";
                break;

            case 0x53:
                text = "i64.lt_s";
                break;

            case 0x54:
                text = "i64.lt_u";

            case 0x55:
                text = "i64.gt_s";

                break;

            case 0x5a:
                text = "i64.ge_u";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x6c:
                text = "i32.mul";
                break;

            case 0x6d:
                text = "i32.div_s";
                break;

            case 0x6e:
                text = "i32.div_u";
                break;

            case 0x6f:
                text = "i32.rem_s";
                break;

            case 0x70:
                text = "i32.rem_u";
                break;

            case 0x71:
                text = "i32.and";
                break;

            case 0x72:
                text = "i32.or";
                break;

            case 0x73:
                text = "i32.xor";
                break;

            case 0x74:
                text = "i32.shl";
                break;

            case 0x75:
                text = "i32.shr_s";
                break;

            case 0x76:
                text = "i32.shr_u";
                break;

            case 0x77:
                text = "i32.rotl";
                break;

            case 0x78:
                text = "i32.rotr";
                break;

            case 0x79:
                text = "i64.clz";
                break;

            case 0x7a:
                text = "i64.ctz";
                break;

            case 0x7b:
                text = "i64.popcnt";
                break;

            case 0x7c:
                text = "i64.add";

            case 0x7d:
                text = "i64.sub";
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                text = "i64.mul";

            default:

                console.log(

                console.log(
                    "RAW REMAINING : " +

                return;

        console.log(`${hex(offset)}  ${text}`);

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1493c;
const END   = 0x14a20;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - LOCAL 9 TRACE 1493C..14A20 v26");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`RANGE     : 0x${START.toString(16)} - 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid SLEB32");
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 1n << shift;
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid SLEB64");
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n = 28) {
    return Array.from(
        wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, END))
    )
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function showMemory(
    addr: number,
    name: string,
    p: number
) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    console.log(
        `${name.padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
        `align=${align.value} offset=${hex8(offset.value)}`
    );

    console.log(`             RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

    return p;
}

let p = START;
let local9Count = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local.get / set / tee
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        const name =
            op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
            op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
            "local.tee";

        if (r.value === 9) {
            local9Count++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `******** LOCAL 9 @ ${hex8(addr)} ********`
            );
            console.log(`${name} 9`);
            console.log(`RAW: ${raw(addr, 36)}`);
        } else {
            console.log(
                `${name.padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)} local=${r.value}`
            );
        }

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // constants
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = readSLEB32(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `i32.const    @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
            `value=${r.value} (${hex8(r.value)})`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {

        const r = readSLEB64(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `i64.const    @ ${hex8(addr)} value=${r.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // memory
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x28) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i32.load", p);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i64.load", p);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i32.load8_u", p);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2f) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i32.load16_u", p);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i32.store", p);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        p = showMemory(addr, "i64.store", p);
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // control flow
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {

        const r = readSLEB32(p);
        p = r.next;

        const name =
            op === 0x02 ? "block" :
            op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
            "if";

        console.log(
            `${name.padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
            `type=${r.value === 64 ? "empty" : r.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
        console.log(`else         @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(`end          @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"}           ` +
            `@ ${hex8(addr)} depth=${r.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
        console.log(`return       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `call         @ ${hex8(addr)} function=${r.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // comparisons
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const compareNames: Record<number, string> = {
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u"
    };

    if (compareNames[op]) {
        console.log(
            `${compareNames[op].padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32 arithmetic / bitwise
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const arithmetic: Record<number, string> = {
        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",
        0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
        0x70: "i32.rem_u",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr"
    };

    if (arithmetic[op]) {
        console.log(
            `${arithmetic[op].padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x1b) {
        console.log(`select       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // unknown
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `STOP @ ${hex8(addr)} UNKNOWN OPCODE=0x${
            op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        }`
    );

    console.log(`RAW: ${raw(addr, 40)}`);
    break;
}

console.log(" SUMMARY");

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x14555;
const END   = 0x1493c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - LOCAL 9 CONTROL-FLOW v23");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`RANGE     : 0x${START.toString(16)} - 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

// Known WASM control-flow opcodes
const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x36: "i32.store",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
};

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    return {
        value: 0xffffffff,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n = 12) {
    return Array.from(wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, END)))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

let p = START;
let local9Hits = 0;
let cfHits = 0;

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // local.get / set / tee
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        if (r.value === 9) {
            local9Hits++;

            const kind =
                op === 0x20 ? "local.get 9" :
                op === 0x21 ? "local.set 9" :
                "local.tee 9";

            console.log(
                `LOCAL9 @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${kind}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(16, END - addr))}`
            );
        }

        continue;
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `CALL   @ ${hex8(addr)}  function=${r.value}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(12, END - addr))}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        cfHits++;

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${names[op]}  blocktype=${r.value}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(12, END - addr))}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // else / end / return
    if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b || op === 0x0f) {
        cfHits++;

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${names[op]}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(12, END - addr))}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // br / br_if
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        cfHits++;

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${names[op]} depth=${r.value}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(12, END - addr))}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // br_table
    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        const count = r.value;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        p = readULEB(p).next;

        cfHits++;

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  br_table count=${count}  RAW: ${raw(addr, Math.min(24, END - addr))}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        // Only show loads involving local9.
        // We cannot know the stack operand here from opcode alone,
        // so the instruction is reported only as structural context.
        continue;
    }

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        continue;
    }

    // Generic known one-byte instruction.
    // Important: unknown opcodes are NOT treated as single-byte
    // instructions because that could desynchronize the decoder.
    if (names[op]) {
        continue;
    }

    // For this focused scan, stop rather than guessing instruction length.
    console.log(
        `STOP   @ ${hex8(addr)}  UNKNOWN OPCODE=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(`RAW: ${raw(addr, 24)}`);
    break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`LOCAL 9 EVENTS : ${local9Hits}`);
console.log(`CONTROL-FLOW    : ${cfHits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY: no TON connection");
console.log("READ ONLY: no wallet");
console.log("READ ONLY: no transaction");
console.log("READ ONLY: no signing");

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x14555;
const END   = 0x1493c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - LOCAL 9 CONTROL-FLOW v24");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`RANGE     : 0x${START.toString(16)} - 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        value: 0xffffffff,
        next: p
    };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let byte = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        byte = wasm[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n = 20) {
    return Array.from(
        wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, END))
    )
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

let p = START;

let local9Hits = 0;
let cfHits = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local.get / local.set / local.tee
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        if (r.value === 9) {

            local9Hits++;

            const name =
                op === 0x20 ? "local.get 9" :
                op === 0x21 ? "local.set 9" :
                "local.tee 9";

            console.log(
                `LOCAL9 @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${name}`
            );

            console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);
        }

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // control flow
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {

        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        cfHits++;

        const name =
            op === 0x02 ? "block" :
            op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
            "if";

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${name} blocktype=${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {

        cfHits++;

        console.log(`CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  else`);
        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {

        cfHits++;

        console.log(`CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  end`);
        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        cfHits++;

        console.log(
            `CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  ${
                op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"
            } depth=${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {

        cfHits++;

        console.log(`CF     @ ${hex8(addr)}  return`);
        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // call
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(
            `CALL   @ ${hex8(addr)}  function=${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(`        RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // constants
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // memory
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x28) {

        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {

        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32 arithmetic / bitwise
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    switch (op) {

        case 0x45: // eqz
        case 0x46: // eq
        case 0x47: // ne
        case 0x48: // lt_s
        case 0x49: // lt_u
        case 0x4a: // gt_s
        case 0x4b: // gt_u
        case 0x4c: // le_s
        case 0x4d: // le_u
        case 0x4e: // ge_s
        case 0x4f: // ge_u

        case 0x67: // clz
        case 0x68: // ctz
        case 0x69: // popcnt
        case 0x6a: // add
        case 0x6b: // sub
        case 0x6c: // mul
        case 0x6d: // div_s
        case 0x6e: // div_u
        case 0x6f: // rem_s
        case 0x70: // rem_u
        case 0x71: // and
        case 0x72: // or
        case 0x73: // xor
        case 0x74: // shl
        case 0x75: // shr_s
        case 0x76: // shr_u
        case 0x77: // rotl
        case 0x78: // rotr
            continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // select
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x1b) {
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Unknown
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        `STOP   @ ${hex8(addr)}  UNKNOWN OPCODE=0x${
            op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        }`
    );

    console.log(`RAW: ${raw(addr, 32)}`);
    break;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 259;

const TARGETS = [
    0x0025587c,
    0x00255880,
    0x00255884,
    0x00255888,
    0x0025588c,
    0x00255890,
    0x00255894,
    0x00255898,
    0x0025589c,
    0x002558a0,
    0x002558a4,
];

const TABLE = 0x002559ac;

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSections(b: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p++];

        const size = readULEB(b, p);
        p = size.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + size.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: size.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function getSection(sections: any[], id: number) {
    const s = sections.find(x => x.id === id);

    if (!s) {
        throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
    }

    return s;
}

function countImportedFunctions(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const ml = readULEB(b, p);
        p = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = readULEB(b, p);
        p = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {

            const t = readULEB(b, p);
            p = t.next;

            count++;

        } else if (kind === 1) {

            const flags = b[p++];

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const y = readULEB(b, p);
                p = y.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 2) {

            const flags = b[p++];

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const y = readULEB(b, p);
                p = y.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 3) {

            p += 2;

        } else if (kind === 4) {

            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;

            const y = readULEB(b, p);
            p = y.next;
        }
    }

    return count;
}

function getFunctionTypes(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const x = readULEB(b, p);
        p = x.next;

        result.push(x.value);
    }

    return result;
}

function getBodies(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const size = readULEB(b, p);
        p = size.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

        const locals = readULEB(b, p);
        p = locals.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {

            const count = readULEB(b, p);
            p = count.next;

            p += 1;
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart,
            size: size.value,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function name(op: number) {

    const names: Record<number, string> = {

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",

        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",

        0x10: "call",

        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode_${hex8(op)}`;
}

type Instr = {
    pos: number;
    abs: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
};

function decode(
    code: Buffer,
    codeStart: number
) {

    const result: Instr[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < code.length) {

        const start = p;
        const op = code[p++];

        try {

            let text = name(op);
            let imm: number | undefined;

            if (
                op === 0x20 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22 ||
                op === 0x23 ||
                op === 0x24
            ) {

                const x = readULEB(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                imm = x.value;
                text += ` ${x.value}`;

            } else if (op === 0x41) {

                const x = readSLEB(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                imm = x.value >>> 0;

                text +=
                    ` ${x.value} (${hex8(x.value)})`;

            } else if (op === 0x42) {

                const x = readSLEB(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                text += ` ${x.value}`;

            } else if (op === 0x10) {

                const x = readULEB(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                imm = x.value;

                text += ` function ${x.value}`;

            } else if (
                op >= 0x28 &&
                op <= 0x3e
            ) {

                const align = readULEB(code, p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(code, p);
                p = offset.next;

                imm = offset.value;

                text +=
                    ` align=${align.value}` +
                    ` offset=${hex8(offset.value)}`;

            } else if (
                op === 0x3f ||
                op === 0x40
            ) {

                p += 1;

            } else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

                const x = readULEB(code, p);
                p = x.next;

                imm = x.value;

                text += ` ${x.value}`;
            }

            result.push({
                pos: start,
                abs: codeStart + start,
                op,
                text,
                imm,
            });

        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function printWindow(
    ins: Instr[],
    address: number,
    radius = 28
) {

    const index = ins.findIndex(
        x => x.abs === address
    );

    if (index < 0) {
        console.log(
            `Target instruction not found: ${hex8(address)}`
        );
        return;
    }

    const a = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const z = Math.min(
        ins.length,
        index + radius + 1
    );

    for (let i = a; i < z; i++) {

        const x = ins[i];

        const marker =
            x.abs === address
                ? " >>> "
                : "     ";

        console.log(
            marker +
            `${hex8(x.abs)}  ${x.text}`
        );
    }
}

function targetText(
    x: Instr
) {

    if (
        x.op < 0x28 ||
        x.op > 0x3e ||
        x.imm === undefined
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    if (TARGETS.includes(x.imm)) {
        return `TARGET ${hex8(x.imm)}`;
    }

    if (x.imm === TABLE) {
        return `TABLE ${hex8(TABLE)}`;
    }

    return null;
}

async function main() {

    console.log(
        "Using file: traceFunction259StateWritesV29"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " FUNCTION 259 STATE WRITE / READ DATA-FLOW v29"
    );

    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const wasm =
        fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const sections =
        readSections(wasm);

    const imported =
        countImportedFunctions(
            wasm,
            getSection(sections, 2)
        );

    const types =
        getFunctionTypes(
            wasm,
            getSection(sections, 3)
        );

    const bodies =
        getBodies(
            wasm,
            getSection(sections, 10)
        );

    const bodyIndex =
        TARGET_FUNCTION - imported;

    const body =
        bodies[bodyIndex];

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNCTION} not found`
        );
    }

    const code =
        wasm.subarray(
            body.codeStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        );

    const ins =
        decode(
            code,
            body.codeStart
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTION");

    console.log(
        `real function index : ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body index          : ${bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `type index           : ${types[bodyIndex]}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code start           : ${hex8(body.codeStart)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body end             : ${hex8(body.bodyEnd)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `decoded instructions : ${ins.length}`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "TARGET STATE ACCESS MAP"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    for (const x of ins) {

        const t = targetText(x);

        if (t) {

            console.log(
                `${hex8(x.abs)}  ${x.text}`
            );

            console.log(
                `             ${t}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "ALL STATE AREA CONSTANTS"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    for (const x of ins) {

        if (
            x.op === 0x41 &&
            x.imm !== undefined
        ) {

            const v = x.imm >>> 0;

            if (
                TARGETS.includes(v) ||
                v === TABLE
            ) {

                console.log(
                    `${hex8(x.abs)}  i32.const ${hex8(v)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "WINDOW: 0x255880 FIRST READ"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    printWindow(
        ins,
        0x144ed,
        25
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "WINDOW: WRITE/READ STATE REGION"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const stateInstructions =
        ins.filter(x => {

            if (
                x.op !== 0x41 ||
                x.imm === undefined
            ) {
                return false;
            }

            const v = x.imm >>> 0;

            return TARGETS.includes(v);
        });

    for (const x of stateInstructions) {

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `STATE CONSTANT @ ${hex8(x.abs)}`
        );

        printWindow(
            ins,
            x.abs,
            10
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "DONE"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no TON"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no RPC"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no wallet"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no transaction"
    );

    console.log(
        "READ ONLY: no signing"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
}

    console.error("");

    console.error(err);

====================================================================================================
