const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 3;
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, b;
  while (true) {
    b = bytes[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function sections() {
  let p = 8;
  const out = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    out.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + x.value
    });

    p += x.value;
  }

  return out;
}

function importedFunctions(sec) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.next + a.value;

    const b = uleb(p);
    p = b.next + b.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      count++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function functionTypes(sec) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    out.push(x.value);
  }

  return out;
}

function codeFunctions(sec, types, imported) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    out.push({
      funcIndex: imported + i,
      typeIndex: types[i],
      start,
      end
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return out;
}

function memArg(p) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  p = a.next;

  const o = uleb(p);
  p = o.next;

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: p
  };
}

function decodeFC(p) {

  const x = uleb(p);
  p = x.next;

  let text = "";

  switch (x.value) {

    case 0:
      text = "i32.trunc_sat_f32_s";
      break;

    case 1:
      text = "i32.trunc_sat_f32_u";
      break;

    case 2:
      text = "i32.trunc_sat_f64_s";
      break;

    case 3:
      text = "i32.trunc_sat_f64_u";
      break;

    case 4:
      text = "i64.trunc_sat_f32_s";
      break;

    case 5:
      text = "i64.trunc_sat_f32_u";
      break;

    case 6:
      text = "i64.trunc_sat_f64_s";
      break;

    case 7:
      text = "i64.trunc_sat_f64_u";
      break;

    case 8: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      text = `memory.init ${a.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 9: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      text = `data.drop ${a.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 10: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const b = uleb(p);
      p = b.next;
      text = `memory.copy ${a.value} ${b.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 11: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      text = `memory.fill ${a.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 12: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const b = uleb(p);
      p = b.next;
      text = `table.init ${a.value} ${b.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 13: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      text = `elem.drop ${a.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 14: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const b = uleb(p);
      p = b.next;
      text = `table.copy ${a.value} ${b.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 15:
      text = "table.grow";
      break;

    case 16:
      text = "table.size";
      break;

    case 17:
      text = "table.fill";
      break;

    default:
      text = `0xfc ${x.value}`;
  }

  return { text, next: p };
}

function decode(func) {

  let p = func.start;

  const locals = uleb(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;
    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  while (p < func.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = "";

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {
        const bt = bytes[p++];

        const name =
          op === 0x02 ? "block" :
          op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
          "if";

        text = `${name} type=${hex(bt)}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        text =
          `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {

        const m = memArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {

        const m = memArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        text =
          `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {

        let value = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {

          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);

          value |=
            (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if (!(b & 0x80n))
            break;

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        text = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x77:
        text = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x78:
        text = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      case 0xfc: {
        const r = decodeFC(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = r.text;
        break;
      }

      default:

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(
          "RAW : " +
          Buffer.from(
            bytes.subarray(at, func.end)
          ).toString("hex")
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 3 TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

const secs = sections();

const importSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 2);
const typeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = secs.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!importSec || !typeSec || !codeSec)
  throw new Error("Required sections not found");

const imported = importedFunctions(importSec);

const target = funcs.find(x => x.funcIndex === TARGET);

if (!target)
  throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET} not found`);

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET}`);
decode(target);

console.log("");

console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceFunction3550ExpressionsV56.ts

import fs from "fs";

const path =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x001244a0;
const END   = 0x001246f3;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3550 EXPRESSION TRACE v56");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[pos++];

        result |=
            BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n: bigint): string {
    if (n < 0n) {
        return "-0x" + (-n).toString(16);
    }

    return "0x" + n.toString(16);
}

function shortExpr(x: string): string {
    if (x.length <= 100) {
        return x;
    }

    return x.slice(0, 97) + "...";
}

interface StackItem {
    expr: string;
}

const stack: StackItem[] = [];

function pop(): StackItem {
    return stack.pop() ?? { expr: "<EMPTY>" };
}

function push(expr: string) {
    stack.push({ expr });
}

function binary(op: string) {
    const rhs = pop().expr;
    const lhs = pop().expr;

    push(`(${lhs} ${op} ${rhs})`);
}

let p = START;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXPRESSION STREAM");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push(`local${r.value}`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `local.get ${r.value}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop().expr;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `local.set ${r.value} = ${shortExpr(value)}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop().expr;

            push(value);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `local.tee ${r.value} = ${shortExpr(value)}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push(r.value.toString());

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `i32.const ${r.value}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push(r.value.toString());

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `i64.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x45: {
            const x = pop().expr;

            push(`(${x} == 0)`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i32.eqz`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x7c:
            binary("+");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.add`
            );
            break;

        case 0x7d:
            binary("-");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.sub`
            );
            break;

        case 0x7e:
            binary("*");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.mul`
            );
            break;

        case 0x83:
            binary("&");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.and`
            );
            break;

        case 0x84:
            binary("|");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.or`
            );
            break;

        case 0x85:
            binary("^");
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.xor`
            );
            break;

        case 0x86: {
            const rhs = pop().expr;
            const lhs = pop().expr;

            push(`(${lhs} << ${rhs})`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.shl`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x87: {
            const rhs = pop().expr;
            const lhs = pop().expr;

            push(`(${lhs} >>s ${rhs})`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.shr_s`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const rhs = pop().expr;
            const lhs = pop().expr;

            push(`(${lhs} >> ${rhs})`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  i64.shr_u`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            /*
             * i64.load
             *
             * memarg:
             *   alignment
             *   offset
             */
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const base = pop().expr;

            push(`MEM64[${base}+${off.value}]`);

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            /*
             * i64.store
             */
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const value = pop().expr;
            const base = pop().expr;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `i64.store [${base}+${off.value}] = ${shortExpr(value)}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            /*
             * if
             *
             * For expression tracing, preserve stack but report
             * the condition.
             */
            const cond = pop().expr;

            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `if condition=${shortExpr(cond)}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  else`
            );
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  end`
            );
            break;

        default:
            /*
             * Do not attempt to guess unknown instructions.
             * Report them and continue.
             */
            console.log(
                `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );

            break;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("No bytes modified.");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 3837;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(buf, pos, bits = 32) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  while (true) {
    byte = buf[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function readName(buf, pos) {
  const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
  const end = n.next + n.value;
  return {
    text: buf.slice(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

function skipLimits(buf, pos) {
  const flags = buf[pos++];
  const min = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    const max = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

function skipTableType(buf, pos) {
  pos++; // elemtype
  return skipLimits(buf, pos);
}

function skipMemoryType(buf, pos) {
  return skipLimits(buf, pos);
}

function skipGlobalType(buf, pos) {
  pos += 2; // valtype + mutability
  return pos;
}

function countImports(buf, importStart, importSize) {
  let pos = importStart;
  const end = importStart + importSize;

  const count = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = count.next;

  let importedFunctions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readName(buf, pos);
    pos = mod.next;

    const name = readName(buf, pos);
    pos = name.next;

    const kind = buf[pos++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      readULEB(buf, pos);
      const t = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      pos = skipTableType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      pos = skipMemoryType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      pos = skipGlobalType(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 4) {
      const tagType = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = tagType.next;
    } else {
      throw new Error("Unknown import kind " + kind);
    }
  }

  return importedFunctions;
}

function getSections(buf) {
  let pos = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[pos++];

    const sz = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = sz.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + sz.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start,
      end,
      size: sz.value
    });

    pos = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

function decodeBody(buf, start, end) {
  let pos = start;

  const localDecl = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = localDecl.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = n.next;

    const type = buf[pos++];

    for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
      locals.push(type);
    }
  }

  const instructions = [];

  function add(addr, op, extra = {}) {
    instructions.push({
      addr,
      op,
      ...extra
    });
  }

  while (pos < end) {
    const addr = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      add(addr, "end");
      break;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      add(addr, "local.get", { local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      add(addr, "local.set", { local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      add(addr, "local.tee", { local: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      add(addr, "i32.const", { value: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = readSLEB(buf, pos, 64);
      pos = x.next;
      add(addr, "i64.const", { value: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      add(addr, "i32.add");
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      add(addr, "i32.sub");
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = off.next;

      add(addr, "i32.load", {
        align: align.value,
        offset: off.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = off.next;

      add(addr, "i64.load", {
        align: align.value,
        offset: off.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = off.next;

      add(addr, "i32.load8_u", {
        align: align.value,
        offset: off.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = off.next;

      add(addr, "i32.store", {
        align: align.value,
        offset: off.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
      const align = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = off.next;

      add(addr, "i64.store", {
        align: align.value,
        offset: off.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      add(addr, "call", { func: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = table.next;

      add(addr, "call_indirect", {
        type: type.value,
        table: table.value
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x25) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      add(addr, "table.get", { table: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x26) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;

      add(addr, "table.set", { table: x.value });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x1b) {
      add(addr, "select");
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x46) {
      add(addr, "i32.eq");
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
      add(addr, "i32.eqz");
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
      add(addr, "return");
      continue;
    }
if (op === 0x23) {
  const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = x.next;

  add(addr, "global.get", {
    global: x.value
  });
  continue;
}
if (op === 0x24) {
  const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = x.next;

  add(addr, "global.set", {
    global: x.value
  });
  continue;
}

if (op === 0x1a) {
  add(addr, "drop");
  continue;
}

    // Structured control instructions.
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04 ||
      op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x06 ||
      op === 0x07 ||
      op === 0x08 ||
      op === 0x09 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x0e
    ) {
      // Conservative skip.
      if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        pos++;
      } else if (op === 0x05) {
        // no immediate
      } else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
        pos = x.next;
      } else if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
        pos = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
          pos = x.next;
        }

        const def = readULEB(buf, pos);
        pos = def.next;
      }

      add(addr, "control-op", { opcode: "0x" + op.toString(16) });
      continue;
    }

    add(addr, "UNKNOWN", {
      opcode: "0x" + op.toString(16)
    });

    break;
  }

  return { locals, instructions };
}

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const functionSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!importSec || !functionSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

const importedFunctions =
  countImports(wasm, importSec.start, importSec.size);

let pos = functionSec.start;
const functionCount = readULEB(wasm, pos);
pos = functionCount.next;

const definedTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount.value; i++) {
  const x = readULEB(wasm, pos);
  pos = x.next;
  definedTypes.push(x.value);
}

let cp = codeSec.start;
const bodyCount = readULEB(wasm, cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
  const bodySize = readULEB(wasm, cp);
  cp = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = cp;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

  const funcIndex = importedFunctions + i;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
    targetBody = {
      funcIndex,
      bodyIndex: i,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd
    };
    break;
  }

  cp = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetBody) {
  throw new Error("Target function not found");
}

const decoded =
  decodeBody(wasm, targetBody.bodyStart, targetBody.bodyEnd);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNCTION 3837 GRAPH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctions);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", bodyCount.value);
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("BODY INDEX :", targetBody.bodyIndex);
console.log(
  "BODY :",
  "0x" + targetBody.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
  "..",
  "0x" + targetBody.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);
console.log("LOCALS :", decoded.locals.length);

console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const ins of decoded.instructions) {
  let line =
    "0x" + ins.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
    "  " + ins.op;

  if (ins.local !== undefined)
    line += " local=" + ins.local;

  if (ins.value !== undefined)
    line += " value=" + ins.value;

  if (ins.offset !== undefined)
    line += " offset=" + ins.offset;

  if (ins.align !== undefined)
    line += " align=" + ins.align;

  if (ins.func !== undefined)
    line += " func=" + ins.func;

    line += " type=" + ins.type;

  if (ins.table !== undefined)
    line += " table=" + ins.table;

  if (ins.opcode !== undefined)
    line += " opcode=" + ins.opcode;

  console.log(line);
}

for (let i = 0; i < decoded.instructions.length; i++) {
  const ins = decoded.instructions[i];

  if (ins.op === "i32.store" || ins.op === "i64.store") {
    console.log(
      "STORE @ 0x" +
      ins.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +

    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < decoded.instructions.length; i++) {
  const a = decoded.instructions[i];
  const b = decoded.instructions[i + 1];

  if (
    a &&

    (b.op === "i32.store" || b.op === "i64.store")

    );

  if (
    a.op === "local.get" &&

    console.log(

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALLS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const ins of decoded.instructions) {
  if (ins.op === "call") {
    console.log(
      "CALL @ 0x" +
      ins.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
      " -> FUNC " + ins.func
    );
  }
}

console.log("");

console.log("FINAL");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 446;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos, b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.value;
  p = s.next;

  if (id === 10)
    break;

  p += size;
}

const count = uleb(p);
const bodyCount = count.value;

p = count.next;

let start = -1;
let end = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const bs = p;
  const be = p + s.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    start = bs;
    end = be;
    break;
  }

  p = be;
}

if (start < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC 446 NOT FOUND");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = start;

const decl = uleb(q);
q = decl.next;

for (let i = 0; i < decl.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(q);
  q = n.next;
  q++;
}

const ins = [];

while (q < end) {

  const at = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.push({ at, op, text: "end" });
    break;
  }

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    const name =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      local: x.value,
      text: `${name} local${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = sleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      value: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      target: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x2d) {

    const a = uleb(q);
    q = a.next;

    const o = uleb(q);
    q = o.next;

    const name =
      op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
      op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
      "i32.load8_u";

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      text: `${name} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  const names = {
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x67: "i32.clz",
    0x68: "i32.ctz",
    0x69: "i32.popcnt",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",

    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0x77: "i32.rotl",
    0x78: "i32.rotr"
  };

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const x = uleb(q);
    q = x.next;

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`
    });

    continue;
  }

  ins.push({
    at,
    op,
    text: names[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  });
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 446;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = null;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sz = readULEB(pos);
  pos = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = pos;
    break;
  }

  pos = sectionStart + 1 + (sz.next - (sectionStart + 1)) + sz.value;
}

if (codeStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

const count = readULEB(codeStart);
const bodyCount = count.value;

let p = count.next;

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
  throw new Error("TARGET BODY NOT FOUND");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Block type
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  const names = {
    0x40: "void",
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64"
  };

  if (names[b]) {
    return {
      value: names[b],
      next: pos + 1
    };
  }

  return readSLEB(pos);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decoder
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decode(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  const decl = readULEB(p);
  const declCount = decl.value;
  p = decl.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < declCount; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const type = wasm[p++];

    for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
      locals.push(type);
    }
  }

  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CONTROL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      const bt = readBlockType(p);
      p = bt.next;

      const name =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
        "if";

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `${name} type=${bt.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "else"
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "end"
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text:
          op === 0x0c
            ? `br ${x.value}`
            : `br_if ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "return"
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCALS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        local: x.value,
        text: `${name} local${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CONSTANTS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        value: x.value,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      let q = p;
      let result = 0n;
      let shift = 0n;
      let b;

      while (true) {
        b = wasm[q++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
      }

      p = q;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i64.const ${result}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        target: x.value,
        text: `call ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const memNames = {
      0x28: "i32.load",
      0x29: "i64.load",
      0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
      0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
      0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
      0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
      0x30: "i64.load8_s",
      0x31: "i64.load8_u",
      0x32: "i64.load16_s",
      0x33: "i64.load16_u",
      0x34: "i64.load32_s",
      0x35: "i64.load32_u",
      0x36: "i32.store",
      0x37: "i64.store",
      0x3a: "i32.store8",
      0x3b: "i32.store16",
      0x3e: "i64.store32"
    };

    if (memNames[op]) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        offset: o.value,
        text:
          `${memNames[op]} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // NUMERIC OPS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const names = {

      0x1a: "drop",
      0x1b: "select",

      0x45: "i32.eqz",
      0x46: "i32.eq",
      0x47: "i32.ne",

      0x48: "i32.lt_s",
      0x49: "i32.lt_u",
      0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
      0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
      0x4c: "i32.le_s",
      0x4d: "i32.le_u",
      0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
      0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

      0x50: "i64.eqz",
      0x51: "i64.eq",
      0x52: "i64.ne",
      0x53: "i64.lt_s",
      0x54: "i64.lt_u",
      0x55: "i64.gt_s",
      0x56: "i64.gt_u",
      0x57: "i64.le_s",
      0x58: "i64.le_u",
      0x59: "i64.ge_s",
      0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

      0x67: "i32.clz",
      0x68: "i32.ctz",
      0x69: "i32.popcnt",

      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x6c: "i32.mul",

      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor",
      0x74: "i32.shl",
      0x75: "i32.shr_s",
      0x76: "i32.shr_u",

      0x7c: "i64.add",
      0x7d: "i64.sub",
      0x7e: "i64.mul",

      0x83: "i64.and",
      0x84: "i64.or",
      0x85: "i64.xor",
      0x86: "i64.shl",
      0x87: "i64.shr_s",
      0x88: "i64.shr_u",

      0xad: "i64.extend_i32_s",

      0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64"
    };

    out.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: names[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    });
  }

  return {
    locals,
    instructions: out
  };
}

const result = decode(bodyStart, bodyEnd);
const ins = result.instructions;

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction87ArgsV1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END = 0x0001295c;

const TARGET = 87;

const body = wasm.subarray(START, END);

type Val = {
  type: "i32" | "i64";
  expr: string;
};

const stack: Val[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = body[p++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let p = pos;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = body[p++];

    value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7n;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
  }

  return {
    value,
    next: p,
  };
}

function pop(): Val {
  const v = stack.pop();

  if (!v) {
    return {
      type: "i64",
      expr: "<STACK_UNDERFLOW>",
    };
  }

  return v;
}

function push(type: "i32" | "i64", expr: string) {
  stack.push({ type, expr });
}

function pop2() {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();

  return { a, b };
}

function memExpr(base: Val, offset: number) {
  if (offset === 0) {
    return `MEM64[${base.expr}]`;
  }

  return `MEM64[${base.expr}+${offset}]`;
}

let p = 0;

// Skip local declarations.
const localDeclCount = body[p++];

for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  p++; // valtype
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 ARGUMENT TRACE FROM FUNCTION 122 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Function 122 : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Target call  : ${TARGET}`);
console.log();

let callNo = 0;

while (p < body.length) {
  const addr = START + p;
  const op = body[p++];

  switch (op) {
    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // control
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x0b:
      // end
      break;

    case 0x02: {
      // block
      p++; // blocktype
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      // loop
      p++;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      // if
      p++;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      // else
      break;

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d:
      // br / br_if
      uleb(p);
      p = uleb(p).next;
      break;

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // locals
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const existing = locals.get(x.value);

      push(
        existing?.type ?? "i64",
        existing?.expr ?? `local${x.value}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const v = pop();

      locals.set(x.value, v);

      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const v = stack[stack.length - 1];

      if (v) {
        locals.set(x.value, v);
      }

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // constants
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      push("i32", x.value.toString());

      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      push("i64", x.value.toString());

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // memory
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      const base = pop();

      push("i32", `MEM32[${base.expr}+${o.value}]`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      const base = pop();

      push("i64", memExpr(base, o.value));

      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      pop();
      pop();

      break;
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      pop();
      pop();

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // i32 arithmetic
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x6a: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i32", `(${a.expr} + ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x6b: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i32", `(${a.expr} - ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // i64 arithmetic
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x7c: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} + ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x7d: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} - ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x7e: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} * ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x83: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} & ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x84: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} | ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x85: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} ^ ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x86: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} << ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x87: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} >>s ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x88: {
      const { a, b } = pop2();

      push("i64", `(${a.expr} >>u ${b.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // conversions
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0xad: {
      const v = pop();

      push("i64", `i64.extend_i32_s(${v.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0xac: {
      const v = pop();

      push("i64", `i64.extend_i32_s(${v.expr})`);

      break;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // calls
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const fn = x.value;

      if (fn === TARGET) {
        callNo++;

        const args: Val[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          args.unshift(pop());
        }

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`CALL #${callNo}`);
        console.log(`WASM offset : ${hex(addr)}`);
        console.log();

        console.log(`arg0 [i32] = ${args[0].expr}`);
        console.log(`arg1 [i64] = ${args[1].expr}`);
        console.log(`arg2 [i64] = ${args[2].expr}`);
        console.log(`arg3 [i64] = ${args[3].expr}`);
        console.log(`arg4 [i64] = ${args[4].expr}`);
        console.log();

        console.log(`stack after call: ${stack.length}`);
      } else {
        // Unknown return behavior.
        // Keep analysis conservative.
        console.log(
          `[non-target call] fn=${fn} at ${hex(addr)}`
        );
      }

      break;
    }

    default:
      // We deliberately do not guess unknown opcodes.
      break;
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET CALLS FOUND = ${callNo}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction87ArgsV2.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295c;

const TARGET_FUNC = 87;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(pos: number): number {
    return wasm[pos];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function localName(index: number): string {
    return `local${index}`;
}

/*
 * We only need a symbolic representation.
 */
type Expr = {
    type: "i32" | "i64" | "unknown";
    text: string;
};

function i32(text: string): Expr {
    return { type: "i32", text };
}

function i64(text: string): Expr {
    return { type: "i64", text };
}

function unknown(): Expr {
    return { type: "unknown", text: "<UNKNOWN>" };
}

function pop(stack: Expr[], what: string): Expr {
    const x = stack.pop();

    if (!x) {
        return unknown();
    }

    return x;
}

function printExpr(x: Expr): string {
    return x.text;
}

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * Function 122 begins with:
 *
 *   global.get 0
 *   i32.const 512
 *   i32.sub
 *   local.tee 2
 *   global.set 0
 *
 * We explicitly seed local2 as FRAME_BASE.
 */
const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    locals.set(i, i32(localName(i)));
}

for (let i = 3; i < 40; i++) {
    locals.set(i, i64(localName(i)));
}

locals.set(2, i32("FRAME_BASE"));

const stack: Expr[] = [];

let p = FUNC_START;

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Parse Function 122 local declarations
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * Function body begins with:
 *
 *   local_decl_count
 *   repeated:
 *       local_count
 *       value_type
 *
 * These bytes are NOT instructions.
 */
const localDecl = readULEB(p);
p = localDecl.next;

console.log(`Local declaration groups : ${localDecl.value}`);

for (let g = 0; g < localDecl.value; g++) {

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    const valueType = u8(p++);

    const typeName =
        valueType === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        valueType === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        valueType === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        valueType === 0x7c ? "f64" :
        `0x${valueType.toString(16)}`;

    console.log(
        `  group ${g}: ${count.value} × ${typeName}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log(`Instruction stream starts : ${hex(p)}`);
console.log();

let callNo = 0;
let blockDepth = 0;
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 ARGUMENT TRACE FROM FUNCTION 122 v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`Function 122 : ${hex(FUNC_START)} .. ${hex(FUNC_END)}`);
console.log(`Target call  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log();
console.log("FRAME BASE IS EXPLICITLY SEEDED AS local2 = FRAME_BASE");
console.log();

while (p < FUNC_END) {

    const opPos = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    switch (op) {

        case 0x0b: {
    /*
     * 0x0b can terminate:
     *
     *   - block
     *   - loop
     *   - if
     *   - function body
     *
     * Only depth 0 means the actual function end.
     */

    if (blockDepth > 0) {
        blockDepth--;
    } else {
        p = FUNC_END;
    }

    break;
}

        case 0x20: {
            // local.get
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = locals.get(r.value) ?? unknown();
            stack.push(value);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            // local.set
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop(stack, "local.set");
            locals.set(r.value, value);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            // local.tee
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : unknown();

            locals.set(r.value, value);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            // i32.const
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i32(r.value.toString()));
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            // i64.const
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i64(r.value.toString()));
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a: {
            // i32.add
            const b = pop(stack, "i32.add");
            const a = pop(stack, "i32.add");

            stack.push(i32(`(${a.text} + ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x6b: {
            // i32.sub
            const b = pop(stack, "i32.sub");
            const a = pop(stack, "i32.sub");

            stack.push(i32(`(${a.text} - ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            /*
             * i64.load
             *
             * memarg:
             *   align
             *   offset
             */
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const address = pop(stack, "i64.load");

            let text = `MEM64[${address.text}`;

            if (off.value !== 0) {
                text += `+${off.value}`;
            }

            text += "]";

            stack.push(i64(text));
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            /*
             * i64.store
             *
             * memarg:
             *   align
             *   offset
             */
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            /*
             * value is on top, address below it.
             */
            const value = pop(stack, "i64.store value");
            const address = pop(stack, "i64.store address");

            /*
             * Store has no result.
             */
            void value;
            void address;

            break;
        }

        /*
         * i64 arithmetic used around the target calls.
         */

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.add");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.add");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} + ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.sub");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.sub");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} - ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x7e: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.mul");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.mul");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} * ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x74: {
            // i32.shr_s
            const b = pop(stack, "i32.shr_s");
            const a = pop(stack, "i32.shr_s");

            stack.push(i32(`(${a.text} >> ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.and");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.and");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} & ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.or");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.or");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} | ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x85: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.xor");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.xor");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} ^ ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x86: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.shl");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.shl");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} << ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop(stack, "i64.shr_u");
            const a = pop(stack, "i64.shr_u");

            stack.push(i64(`(${a.text} >>> ${b.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            /*
             * call
             */
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const target = r.value;

            if (target === TARGET_FUNC) {

                callNo++;

                const args: Expr[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                    args.push(pop(stack, `call ${TARGET_FUNC}`));
                }

                args.reverse();

                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log(`CALL #${callNo}`);
                console.log(`WASM offset : ${hex(opPos)}`);
                console.log();
                console.log(`arg0 [i32] = ${printExpr(args[0])}`);
                console.log(`arg1 [i64] = ${printExpr(args[1])}`);
                console.log(`arg2 [i64] = ${printExpr(args[2])}`);
                console.log(`arg3 [i64] = ${printExpr(args[3])}`);
                console.log(`arg4 [i64] = ${printExpr(args[4])}`);
                console.log();
            }

            /*
             * Function 87 returns void.
             *
             * For non-target calls we cannot safely infer stack
             * effects here, so do not fabricate a return value.
             */
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Comparisons / control-flow instructions that do not need
         * to participate in the target argument expressions.
         *
         * We intentionally preserve the symbolic state rather than
         * pretending to evaluate branches.
         */

        case 0x45: {
            pop(stack, "i32.eqz");
            stack.push(i32("<i32.eqz>"));
            break;
        }

        case 0x46:
        case 0x47:
        case 0x48:
        case 0x49:
        case 0x4a:
        case 0x4b:
        case 0x4c:
        case 0x4d:
        case 0x4e:
        case 0x4f: {
            pop(stack, "i32 comparison");
            pop(stack, "i32 comparison");
            stack.push(i32("<i32.cmp>"));
            break;
        }

        case 0x50: {
            pop(stack, "i64.eqz");
            stack.push(i32("<i64.eqz>"));
            break;
        }

        case 0x51:
        case 0x52:
        case 0x53:
        case 0x54:
        case 0x55:
        case 0x56:
        case 0x57:
        case 0x58:
        case 0x59:
        case 0x5a: {
            pop(stack, "i64 comparison");
            pop(stack, "i64 comparison");
            stack.push(i32("<i64.cmp>"));
            break;
        }

       case 0x02:
case 0x03:
case 0x04: {
    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */

    const blockType = u8(p);

    if (blockType === 0x40) {
        p++;
    } else {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
    }

    blockDepth++;

    break;
}

        case 0x05:
            // else
            break;

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            // return
            break;

        case 0x1a:
            // drop
            pop(stack, "drop");
            break;

        case 0x1b: {
            /*
             * select
             *
             * stack: a b condition
             */
            const cond = pop(stack, "select condition");
            const b = pop(stack, "select b");
            const a = pop(stack, "select a");

            stack.push({
                type: a.type === b.type ? a.type : "unknown",
                text: `select(${a.text},${b.text},${cond.text})`
            });

            break;
        }

        case 0x3f: {
            // memory.size
            p++; // memory index
            stack.push(i32("memory.size"));
            break;
        }

        case 0x40: {
            // memory.grow
            p++; // memory index
            pop(stack, "memory.grow");
            stack.push(i32("memory.grow"));
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            // global.get
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            /*
             * Global 0 is the stack pointer.
             *
             * Function 122 immediately subtracts 512 and tees
             * the result into local2.
             */
            if (r.value === 0) {
                stack.push(i32("STACK_PTR"));
            } else {
                stack.push(i32(`GLOBAL${r.value}`));
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            // global.set
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            pop(stack, "global.set");
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Conversions used in Function 122.
         */

        case 0xa7: {
            // i32.wrap_i64
            const x = pop(stack, "i32.wrap_i64");
            stack.push(i32(`wrap_i64(${x.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0xac: {
            // i64.extend_i32_s
            const x = pop(stack, "i64.extend_i32_s");
            stack.push(i64(`extend_i32_s(${x.text})`));
            break;
        }

        case 0xad: {
            // i64.extend_i32_u
            const x = pop(stack, "i64.extend_i32_u");
            stack.push(i64(`extend_i32_u(${x.text})`));
            break;
        }

        default: {
            /*
             * Unknown opcode.
             *
             * We stop instead of corrupting the symbolic stack.
             */
            console.log();
            console.log(`STOP: unsupported opcode ${hex(op)} at ${hex(opPos)}`);
            console.log(`Calls decoded before stop: ${callNo}`);
            process.exit(0);
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET CALLS FOUND = ${callNo}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction87ConsumersV1.ts
