import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295c;

// Function 87 outputs:
// S0  = frame+0
// S1  = frame+16
// ...
// S29 = frame+464
//
// Each S occupies 16 bytes.
// We therefore watch the two i64 words of each slot:
//   +0 and +8

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function slotFromOffset(offset: number): {
    slot: number;
    word: number;
} | null {
    if (offset < 0 || offset >= 480)
        return null;

    const slot = Math.floor(offset / 16);
    const inside = offset % 16;

    if (inside !== 0 && inside !== 8)
        return null;

    return {
        slot,
        word: inside === 0 ? 0 : 1
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 CONSUMER SEARCH v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(
    `Function 122 : ${hex(FUNC_START)} .. ${hex(FUNC_END)}`
);
console.log("Watching S0..S29");
console.log("");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan raw instructions in Function 122.
//
// i64.load opcode:
//   0x29
//
// Encoding:
//   29 <memarg align> <memarg offset>
//
// We are interested in offsets:
//
//   0, 8, 16, 24, ... 472
//
// because those are the two i64 words of S0..S29.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Hit = {
    pc: number;
    offset: number;
    slot: number;
    word: number;
    align: number;
};

const hits: Hit[] = [];

let pc = FUNC_START;

while (pc < FUNC_END) {
    const opPc = pc;
    const op = buf[pc++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i64.load
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    if (op === 0x29) {
        const align = readU32Leb(pc);
        pc = align.next;

        const offset = readU32Leb(pc);
        pc = offset.next;

        const info = slotFromOffset(offset.value);

        if (info !== null) {
            hits.push({
                pc: opPc,
                offset: offset.value,
                slot: info.slot,
                word: info.word,
                align: align.value
            });
        }

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // For this search we only need instruction boundaries well
    // enough to identify i64.load.
    //
    // Skip immediate operands for common opcodes.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00: // unreachable
        case 0x01: // nop
        case 0x05: // else
        case 0x0b: // end
        case 0x0f: // return
        case 0x1a: // drop
        case 0x45: // i32.eqz
        case 0x46:
        case 0x47:
        case 0x48:
        case 0x49:
        case 0x4a:
        case 0x4b:
        case 0x4c:
        case 0x4d:
        case 0x4e:
        case 0x4f:
        case 0x50: // i32.eqz / comparisons
        case 0x51:
        case 0x52:
        case 0x53:
        case 0x54:
        case 0x55:
        case 0x56:
        case 0x57:
        case 0x58:
        case 0x59:
        case 0x5a:
        case 0x6a: // i32.add
        case 0x6b: // i32.sub
        case 0x6c: // i32.mul
        case 0x7c: // i64.add
case 0xad: { // i64.extend_i32_u
    // no immediate operand
    break;
}
        case 0x74: { // i32.shr_s
            // no immediate operand
            break;
        }
        case 0x7d: // i64.sub
        case 0x7e: // i64.mul
        case 0x7f: // i64.div_s
        case 0x80: // i64.div_u
        case 0x81: // i64.rem_s
        case 0x82: // i64.rem_u
        case 0x83: // i64.and
        case 0x84: // i64.or
        case 0x85: // i64.xor
        case 0x86: // i64.shl
        case 0x87: // i64.shr_s
        case 0x88: // i64.shr_u
            break;

        case 0x02: // block
        case 0x03: // loop
        case 0x04: { // if
            // block type
            if (pc < FUNC_END) {
                if (buf[pc] === 0x40) {
                    pc++;
                } else {
                    const r = readS32Leb(pc);
                    pc = r.next;
                }
            }
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: // br
        case 0x0d: // br_if
        case 0x10: { // call
            const r = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: { // call_indirect
            const a = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = a.next;

            const b = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = b.next;

            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: { // br_table
            const n = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const r = readU32Leb(pc);
                pc = r.next;
            }

            const d = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = d.next;

            break;
        }

        case 0x20: // local.get
        case 0x21: // local.set
        case 0x22: // local.tee
        case 0x23: // global.get
        case 0x24: { // global.set
            const r = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = r.next;
            break;
        }
        case 0x25: { // table.get
            const r = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: // i32.load
        case 0x2a: // i64.load8_s
        case 0x2b: // i64.load8_u
        case 0x2c: // i64.load16_s
        case 0x2d: // i64.load16_u
        case 0x2e: // i64.load32_s
        case 0x2f: // i64.load32_u
        case 0x36: // i32.store
        case 0x37: { // i64.store
            const a = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = a.next;

            const o = readU32Leb(pc);
            pc = o.next;

            break;
        }

        case 0x41: { // i32.const
            const r = readS32Leb(pc);
            pc = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: { // i64.const
            const r = readU64Leb(pc);
            pc = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x43: // f32.const
            pc += 4;
            break;

        case 0x44: // f64.const
            pc += 8;
            break;

        default:
            // We deliberately do NOT guess unknown instruction sizes.
            //
            // Function 122 was already decoded successfully by the
            // previous analysis. If this lightweight scanner encounters
            // something unknown, we report it and stop rather than
            // producing potentially false consumer locations.
            console.log(
                `STOP: unsupported opcode ${hex(op)} at ${hex(opPc)}`
            );
            pc = FUNC_END;
            break;
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Results
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("CONSUMER LOADS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (hits.length === 0) {
    console.log("No matching i64.load consumers found.");
} else {
    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(h.pc)}  i64.load  offset=${h.offset}` +
            `  → S${h.slot} word${h.word}` +
            `  align=${h.align}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("SUMMARY BY SLOT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let s = 0; s < 30; s++) {
    const slotHits = hits.filter(h => h.slot === s);

    if (slotHits.length === 0) {
        console.log(`S${s}`.padEnd(5) + " NO LOAD FOUND");
    } else {
        const pcs = slotHits
            .map(h => hex(h.pc))
            .join(", ");

        console.log(
            `S${s}`.padEnd(5) +
            ` loads=${slotHits.length}  ${pcs}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHING LOADS = ${hits.length}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x00007675;
const END   = 0x000076e7;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 DATAFLOW v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" SYMBOLIC STACK / LOCAL TRACKING");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7n;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7n;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 64n && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << s;

  return { value: v, next: pos };
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = uleb(pos);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: Number(a.value),
    offset: Number(o.value),
    next: o.next
  };
}

function H(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function short(x: string) {
  return x.length > 500 ? x.slice(0, 497) + "..." : x;
}

type V = string;

const stack: V[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, V>();

function push(x: V) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): V {
  if (!stack.length)
    return "STACK_UNKNOWN";

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function binary(op: string) {
  const b = pop();
  const a = pop();

  push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

function unary(op: string) {
  const a = pop();

  push(`${op}(${a})`);
}

function printStack(label: string) {
  console.log(`STACK ${label}:`);

  if (!stack.length) {
    console.log("  <empty>");
    return;
  }

  for (let i = stack.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    console.log(`  [${i}] ${short(stack[i])}`);
  }
}

let p = START;
let count = 0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INITIAL LOCALS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
  locals.set(i, `local${i}`);
  console.log(`local${i} = local${i}`);
}

console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TRACE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  count++;

  switch (op) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // locals

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      const v = locals.get(n) ?? `local${n}`;

      push(v);

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} local.get ${n} -> ${v}`
      );

    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      const v = pop();

      locals.set(n, v);

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} local.set ${n} = ${short(v)}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const n = Number(x.value);
      const v = pop();

      locals.set(n, v);
      push(v);

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} local.tee ${n} = ${short(v)}`
      );

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i64.const

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} i64.const ${x.value}`
      );

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.const
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      push(`i32(${x.value})`);

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} i32.const ${x.value}`
      );

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i64 arithmetic
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x7b:
      binary("i64.add");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.add -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x7c:
      binary("i64.sub");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.sub -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x7d:
      binary("i64.mul");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.mul -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x7e:
      binary("i64.div_s");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.div_s -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x7f:
      binary("i64.div_u");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.div_u -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x80:
      binary("i64.rem_s");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.rem_s -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x81:
      binary("i64.rem_u");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.rem_u -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x82:
      binary("i64.and");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.and -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x83:
      binary("i64.or");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.or -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x84:
      binary("i64.xor");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.xor -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x85:
      binary("i64.shl");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.shl -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x86:
      binary("i64.shr_s");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.shr_s -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x87:
      binary("i64.shr_u");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.shr_u -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x88:
      binary("i64.rotl");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.rotl -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0x89:
      binary("i64.rotr");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.rotr -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // conversions
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0xa7:
      unary("i32.wrap_i64");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i32.wrap_i64 -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0xac:
      unary("i64.extend_i32_s");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.extend_i32_s -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    case 0xad:
      unary("i64.extend_i32_u");
      console.log(`${H(at)} i64.extend_i32_u -> ${short(stack[stack.length - 1])}`);
      break;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // memory
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x29: {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      const addr = pop();

      const expr =
        `MEM64[${addr}${m.offset ? "+" + m.offset : ""}]`;

      push(expr);

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} i64.load ${expr}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      const destination =
        `${addr}${m.offset ? "+" + m.offset : ""}`;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `${H(at)} STORE64 ${destination}`
      );
      console.log(
        `  value = ${short(value)}`
      );
      console.log("");

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // drop
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x1a: {
      const v = pop();

      console.log(
        `${H(at)} drop ${short(v)}`
      );

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // end
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x0b:

      console.log(`${H(at)} end`);

      break;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // unreachable / nop
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    case 0x00:
      console.log(`${H(at)} unreachable`);
      break;

    case 0x01:
      console.log(`${H(at)} nop`);
      break;

    default:

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `UNHANDLED opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${H(at)}`
      );

      throw new Error("Unsupported opcode");
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 87;
const FUNC122_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC122_END   = 0x0001295c;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function slotOf(expr: string): number | null {
    const m = expr.match(
        /\(\(STACK_PTR - 512\) \+ (\d+)\)/
    );

    if (!m)
        return null;

    const offset = Number(m[1]);

    if (offset < 0 || offset >= 480 || offset % 16 !== 0)
        return null;

    return offset / 16;
}

function clean(expr: string): string {
    return expr
        .replace(/\(\(STACK_PTR - 512\) - -(\d+)\)/g,
            "((STACK_PTR - 512) + $1)");
}

function describeInput(expr: string): string {
    expr = clean(expr);

    const s = slotOf(expr);

    if (s !== null)
        return `S${s} [frame+${s * 16}]`;

    return expr;
}

 * already verified symbolic trace.
 *
 * Each entry:
 *   destination offset
 *   arg1
 *   arg3
 */
const calls = [
    { n: 1,  off: 432, a1: "MEM64[local1+24]",                    a3: "0" },
    { n: 2,  off:   0, a1: "MEM64[(STACK_PTR - 512)+504]",        a3: "MEM64[(STACK_PTR - 512)+480]" },

    { n:27, off: 176, a1: "MEM64[local1+48]",                     a3: "(MEM64[local1+32] << 1)" },

    { n:30, off: 240, a1: "MEM64[local1+40]",                     a3: "MEM64[local1+40]" },

for (const c of sorted) {

        ` frame+${c.off}`.padEnd(12) +
        ` ← CALL #${c.n}`
    );

    const s1 = slotOf(c.a1);
    const s3 = slotOf(c.a3);

    if (s1 !== null)
        console.log(`  EDGE = S${s1} → S${c.off / 16}`);

    if (s3 !== null)

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction87WritesV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x0001226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x0001295c;
const TARGET_FUNC = 87;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(pos: number): number {
    return wasm[pos];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: result,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (b & 0x40) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return {
                value: result,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type Expr = {
    type: "i32" | "i64" | "unknown";
    text: string;
};

function i32(text: string): Expr {
    return { type: "i32", text };
}

function i64(text: string): Expr {
    return { type: "i64", text };
}

function unknown(): Expr {
    return { type: "unknown", text: "<UNKNOWN>" };
}

function pop(stack: Expr[]): Expr {
    return stack.pop() ?? unknown();
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Function 122 locals
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * local0..local2 = i32
 * local3..local39 = i64
 */
const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    locals.set(i, i32(`local${i}`));
}

for (let i = 3; i < 40; i++) {
    locals.set(i, i64(`local${i}`));
}

/*
 * Function 122:
 *
 * global.get 0
 * i32.const 512
 * i32.sub
 * local.tee 2
 *
 * Therefore local2 is the frame base.
 */
locals.set(2, i32("FRAME_BASE"));

const stack: Expr[] = [];

let p = FUNC_START;

/*
 * Parse local declarations.
 */
const groups = readULEB(p);
p = groups.next;

for (let g = 0; g < groups.value; g++) {
    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    p++; // value type
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Write Map
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */
type WriteRecord = {
    callNo: number;
    callOffset: number;
    arg0: string;
    write0: string;
    write1: string;
};

const writes: WriteRecord[] = [];

let callNo = 0;
let blockDepth = 0;

while (p < FUNC_END) {

    const opPos = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    switch (op) {

        case 0x0b: {
            if (blockDepth > 0) {
                blockDepth--;
            } else {
                p = FUNC_END;
            }
            break;
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const bt = u8(p);

            if (bt === 0x40) {
                p++;
            } else {
                const r = readSLEB(p);
                p = r.next;
            }

            blockDepth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            break;

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            break;

        case 0x1a:
            pop(stack);
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(
                locals.get(r.value) ?? unknown()
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            locals.set(r.value, pop(stack));
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value =
                stack.length > 0
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : unknown();

            locals.set(r.value, value);
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            if (r.value === 0) {
                stack.push(i32("STACK_PTR"));
            } else {
                stack.push(i32(`GLOBAL${r.value}`));
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            pop(stack);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i32(r.value.toString()));
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            stack.push(i64(r.value.toString()));
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`(${a.text} + ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x6b: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`(${a.text} - ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x74: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`(${a.text} >> ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} + ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} - ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x7e: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} * ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} & ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} | ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x85: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} ^ ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x86: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} << ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`(${a.text} >>> ${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const address = pop(stack);

            let text = `MEM64[${address.text}`;

            if (off.value !== 0) {
                text += `+${off.value}`;
            }

            text += "]";

            stack.push(i64(text));

            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            /*
             * i64.store:
             *
             * stack = [..., address, value]
             */
            const value = pop(stack);
            const address = pop(stack);

            /*
             * We are only interested in stores made by Function 87.
             * Function 87 itself is not executed here, so this is
             * intentionally not added to the map.
             */
            void value;
            void address;
            void off;

            break;
        }

        case 0xa7: {
            const x = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`wrap_i64(${x.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0xac: {
            const x = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`extend_i32_s(${x.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0xad: {
            const x = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i64(`extend_i32_u(${x.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x45: {
            const x = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`eqz(${x.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }
        /*
         * i32 comparisons
         *
         * 0x45 = i32.eqz
         * 0x46 = i32.eq
         * 0x47 = i32.ne
         * 0x48 = i32.lt_s
         * 0x49 = i32.lt_u
         * 0x4a = i32.gt_s
         * 0x4b = i32.gt_u
         * 0x4c = i32.le_s
         * 0x4d = i32.le_u
         * 0x4e = i32.ge_s
         * 0x4f = i32.ge_u
         */

        case 0x46:
        case 0x47:
        case 0x48:
        case 0x49:
        case 0x4a:
        case 0x4b:
        case 0x4c:
        case 0x4d:
        case 0x4e:
        case 0x4f: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x46: "eq",
                0x47: "ne",
                0x48: "lt_s",
                0x49: "lt_u",
                0x4a: "gt_s",
                0x4b: "gt_u",
                0x4c: "le_s",
                0x4d: "le_u",
                0x4e: "ge_s",
                0x4f: "ge_u"
            };

            stack.push(
                i32(`${names[op]}(${a.text},${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x50: {
            const x = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`i64.eqz(${x.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x51:
        case 0x52:
        case 0x53:
        case 0x54:
        case 0x55:
        case 0x56:
        case 0x57:
        case 0x58:
        case 0x59:
        case 0x5a: {
            const b = pop(stack);
            const a = pop(stack);

            stack.push(
                i32(`cmp(${a.text},${b.text})`)
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            if (r.value === TARGET_FUNC) {

                callNo++;

                /*
                 * Function 87 signature:
                 *
                 * (i32, i64, i64, i64, i64) -> ()
                 */
                const arg4 = pop(stack);
                const arg3 = pop(stack);
                const arg2 = pop(stack);
                const arg1 = pop(stack);
                const arg0 = pop(stack);

                /*
                 * Function 87 performs:
                 *
                 * i64.store offset=8
                 * i64.store offset=0
                 *
                 * using arg0 as destination.
                 */
                const write0 = `${arg0.text}`;
                const write1 = `(${arg0.text} + 8)`;

                writes.push({
                    callNo,
                    callOffset: opPos,
                    arg0: arg0.text,
                    write0,
                    write1
                });

                /*
                 * Function 87 returns void.
                 */
                void arg1;
                void arg2;
                void arg3;
                void arg4;
            }

            break;
        }

        default: {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                `STOP: unsupported opcode ${hex(op)} at ${hex(opPos)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `Calls decoded before stop: ${callNo}`
            );

            process.exit(0);
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 WRITE MAP FROM FUNCTION 122 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Function 122 : ${hex(FUNC_START)} .. ${hex(FUNC_END)}`);
console.log(`Target call  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log();

console.log("CALL    CALL OFFSET       DESTINATION              WRITE +0                 WRITE +8");
console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const w of writes) {
    console.log(
        `#${String(w.callNo).padEnd(5)}` +
        `${hex(w.callOffset).padEnd(19)}` +
        `${w.arg0.padEnd(27)}` +
        `${w.write0.padEnd(25)}` +
        `${w.write1}`
    );
}

console.log();

FILE: scripts\traceFunction9368.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 9368;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readName(pos) {
    const r = uleb(pos);
    const len = r.value;
    const start = r.next;
    return {
        name: bytes.subarray(start, start + len).toString("utf8"),
        next: start + len
    };
}

function readSection(pos) {
    const id = bytes[pos++];
    const s = uleb(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    const payload = s.next;

    return {
        id,
        size,
        payload,
        end: payload + size
    };
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const s = readSection(p);

        if (s.id === 10) {
            return s;
        }

        p = s.end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decodeInstruction(p) {
    const start = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    const hex = (n) => "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: r.next,
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: r.next,
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: r.next,
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: r.next,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`
        };
    }

    // i64.const
    if (op === 0x42) {
        let q = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: q.next,
            text: `i64.const ${q.value}`
        };
    }

    // i32.add
    if (op === 0x6a) {
        return { next: p, text: "i32.add" };
    }

    // i32.sub
    if (op === 0x6b) {
        return { next: p, text: "i32.sub" };
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            next: o.next,
            text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    // i32.load8_u
    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            next: o.next,
            text: `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    // i64.load
    if (op === 0x29) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            next: o.next,
            text: `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            next: o.next,
            text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    // i64.store
    if (op === 0x37) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        return {
            next: o.next,
            text: `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        };
    }

    // i32.eq
    if (op === 0x46) {
        return { next: p, text: "i32.eq" };
    }

    // i32.ne
    if (op === 0x47) {
        return { next: p, text: "i32.ne" };
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return {
            next: r.next,
            text: `call func=${r.value}`
        };
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = uleb(p);
        const table = bytes[t.next];

        return {
            next: t.next + 1,
            text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table}`
        };
    }

    // select
    if (op === 0x1b) {
        return { next: p, text: "select" };
    }

    // end
    if (op === 0x0b) {
        return { next: p, text: "end" };
    }

    return {
        next: p,
        text: `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.payload;

const functionCount = uleb(p);
p = functionCount.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount.value; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (i === TARGET) {
        targetBody = {
            bodyIndex: i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            bodySize
        };
        break;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetBody) {
    throw new Error(`FUNCTION ${TARGET} NOT FOUND`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNCTION 9368");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", bytes.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("BODY INDEX:", targetBody.bodyIndex);
console.log(
    "BODY START:",
    "0x" + targetBody.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);
console.log(
    "BODY END  :",
    "0x" + targetBody.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);
console.log("BODY SIZE :", targetBody.bodySize);
console.log("");

let q = targetBody.bodyStart;

// local declaration vector
const localGroups = uleb(q);
q = localGroups.next;

console.log("LOCAL DECLARATION GROUPS :", localGroups.value);
console.log("");

for (let g = 0; g < localGroups.value; g++) {
    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    const type = bytes[q++];

    console.log(
        `LOCAL GROUP ${g}: count=${count.value} type=0x${type
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const instructions = [];

while (q < targetBody.bodyEnd) {
    const addr = q;
    const ins = decodeInstruction(q);

    instructions.push({
        addr,
        text: ins.text
    });

    console.log(
        "0x" + addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        ins.text
    );

    q = ins.next;

    if (ins.text === "end") break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("OFFSET +4 STORES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const x = instructions[i];

    if (
        x.text.includes("i32.store") ||
        x.text.includes("i64.store")
    ) {
        const m = x.text.match(/offset=(\d+)/);

        if (m && Number(m[1]) === 4) {
            hits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `HIT #${hits} @ 0x${x.addr
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, "0")}`
            );

            const from = Math.max(0, i - 6);
            const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 4);

            console.log("CONTEXT");

            for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
                const mark = j === i ? ">>" : "  ";

                console.log(
                    mark,
                    "0x" +
                        instructions[j].addr
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, "0"),
                    instructions[j].text
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 96;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too long");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  let functions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const mod = uleb(p);
    p = mod.next + mod.value;

    const name = uleb(p);
    p = name.next + name.value;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;
      functions++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const max = uleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return functions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const ids = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    ids.push(x.value);
  }

  return ids;
}

function parseCode(section, functionTypes, importedCount) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const funcs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    funcs.push({
      funcIndex: importedCount + i,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return funcs;
}

function decode(func) {

  let p = func.start;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(func.start)} .. ${hex(func.end)}`);
  console.log(`TYPE : ${func.typeIndex}`);

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  while (p < func.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = null;

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        text = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `call ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {

        let value = 0n;
        let shift = 0n;

        while (true) {

          const b = BigInt(bytes[p++]);

          value |=
            (b & 0x7fn) << shift;

          if ((b & 0x80n) === 0n) break;

          shift += 7n;
        }

        text = `i64.const ${value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x28: "i32.load",
          0x29: "i64.load",
          0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
          0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
          0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
          0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
          0x30: "i64.load8_s",
          0x31: "i64.load8_u",
          0x32: "i64.load16_s",
          0x33: "i64.load16_u",
          0x34: "i64.load32_s",
          0x35: "i64.load32_u"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e: {

        const align = uleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        const names = {
          0x36: "i32.store",
          0x37: "i64.store",
          0x3a: "i32.store8",
          0x3b: "i32.store16",
          0x3c: "i64.store8",
          0x3d: "i64.store16",
          0x3e: "i64.store32"
        };

        text =
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        text = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        text = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        text = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x77:
        text = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x78:
        text = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {

        const blockType = bytes[p++];

        const name =
          op === 0x02
            ? "block"
            : op === 0x03
              ? "loop"
              : "if";

        text =
          `${name} type=${hex(blockType)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0e: {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const labels = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;

          labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const d = uleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);

        text =
          `br_table ${labels.join(",")}`;

        break;
      }

      default:

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode ${hex(op)}`
        );

        console.log(
          "RAW REMAINING : " +
          Buffer.from(
            bytes.subarray(at, func.end)
          ).toString("hex")
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
  }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x00008111;
const END   = 0x00008174;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode() {

  let p = START;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNCTION 96 LOCAL CONSUMER TRACE");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
  console.log(`RANGE     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    let text = `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `local.get ${x.value}`;

      if (x.value === 0 || x.value === 2)
        text += "   <== TRACKED";
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `local.set ${x.value}`;

      if (x.value === 0 || x.value === 2)
        text += "   <== TRACKED";
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `local.tee ${x.value}`;

      if (x.value === 0 || x.value === 2)
        text += "   <== TRACKED";
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `i32.const ${x.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a) {
      text += "i32.add";
    }

    else if (op === 0x71) {
      text += "i32.and";
    }

    else if (op === 0x72) {
      text += "i32.or";
    }

    else if (op === 0x46) {
      text += "i32.eq";
    }

    else if (op === 0x47) {
      text += "i32.ne";
    }

    else if (op === 0x45) {
      text += "i32.eqz";
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `call ${x.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text += `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text += `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x0d) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text += `br_if ${x.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x04) {
      text += "if";
    }

    else if (op === 0x02) {
      text += "block";
    }

    else if (op === 0x0b) {
      text += "end";
    }

    else {
      text += `UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }

    console.log(text);
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("END");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceFunction98.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 98;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                "ULEB too long at 0x" + pos.toString(16)
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readSLEB(pos, bits = 32) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let byte;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        byte = buf[p++];

        value += (byte & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > bits + 7) {
            throw new Error(
                "SLEB too long at 0x" + pos.toString(16)
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections() {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        p = sizeR.next;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start: p,
            end: p + sizeR.value
        });

        p += sizeR.value;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseFunctionSection(section) {

    const types = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    p = countR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);

        types.push(r.value);

        p = r.next;
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCodeSection(section, functionTypes) {

    const bodies = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    p = countR.next;

    const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);

        const bodySize = sizeR.value;

        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localsR = readULEB(p);

        p = localsR.next;

        for (let g = 0; g < localsR.value; g++) {

            const nR = readULEB(p);

            p = nR.next;

            p++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function memarg(p) {

    const align = buf[p++];

    const off = readULEB(p);

    return {
        align,
        offset: off.value,
        next: off.next
    };
}

function decode(body) {

    let p = body.codeStart;

    let depth = 0;

    while (p < body.codeEnd) {

        const offset = p;

        const op = buf[p++];

        let text;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x00:
                text = "unreachable";
                break;

            case 0x01:
                text = "nop";
                break;

            case 0x02: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];
                text = `block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                depth++;
                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];
                text = `loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                depth++;
                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];
                text = `if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                depth++;
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";

                if (depth > 0)
                    depth--;

                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `br ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const countR = readULEB(p);
                p = countR.next;

                const labels = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    labels.push(r.value);
                }

                const def = readULEB(p);
                p = def.next;

                labels.push(`default=${def.value}`);

                text =
                    `br_table ${labels.join(", ")}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                text = "return";
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `call ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const a = readULEB(p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(p);
                p = b.next;

                text =
                    `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x1b:
                text = "select";
                break;

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;

                text =
                    `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;

                text =
                    `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x3a: {
                const m = memarg(p);
                p = m.next;

                text =
                    `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const r = readSLEB(p, 32);
                p = r.next;

                text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = readSLEB(p, 64);
                p = r.next;

                text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

                break;

            case 0x49:

                break;

            case 0x7c:

            case 0x7d:
                text = "i64.sub";
                break;

            default:
                console.log(
                    `${hex(offset)}  UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")}`
                );

                console.log(

                    buf
                        .subarray(offset, body.codeEnd)
                        .toString("hex")
                        .match(/../g)
                        .join(" ")
                );

                return;
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  ${text}`
        );
    }

  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function u8(p: number) {
  return buf[p];

function i32le(p: number) {
  return buf.readInt32LE(p);
}

function u32le(p: number) {
  return buf.readUInt32LE(p);
}

function i64le(p: number) {
  return buf.readBigInt64LE(p);
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x965c7;
const END   = 0x96654;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number) {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (-1n) << shift;

  return { value: result, next: p };
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

  try {

      case 0x20: {

      case 0x42: {

        p = r.next;
        text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        break;

      case 0x29: {

        p = m.next;

        break;

      case 0x37: {

        p = m.next;

        break;

      case 0x28: {

        p = m.next;

        break;

      case 0x36: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.next;
        text = `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        text = "i64.add";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        text = "i64.sub";
        break;

      case 0x7e:
        text = "i64.mul";
        break;

      case 0x03:
        text = "loop";
        break;

      case 0x02:

        p = r.next;

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x965cf;
const END   = 0x96612;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",
  0x23: "global.get",
  0x24: "global.set",

  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",

  0x36: "i32.store",
  0x37: "i64.store",

  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",
  0x74: "i32.shl",

  0x7c: "i64.add",
  0x7d: "i64.sub",
  0x7e: "i64.mul",

  // From the actual byte stream:
  0x83: "i64.and",
  0x85: "i64.xor",

  0xa7: "i64.extend_i32_s"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" G208 INPUT / RECURRENCE TRACE V13");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Range: ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let name =
    names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  try {

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb32(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
             op === 0x36 || op === 0x37) {

      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      extra =
        ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
    }

  } catch {
    console.log(`${hx(at)} ${name} [DECODE ERROR]`);
    p = at + 1;
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`);
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x965cf;
const END   = 0x9661c;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function sleb64(p: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << shift;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",
  0x23: "global.get",
  0x24: "global.set",

  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",

  0x29: "i64.load",
  0x37: "i64.store",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",
  0x74: "i32.shl",

  0x7c: "i64.add",
  0x7d: "i64.sub",
  0x7e: "i64.mul",

  0x83: "i64.extend_i32_s",
  0x84: "i64.extend_i32_u",
  0x85: "i64.extend32_s",

  0x03: "loop",
  0x0b: "end",
  0x0c: "br",
  0x0d: "br_if",
  0x10: "call",
  0x1a: "drop"
};

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("Range:", hx(START), "..", hx(END));
console.log("TARGET STORE: 0x96611");
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  let name = names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  try {

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x41) {

      const r = sleb32(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x42) {

      const r = sleb64(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x29 || op === 0x37) {

      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      extra =
        ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

      if (op === 0x37) {
        console.log(
          `${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}  <<< STORE`
        );
        continue;
      }

    } else if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x10
    ) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    }

  } catch {
    console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name} [DECODE ERROR]`);
    p = at + 1;
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`);
}
