import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_BODIES = [
    {
        name: "GLOBAL FUNC 457",
        start: 0x0001ddd6,
        end: 0x0001dea0
    },
    {
        name: "GLOBAL FUNC 459",
        start: 0x0001dee3,
        end: 0x0001e5f1
    }
];

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opName(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0f: "return"
    };

    return m[op] ?? `opcode_${hex(op)}`;
}

console.log("TRACE GLOBAL 0 DERIVED STORE ADDRESSES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGET_BODIES) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(target.name);
    console.log(
        `BODY ${hex(target.start)} .. ${hex(target.end)}`
    );
    console.log("============================================================");

    let p = target.start;

    const localDecls = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    console.log("LOCAL DECL COUNT =", localDecls.value);

    /*
     * Skip local declarations.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = n.next;

        p++; // valtype
    }

    /*
     * Track only simple symbolic values.
     *
     * local N may become:
     *
     *   GLOBAL0
     *   GLOBAL0 +/- CONST
     *   LOCAL M
     *   LOCAL M +/- CONST
     *   CONST
     *   UNKNOWN
     */

    const locals = new Map<number, string>();

    const stack: string[] = [];

    function pop() {
        return stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : "UNKNOWN";
    }

    function push(v: string) {
        stack.push(v);
    }

    let global0Derived = 0;
    let stores = 0;

    while (p < target.end) {

        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = "";

        if (op === 0x20) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            const local = r.value;
            const value = locals.get(local) ?? `LOCAL${local}`;

            push(value);

            text =
                `local.get ${local} => ${value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x21) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            const local = r.value;
            const value = pop();

            locals.set(local, value);

            text =
                `local.set ${local} <= ${value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x22) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            const local = r.value;
            const value = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : "UNKNOWN";

            locals.set(local, value);

            text =
                `local.tee ${local} <= ${value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x23) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            if (r.value === 0) {
                push("GLOBAL0");
                global0Derived++;
                text = "global.get 0 => GLOBAL0";
            }
            else {
                push(`GLOBAL${r.value}`);
                text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
            }
        }

        else if (op === 0x24) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop();

            text =
                `global.set ${r.value} <= ${value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            push(`CONST(${r.value})`);

            text =
                `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {

            const a = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = o.next;

            const address = pop();

            push(`LOAD(${address}+${hex(o.value)})`);

            text =
                `${opName(op)} ${address} + ${hex(o.value)}`;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b ||
            op === 0x3e
        ) {

            const a = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = o.next;

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            stores++;

            const related =
                address.includes("GLOBAL0") ||
                address.includes("LOCAL2") ||
                address.includes("GLOBAL0");

            console.log("");
            console.log(">>> STORE");
            console.log("    OFFSET =", hex(at));
            console.log("    OP     =", opName(op));
            console.log("    ADDR   =", address);
            console.log("    OFFSET =", hex(o.value));
            console.log("    VALUE  =", value);

            if (related) {
                console.log(
                    "    *** GLOBAL0-DERIVED ADDRESS ***"
                );
            }

            console.log("<<< STORE");

            text =
                `${opName(op)} addr=${address} value=${value}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            let result = "UNKNOWN";

            if (
                a === "GLOBAL0" &&
                b.startsWith("CONST(")
            ) {
                result =
                    `GLOBAL0 ${op === 0x6a ? "+" : "-"} ${b}`;
            }
            else if (
                a.startsWith("LOCAL") &&
                b.startsWith("CONST(")
            ) {
                result =
                    `${a} ${op === 0x6a ? "+" : "-"} ${b}`;
            }

            push(result);

            text =
                `${opName(op)} ${a}, ${b} => ${result}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x6c) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push("UNKNOWN");

            text =
                `i32.mul ${a}, ${b}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 || op === 0x73) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push("UNKNOWN");

            text =
                `${opName(op)} ${a}, ${b} => UNKNOWN`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            text = `call ${r.value}`;

            /*
             * Unknown call result.
             */
            push("CALL_RESULT");
        }

        else if (op === 0x11) {

            const t = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = table.next;

            text =
                `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

            push("CALL_RESULT");
        }

        else {

            text = opName(op);
        }

        /*
         * Only print significant dataflow operations.
         */
        if (
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24 ||
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b ||
            op === 0x3e ||
            op === 0x41 ||
            op === 0x6a ||
            op === 0x6b
        ) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("SUMMARY");
    console.log("  GLOBAL0 DERIVED OPS =", global0Derived);
    console.log("  STORES              =", stores);
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("TRACE GLOBAL 0 -> MEMORY STORE PATHS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

// Find CODE section
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const sectionSize = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payloadStart;
        codeEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const fc = readULEB(wasm, q);
const functionCount = fc.value;
q = fc.next;

console.log("FUNCTION COUNT =", functionCount);
console.log("CODE SECTION   =", hex(codeStart), "..", hex(codeEnd));
console.log("");

let global0SetTotal = 0;
let functionsWithGlobal0Set = 0;
let functionsWithSetAndStore = 0;

const interesting: {
    func: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    sets: number[];
    stores: number[];
}[] = [];

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {
    const bs = readULEB(wasm, q);
    const bodySize = bs.value;
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

    let x = bodyStart;

    // locals vector
    const lc = readULEB(wasm, x);
    const localDeclCount = lc.value;
    x = lc.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(wasm, x);
        x = n.next;

        // valtype
        x++;
    }

    const instrStart = x;

    const sets: number[] = [];
    const stores: number[] = [];

    /*
      We intentionally do a conservative byte scan.

      global.set 0 = 0x24 0x00

      Memory stores:
        i32.store       0x36
        i64.store       0x37
        f32.store       0x38
        f64.store       0x39
        i32.store8      0x3a
        i32.store16     0x3b
        i64.store8      0x3c
        i64.store16     0x3d
        i64.store32     0x3e
    */

    for (let i = instrStart; i + 1 < bodyEnd; i++) {
        if (wasm[i] === 0x24 && wasm[i + 1] === 0x00) {
            sets.push(i);
            global0SetTotal++;
        }

        if (
            wasm[i] >= 0x36 &&
            wasm[i] <= 0x3e
        ) {
            stores.push(i);
        }
    }

    if (sets.length > 0) {
        functionsWithGlobal0Set++;

        if (stores.length > 0) {
            functionsWithSetAndStore++;

            interesting.push({
                func,
                bodyStart,
                bodyEnd,
                sets,
                stores
            });
        }
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("GLOBAL 0 SET TOTAL =", global0SetTotal);
console.log("FUNCTIONS WITH GLOBAL 0 SET =", functionsWithGlobal0Set);
console.log("FUNCTIONS WITH SET + STORE =", functionsWithSetAndStore);
console.log("");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("FUNCTIONS WITH GLOBAL 0 SET + MEMORY STORE");

const MAX_FUNCTIONS = 80;

for (const item of interesting.slice(0, MAX_FUNCTIONS)) {
    console.log("");

        item.stores.slice(0, 20).map(hex).join(", "),
        item.stores.length > 20 ? `... ${item.stores.length - 20} more` : ""
    );

    /*
      Show a compact byte window around each GLOBAL.SET 0.

    for (const s of item.sets.slice(0, 5)) {
        const from = Math.max(item.bodyStart, s - 24);
        const to = Math.min(item.bodyEnd, s + 40);

        const bytes = Array.from(wasm.subarray(from, to))
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ");

        console.log(`  SET WINDOW @ ${hex(s)}`);
        console.log(`    ${bytes}`);
    }
}

const targets = [
    0x195350,

let targetConstHits = 0;

for (const item of interesting) {
    for (const s of item.sets) {
        const from = Math.max(item.bodyStart, s - 128);
        const to = Math.min(item.bodyEnd, s + 128);

        for (const target of targets) {
            const b0 = target & 0xff;
            const b1 = (target >>> 8) & 0xff;
            const b2 = (target >>> 16) & 0xff;
            const b3 = (target >>> 24) & 0xff;

            for (let i = from; i + 3 < to; i++) {
                if (
                    wasm[i] === b0 &&
                    wasm[i + 1] === b1 &&
                    wasm[i + 2] === b2 &&
                    wasm[i + 3] === b3
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `TARGET CONSTANT ${hex(target)} NEAR GLOBAL.SET 0`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `  FUNC ${item.func}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `  SET  @ ${hex(s)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `  REF  @ ${hex(i)}`
                    );

                    targetConstHits++;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceGlobalFuncs374_376.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_GLOBAL_FUNCS = new Set([374, 376]);

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readString(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);
    const end = r.next + r.value;
    return {
        next: end,
        length: r.value
    };
}

function skipLimits(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const min = uleb(buf, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 0x01) {
        const max = uleb(buf, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

/*
 * Find number of imported functions.
 *
 * Function indices include imported functions before
 * locally-defined functions.
 */
function countImportedFunctions() {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const sectionStart = p;
        const sectionEnd = p + size;

        if (sectionId === 2) {

            let x = sectionStart;

            const c = uleb(wasm, x);
            const importCount = c.value;
            x = c.next;

            let importedFunctions = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

                const mod = readString(wasm, x);
                x = mod.next;

                const name = readString(wasm, x);
                x = name.next;

                const kind = wasm[x++];

                /*
                 * external kind:
                 * 0 = function
                 * 1 = table
                 * 2 = memory
                 * 3 = global
                 * 4 = tag
                 */
                if (kind === 0) {
                    importedFunctions++;

                    const typeIndex = uleb(wasm, x);
                    x = typeIndex.next;
                }
                else if (kind === 1) {
                    /*
                     * table type
                     */
                    x++; // elem type

                    x = skipLimits(wasm, x);
                }
                else if (kind === 2) {
                    /*
                     * memory type
                     */
                    x = skipLimits(wasm, x);
                }
                else if (kind === 3) {
                    /*
                     * global type
                     */
                    x++; // value type
                    x++; // mutability
                }
                else if (kind === 4) {
                    /*
                     * tag type
                     */
                    x++; // attribute
                    const typeIndex = uleb(wasm, x);
                    x = typeIndex.next;
                }
            }

            return importedFunctions;
        }

        p = sectionEnd;
    }

    return 0;
}

const importedFunctions = countImportedFunctions();

console.log("TRACE GLOBAL FUNCTIONS 374 / 376");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS =", importedFunctions);

if (importedFunctions !== 83) {
    console.log("WARNING: EXPECTED 83 IMPORTED FUNCTIONS");
}

console.log("");

/*
 * Locate CODE section.
 */
let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(wasm, p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = sectionStart;
        codeEnd = sectionEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const fc = uleb(wasm, q);
const definedFunctionCount = fc.value;
q = fc.next;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", definedFunctionCount);
console.log("CODE SECTION      =", hex(codeStart), "..", hex(codeEnd));
console.log("");

/*
 * Decode every code body.
 *
 * Global function index =
 * imported function count + code ordinal
 */
for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedFunctionCount; ordinal++) {

    const globalFunc = importedFunctions + ordinal;

    const bs = uleb(wasm, q);
    const bodySize = bs.value;
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

    if (!TARGET_GLOBAL_FUNCS.has(globalFunc)) {
        q = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL ${ordinal}`);
    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log("SIZE =", bodySize);
    console.log("============================================================");

    let x = bodyStart;

    const localCount = uleb(wasm, x);
    const localDecls = localCount.value;
    x = localCount.next;

    console.log("LOCAL DECL COUNT =", localDecls);

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls; i++) {
        const n = uleb(wasm, x);
        x = n.next;

        const type = wasm[x++];

        console.log(
            `  LOCAL GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${n.value} TYPE=${hex(type)}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let i = x;

    const recent: string[] = [];

    while (i < bodyEnd) {

        const at = i;
        const op = wasm[i++];

        let text = "";

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x2f) {
            const a = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = a.next;

            const o = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = o.next;

            text =
                `LOAD op=${hex(op)} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        }
        else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            const a = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = a.next;

            const o = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = o.next;

            text =
                `STORE op=${hex(op)} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;

            text = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;

            text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;

            text = `call ${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = type.next;

            const table = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = table.next;

            text =
                `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
        }
        else {
            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x45: "i32.eqz",
                0x46: "i32.eq",
                0x47: "i32.ne",
                0x48: "i32.lt_s",
                0x49: "i32.lt_u",
                0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
                0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
                0x6a: "i32.add",
                0x6b: "i32.sub",
                0x6c: "i32.mul",
                0x71: "i32.and",
                0x72: "i32.or",
                0x73: "i32.xor",
                0x74: "i32.shl",
                0x75: "i32.shr_s",
                0x76: "i32.shr_u",
                0x0b: "end",
                0x0f: "return",
                0x04: "if",
                0x05: "else",
                0x02: "block",
                0x03: "loop",
                0x0c: "br",
                0x0d: "br_if"
            };

            text = names[op] ?? `opcode ${hex(op)}`;
        }

        const line =
            `${hex(at)}  ${text}`;

        console.log(line);

        recent.push(line);

        if (recent.length > 8) {
            recent.shift();
        }

        /*
         * Highlight every STORE with the preceding stack-producing
         * instructions.
         */
        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

            console.log("");
            console.log(">>> STORE CONTEXT");
            console.log(`>>> STORE @ ${hex(at)}`);

            for (const r of recent) {
                console.log("    " + r);
            }

            console.log(">>> END STORE CONTEXT");
            console.log("");
        }
    }

    console.log("");
}

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTS = 83;

const BODY = GLOBAL_FN - IMPORTS;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

  if (id === 10) codeStart = start;

}

function signedLEB(p: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[p++];

    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
    result |= -(1 << shift);

  return { value: result, p };
}

function readMemArg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  p = a.p;

  const o = uleb(p);
  p = o.p;

  return {
    align: a.v,
    offset: o.v,
    p
  };
}

function localName(i: number) {
  if (i < 4)
    return `PARAM${i}`;

  return `LOCAL${i}`;
}

// Track only definitions of local0/local3.
const defs = new Map<number, string>();

defs.set(0, "PARAM0");
defs.set(1, "PARAM1");
defs.set(2, "PARAM2");
defs.set(3, "PARAM3");

let call2827 = 0;

while (p < bodyEnd) {
  const off = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text = names[op] ?? `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16)})`;

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.p;

    const n = x.v;

    if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      if (n === 0 || n === 3) {
        defs.set(
          n,
          op === 0x21
            ? "SET"
            : "TEE"
        );
      }
    }

    text += ` ${n} (${localName(n)})`;
  }
  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = signedLEB(p);
    p = x.p;
    text += ` ${x.value}`;
  }
  else if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.p;
    text += ` ${x.v}`;
  }
  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.p;

    text += ` ${x.v}`;

    if (x.v === 2827) {
      call2827++;

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(`CALL 2827 #${call2827} @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`);
      console.log("============================================================");

      console.log(
        `Current local0 producer : ${defs.get(0) ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
      );

      console.log(
        `Current local3 producer : ${defs.get(3) ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
      );

      console.log(
        "Mapping:"
      );

      console.log(
        "  2827.arg0 = 5079.local0"
      );

      console.log(
        "  2827.arg1 = 5079.local3"
      );

      console.log("");
    }
  }
  else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37
  ) {
    const m = readMemArg(p);
    p = m.p;

    text += ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }
  else if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {
    const x = b[p++];
    text += ` blocktype=0x${x.toString(16)}`;
  }
  else if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.p;
    text += ` ${x.v}`;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ${text}`
  );

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x95000;
const END   = 0x95e67;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" G-300 BACKWARD STORE SEARCH V24");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`Range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("Target: MEM[local6 + 68] = MEM[G-300]");
console.log();

let p = START;

let stack: string[] = [];
let lastLocal6 = false;
let lastConst: number | null = null;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    if (r.v === 6) {
      lastLocal6 = true;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get 6`);
    } else {
      lastLocal6 = false;
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.set / tee
  if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    lastLocal6 = false;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    lastConst = r.v;

    if (
      r.v === 68 ||
      r.v === -300 ||
      r.v === 300 ||
      r.v === 368
    ) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add / sub
  if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

    if (lastLocal6 && lastConst !== null) {

      const off =
        op === 0x6a
          ? lastConst
          : -lastConst;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ` +
        `${op === 0x6a ? "i32.add" : "i32.sub"} ` +
        `local6 ${off >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${off}`
      );

      if (off === 68) {
        console.log(
          `*** DIRECT TARGET ADDRESS: local6 + 68 = G-300 ***`
        );
      }
    }

    lastLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // i32/i64 stores
  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} STORE op=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
      `align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );

    if (o.v === 68) {
      console.log(
        `*** OFFSET 68 STORE CANDIDATE @ ${hex(at)} ***`
      );
    }

    lastLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // loads
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    if (o.v === 68) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} LOAD offset=68`
      );
    }

    lastLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`
    );

    lastLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} global.get ${r.v}`
    );

    lastLocal6 = false;
    continue;
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} global.set ${r.v}`
    );

    lastLocal6 = false;
    continue;
  }

  // memory.copy/fill
  if (op === 0xfc) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} PREFIX 0xfc subop=${r.v}`
    );

    // memory.copy
    if (r.v === 10) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.p;

      const c = uleb(p);
      p = c.p;
    }

    lastLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // common no-immediate instructions
  if (
    op === 0x0b ||
    op === 0x05 ||
    op === 0x1a ||
    op === 0x6a ||
    op === 0x6b ||
    (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x66) ||
    (op >= 0x67 && op <= 0x91) ||
    (op >= 0xa7 && op <= 0xbf)
  ) {
    continue;
  }

  lastLocal6 = false;
  lastConst = null;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V24");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceImport10.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 10;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too long");
    }
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function readString(pos) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  const end = start + n.value;

  return {
    text: Buffer.from(
      bytes.subarray(start, end)
    ).toString("utf8"),
    next: end
  };
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < bytes.length) {
    const id = bytes[p++];

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + len.value
    });

    p += len.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

function parseImports(section) {
  let p = section.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const imports = [];

  let functionIndex = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const module = readString(p);
    p = module.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = bytes[p++];

    const item = {
      importOrdinal: i,
      functionIndex: null,
      module: module.text,
      name: name.text,
      kind
    };

    if (kind === 0) {

      const type = uleb(p);
      p = type.next;

      item.functionIndex = functionIndex;
      item.typeIndex = type.value;

      functionIndex++;
    }

    else if (kind === 1) {

      const elementType = bytes[p++];

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      let max = null;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        max = x.value;
      }

      item.elementType = elementType;
      item.min = min.value;
      item.max = max;
    }

    else if (kind === 2) {

      const flags = bytes[p++];

      const min = uleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      let max = null;

      if (flags & 1) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        max = x.value;
      }

      item.min = min.value;
      item.max = max;
    }

    else if (kind === 3) {

      item.valueType = bytes[p++];
      item.mutable = bytes[p++];
    }

    imports.push(item);
  }

  return imports;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceImport23Branches.ts
====================================================================================================
if (op === 0xfc) {
    const sub = readU32(buf, state);

    // 0..7: saturating conversions, no immediates
    if (sub <= 7) {
        // No additional bytes to consume.
    } else if (sub === 8) {
        // memory.init: data index + memory index
        readU32(buf, state);
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 9) {
        // data.drop: data index
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 10) {
        // memory.copy: destination + source memory indices
        readU32(buf, state);
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 11) {
        // memory.fill: memory index
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 12) {
        // table.init: element index + table index
        readU32(buf, state);
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 13) {
        // elem.drop: element index
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 14) {
        // table.copy: destination + source table indices
        readU32(buf, state);
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else if (sub === 15 || sub === 16 || sub === 17) {
        // table.grow, table.size, table.fill
        readU32(buf, state);
    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported 0xfc subopcode ${sub} at ${hex(state.p - 1)}`
        );
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 23;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const CONTEXT_BEFORE = 80;
const CONTEXT_AFTER = 100;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE ALL CALLS TO IMPORT FUNC23");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TYPE               = (i32) -> i32`);

function u32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionHeader(pos: number) {
    const id = buf[pos];
    const size = u32leb(pos + 1);

    return {
        id,
        size: size.value,
        payloadStart: size.next,
        payloadEnd: size.next + size.value,
    };
}

function readFunctionBodies() {
    let p = 8;

    let functionStart = -1;
    let codeStart = -1;
    let codeEnd = -1;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const s = sectionHeader(p);

        if (s.id === 3) {
            functionStart = s.payloadStart;
        }

        if (s.id === 10) {
            codeStart = s.payloadStart;
            codeEnd = s.payloadEnd;
        }

        p = s.payloadEnd;
    }

    if (functionStart < 0 || codeStart < 0) {
        throw new Error("FUNCTION/CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
    }

    let fp = functionStart;

    const fc = u32leb(fp);
    fp = fc.next;

    const definedCount = fc.value;

    const typeIndices: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
        const r = u32leb(fp);
        fp = r.next;
        typeIndices.push(r.value);
    }

    let cp = codeStart;

    const bc = u32leb(cp);
    cp = bc.next;

    const bodies: {
        globalFunc: number;
        ordinal: number;
        typeIndex: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bc.value; ordinal++) {
        const sizeInfo = u32leb(cp);

        const start = sizeInfo.next;
        const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

        bodies.push({
            globalFunc: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal,
            ordinal,
            typeIndex: typeIndices[ordinal],
            start,
            end,
        });

        cp = end;
    }

    return {
        functionStart,
        codeStart,
        codeEnd,
        bodies,
    };
}

function opcodeLength(pos: number): number {
    const op = buf[pos];

    let p = pos + 1;

    switch (op) {
        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24:
        case 0x10:
        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            return u32leb(p).next - pos;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            return signedLebLength(p) + 1;

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:

        case 0x2e:

        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {
            const a = u32leb(p);
            const b = u32leb(a.next);
            return b.next - pos;
        }

        case 0x3f:

function signedLebLength(pos: number): number {
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return p - pos;
}

for (const body of info.bodies) {
    let pos = body.start;

    // Skip local declaration vector.
    const groupInfo = u32leb(pos);
    pos = groupInfo.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < groupInfo.value; g++) {
        const countInfo = u32leb(pos);
        pos = countInfo.next;

        pos += 1;

        const at = pos;

        if (op === 0x10) {

                    instructionStart,
                    at - CONTEXT_BEFORE
                );

                    body.end,
                    r.next + CONTEXT_AFTER
                );

                console.log(
                    `CONTEXT = ${hex(beforeStart)} .. ${hex(afterEnd)}`
                );

                let c = beforeStart;

                while (c < afterEnd) {
                    const opcode = buf[c];
                    const len = Math.max(1, opcodeLength(c));

                    const end = Math.min(c + len, buf.length);

                    const bytes = Array.from(
                        buf.subarray(c, end)
                    )
                        .map((x) =>
                            x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                        )
                        .join(" ");

                    let text = "";

                    if (opcode === 0x10) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);

                        text = `CALL FUNC${x.value}`;

                        if (x.value === TARGET_FUNC) {
                            text += "  <<< TARGET IMPORT";
                        }
                    } else if (opcode === 0x20) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x21) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x22) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x23) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x24) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x41) {
                        text = "I32.CONST";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x28) {
                        const a = u32leb(c + 1);
                        const o = u32leb(a.next);
                        text = `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${hex(
                            o.value
                        )}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x36) {
                        const a = u32leb(c + 1);
                        const o = u32leb(a.next);
                        text = `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${hex(
                            o.value
                        )}`;
                    } else if (opcode === 0x45) {
                        text = "I32.EQZ";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x46) {
                        text = "I32.EQ";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x47) {
                        text = "I32.NE";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x49) {
                        text = "I32.LT_U";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x4f) {
                        text = "I32.GE_U";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x6a) {
                        text = "I32.ADD";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x6b) {
                        text = "I32.SUB";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x71) {
                        text = "I32.AND";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x72) {
                        text = "I32.OR";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x73) {
                        text = "I32.XOR";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x1a) {
                        text = "DROP";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x0f) {
                        text = "RETURN";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x0b) {
                        text = "END";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x04) {
                        text = "IF";
                    } else if (opcode === 0x05) {
                        text = "ELSE";
                    } else if (opcode === 0xfc) {
                        const x = u32leb(c + 1);
                        text = `EXTENDED 0xfc ${x.value}`;
                    } else {
                        text = `OPCODE 0x${opcode
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}`;
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(c)}  ${bytes.padEnd(28)} ${text}`
                    );

                    c += len;
                }

                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

                // Raw byte window centered around the call.
                const rawStart = Math.max(
                    instructionStart,
                    at - 32
                );

                const rawEnd = Math.min(
                    body.end,
                    r.next + 48
                );

                console.log(
                    `RAW WINDOW = ${hex(rawStart)} .. ${hex(rawEnd)}`
                );
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

                for (
                    let x = rawStart;
                    x < rawEnd;
                    x += 16
                ) {
                    const y = Math.min(x + 16, rawEnd);

                    const bytes = Array.from(
                        buf.subarray(x, y)
                    )
                        .map((v) =>
                            v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                        )
                        .join(" ");

                    console.log(`${hex(x)}  ${bytes}`);
                }

                console.log("============================================================");
            }
        }

        const len = Math.max(1, opcodeLength(pos));
        pos += len;
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(pos: number, count: number): string {
    return Array.from(bytes.subarray(pos, pos + count))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const sectionId = bytes[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
        const payloadSize = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const payloadStart = p;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return {
                start: payloadStart,
                size: payloadSize,
            };
        }

        p += payloadSize;
    }

    throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" IMPORT #60 CALL TRACE v40");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" TARGET: function #60 = a.ga");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size: ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);

const code = findSection(10);

console.log(
    `Code section: offset=0x${code.start.toString(16)} size=${code.size}`
);
console.log();

let p = code.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readU32Leb(p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log(`Defined function bodies: ${bodyCount}`);
console.log();

let found = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localDecls = readU32Leb(q);
    q = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
        const count = readU32Leb(q);
        q = count.next;

        // Skip valtype byte.
        q++;
    }

    const instructionStart = q;

    for (let i = instructionStart; i < bodyEnd; i++) {
        // call opcode
        if (bytes[i] !== 0x10) continue;

        const target = readU32Leb(i + 1);

        if (target.value !== 60) continue;

        found++;

        // Function index = imported functions + body index.
        const callerFunc = 83 + bodyIndex;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`MATCH #${found}`);
        console.log(`callerFunc  = ${callerFunc}`);
        console.log(`bodyIndex   = ${bodyIndex}`);
        console.log(`callOffset  = 0x${i.toString(16)}`);

        if (callerFunc === 1020) {
            console.log("*** THIS IS FUNCTION 1020 ***");
        }

        console.log();
        console.log("Bytes around call:");

        const start = Math.max(instructionStart, i - 24);
        const end = Math.min(bodyEnd, i + 24);

        console.log(
            `0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${(end - 1).toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(hex(start, end - start));
        console.log();
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALLS TO IMPORT #60 (a.ga): ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceIndirect4815.ts

import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4815;
const TARGET_TABLE_INDEX = 7969;

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    args?: any;
};

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number, bits = 32) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function nameOf(op: number) {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",

        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    };

    return names[op] ??
        `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeBody(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const offset = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let args: any = undefined;

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                local: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                global: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 32);

            args = {
                value: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 64);

            args = {
                value: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);

            args = {
                func: r.value,
            };

            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                type: a.value,
                table: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        out.push({
            offset,
            op,
            name: nameOf(op),
            args,
        });

        if (op === 0x0b) break;
    }

    return out;
}

function readCodeSection(buf: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;

        if (id === 10) {
            const end = payload + size;

            let q = payload;

            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            q = countInfo.next;

            const bodies: any[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
                const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
                q = sizeInfo.next;

                const bodyStart = q;
                const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

                let x = bodyStart;

                const localInfo = readULEB(buf, x);
                const localDeclCount = localInfo.value;
                x = localInfo.next;

                for (
                    let j = 0;
                    j < localDeclCount;
                    j++
                ) {
                    const n = readULEB(buf, x);
                    x = n.next;

                    x++;
                }

                bodies.push({
                    func: 83 + i,
                    instrStart: x,
                    instrEnd: bodyEnd,
                });

                q = bodyEnd;
            }

            if (q !== end) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Code parse mismatch: 0x${q.toString(16)} != 0x${end.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        p = payload + size;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found.");
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TRACE INDIRECT DISPATCH TO FUNCTION 4815");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(`WASM size        : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(`Target table idx : ${TARGET_TABLE_INDEX}`);
    console.log("");

    const bodies = readCodeSection(wasm);

    console.log(
        `Functions decoded : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log("");

    let indirectCount = 0;
    let type0Table0 = 0;
    let exactConst = 0;
    let memoryLoad = 0;
    let localDerived = 0;
    let unknown = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const instr = decodeBody(
            wasm,
            body.instrStart,
            body.instrEnd
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < instr.length; i++) {
            const x = instr[i];

            if (x.name !== "call_indirect") {
                continue;
            }

            indirectCount++;

            /*
             * Previous investigation established that
             * the relevant dispatch family uses:
             *
             * type = 0
             * table = 0
             */
            if (
                x.args?.type !== 0 ||
                x.args?.table !== 0
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            type0Table0++;

            const from = Math.max(0, i - 10);
            const before = instr.slice(from, i);

            let classification = "unknown";

            /*
             * Search backward through a short stack-producing window.
             */

            for (let j = before.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
                const b = before[j];

                if (
                    b.name === "i32.const"
                ) {
                    if (
                        b.args?.value ===
                        TARGET_TABLE_INDEX
                    ) {
                        classification =
                            "EXACT_CONST_7969";
                    } else {
                        classification =
                            "OTHER_CONST";
                    }

                    break;
                }

                if (
                    b.name === "i32.load" ||
                    b.name === "i32.load8_s" ||
                    b.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
                    b.name === "i32.load16_s" ||
                    b.name === "i32.load16_u"
                ) {
                    classification =
                        "MEMORY_LOAD";
                    break;
                }

                if (
                    b.name === "local.get"
                ) {
                    classification =
                        "LOCAL_DERIVED";
                    break;
                }

                if (
                    b.name === "global.get"
                ) {
                    classification =
                        "GLOBAL_DERIVED";
                    break;
                }

                if (
                    b.name === "call"
                ) {
                    classification =
                        "CALL_DERIVED";
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (classification === "EXACT_CONST_7969") {
                exactConst++;

                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log("EXACT TABLE INDEX MATCH");
                console.log("============================================================");

                console.log(
                    `Function : ${body.func}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Offset   : 0x${x.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Index    : ${TARGET_TABLE_INDEX}`
                );

                console.log("");

                for (
                    const b of before
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `  0x${b.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ${b.name}` +
                        (
                            b.args
                                ? " " +
                                  JSON.stringify(b.args)
                                : ""
                        )
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `  >>> 0x${x.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} call_indirect type=0 table=0`
                );

                console.log("");
            }

            else if (
                classification === "MEMORY_LOAD"
            ) {
                memoryLoad++;
            }

            else if (
                classification === "LOCAL_DERIVED"
            ) {
                localDerived++;
            }

            else {
                unknown++;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `All call_indirect        : ${indirectCount}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Type0 / table0           : ${type0Table0}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Exact CONST(7969)        : ${exactConst}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Memory-derived           : ${memoryLoad}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Local-derived            : ${localDerived}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Unknown                  : ${unknown}`
    );

    console.log("");

    if (exactConst > 0) {
        console.log(
            "DIRECT STATIC DISPATCH TO TABLE INDEX 7969 FOUND."
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            "No direct CONST(7969) producer found."
        );

        console.log(
            "The dispatch index is likely memory/object-derived."
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Target mapping:"
    );
    console.log(
        `  table[${TARGET_TABLE_INDEX}] = Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectCallIndex.ts
