import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;
const IMPORTED   = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const x = b[pos + i];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value, end: pos + i + 1 };

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function bodies() {
  let p = CODE_START;
  const c = readULEB(p)!;
  p = c.end;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = readULEB(p)!;
    p = s.end;

    out.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  if (p !== CODE_END)
    throw new Error("CODE END mismatch");

  return out;
}

function ins(p: number) {
  const op = b[p];

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      op,
      kind:
        op === 0x20 ? "get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "set" : "tee",
      local: x.value,
      text:
        `${op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
          op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
          "local.tee"} ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      op,
      kind: "const",
      value: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x6a)
    return { end: p + 1, op, kind: "add", text: "i32.add" };

  if (op === 0x6b)
    return { end: p + 1, op, kind: "sub", text: "i32.sub" };

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!a) return null;

    const o = readULEB(a.end);
    if (!o) return null;

    return {
      end: o.end,
      op,
      kind: "load",
      offset: o.value,
      text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!a) return null;

    const o = readULEB(a.end);
    if (!o) return null;

    return {
      end: o.end,
      op,
      kind: "load8",
      offset: o.value,
      text: `i32.load8_u offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      op,
      kind: "call",
      target: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!t) return null;

    const tab = readULEB(t.end);
    if (!tab) return null;

    return {
      end: tab.end,
      op,
      kind: "indirect",
      type: t.value,
      text:
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`
    };
  }

  return {
    end: p + 1,
    op,
    kind: "other",
    text: `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };
}

function decodeBody(body: any) {
  const out: any[] = [];
  let p = body.start;

  while (p < body.end) {
    const d = ins(p);
    if (!d || d.end <= p || d.end > body.end)
      break;

    out.push({
      pos: p,
      ...d
    });

    p = d.end;
  }

  return out;
}

/*
  Lightweight stack simulation.

  We only care about the stack immediately before
  call_indirect.

  Each stack item is represented symbolically.
*/

function trace(body: any, instructions: any[]) {

  const stack: string[] = [];

  for (const d of instructions) {

    switch (d.kind) {

      case "get":
        stack.push(`local[${d.local}]`);
        break;

      case "const":
        stack.push(`const(${d.value})`);
        break;

      case "load": {

        const base =
          stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : "UNKNOWN";

        stack.push(
          `load(${base}+${d.offset})`
        );

        break;
      }

      case "load8": {

        const base =
          stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : "UNKNOWN";

        stack.push(
          `load8(${base}+${d.offset})`
        );

        break;
      }

      case "add": {

        const b2 =
          stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "UNKNOWN";

        const a =
          stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "UNKNOWN";

        stack.push(
          `(${a}+${b2})`
        );

        break;
      }

      case "sub": {

        const b2 =
          stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "UNKNOWN";

        const a =
          stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "UNKNOWN";

        stack.push(
          `(${a}-${b2})`
        );

        break;
      }

      case "tee": {

        const a =
          stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "UNKNOWN";

        stack.push(a);

        break;
      }

      case "set":
        if (stack.length)
          stack.pop();

        break;

      case "call": {

        /*
          Unknown number of arguments.
          Preserve a symbolic result so the stack
          does not collapse completely.
        */

        stack.push(
          `result(call ${d.target})`
        );

        break;
      }

      case "indirect": {

        if (d.type !== 4)
          break;

        /*
          The index is the LAST stack item.
        */

        const index =
          stack.length
            ? stack[stack.length - 1]
            : "UNKNOWN";

        console.log("");
        console.log(
          `FUNCTION ${body.global} BODY ${body.ordinal}`
        );
        console.log(
          `CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(d.pos)}`
        );
        console.log(
          `INDEX = ${index}`
        );

        if (
          index.includes("load(local[") &&
          index.includes("+4)")
        ) {
          console.log(
            ">>> FIELD +4 DISPATCH CANDIDATE <<<"
          );
        }

        stack.pop();

        /*
          call_indirect consumes its arguments too,
          but we deliberately don't guess their count.
        */

        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

const all = bodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BACKWARD SYMBOLIC INDIRECT INDEX TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let count = 0;

for (const body of all) {

  const instructions = decodeBody(body);

  for (const d of instructions) {

    if (
      d.kind === "indirect" &&
      d.type === 4
    ) {
      count++;
    }
  }

  trace(body, instructions);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT TYPE 4 = ${count}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectDispatchFlow.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;
const IMPORTED   = 83;

const TARGETS = new Set([8637, 8927, 8952]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count)
    throw new Error("Bad code count");

  p = count.end;

  const bodies: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p);
    if (!size)
      throw new Error(`Bad body size @ ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  if (p !== CODE_END)
    throw new Error(
      `CODE END MISMATCH ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
    );

  return bodies;
}

function decode(pos: number) {

  const op = b[pos];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
      kind: "get",
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
      kind: "set",
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
      kind: "tee",
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      end: x.end,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      kind: "const",
      value: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    return {
      end: pos + 1,
      text: "i32.add",
      kind: "add"
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    return {
      end: pos + 1,
      text: "i32.sub",
      kind: "sub"
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!a) return null;

    const o = readULEB(a.end);
    if (!o) return null;

    return {
      end: o.end,
      text: `i32.load offset=${o.value}`,
      kind: "load",
      offset: o.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {

    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!a) return null;

    const o = readULEB(a.end);
    if (!o) return null;

    return {
      end: o.end,
      text: `i32.load8_u offset=${o.value}`,
      kind: "load8",
      offset: o.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const t = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!t) return null;

    const table = readULEB(t.end);
    if (!table) return null;

    return {
      end: table.end,
      text:
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      kind: "indirect",
      type: t.value
    };
  }

  return {
    end: pos + 1,
    text: `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`,
    kind: "other"
  };
}

function localUses(
  body: any,
  center: number,
  local: number
) {
  const hits: string[] = [];

  const from = Math.max(body.start, center - 256);
  const to = Math.min(body.end, center + 32);

  for (let p = from; p < to; p++) {

    if (
      b[p] !== 0x20 &&
      b[p] !== 0x21 &&
      b[p] !== 0x22
    )
      continue;

    const x = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (!x)
      continue;

    if (x.value !== local)
      continue;

    const op =
      b[p] === 0x20 ? "get" :
      b[p] === 0x21 ? "set" :
      "tee";

    hits.push(
      `${hex(p)} local.${op} ${local}`
    );
  }

  return hits;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INDIRECT DISPATCH FLOW TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("Focus: i32.load(+4) -> call_indirect type=4");
console.log("Targets: 8637 / 8927 / 8952");
console.log("");

let exact = 0;
let target = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (b[p] !== 0x28)
      continue;

    const a = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!a) continue;

    const o = readULEB(a.end);
    if (!o) continue;

    if (o.value !== 4)
      continue;

    const q = o.end;

    if (
      b[q] !== 0x11 ||
      b[q + 1] !== 0x04 ||
      b[q + 2] !== 0x00
    )
      continue;

    exact++;

    if (!TARGETS.has(body.global))
      continue;

    target++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNCTION       ${body.global}`);
    console.log(`BODY           ${body.ordinal}`);
    console.log(`LOAD(+4)       ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT  ${hex(q)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    /*
       Decode preceding instructions.
    */

    const ins: any[] = [];

    let x = Math.max(body.start, p - 96);

    while (x < p) {

      const d = decode(x);

      if (!d || d.end <= x || d.end > p)
        break;

      ins.push({
        pos: x,
        ...d
      });

      x = d.end;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("PRECEDING INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("----------------------");

    for (const d of ins.slice(-24)) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(d.pos)}  ${d.text}`
      );
    }

    /*
       Find the immediate base of load(+4).
    */

    const last = ins[ins.length - 1];

    console.log("");
    console.log("LOAD(+4) BASE ANALYSIS");
    console.log("----------------------");

    if (last?.kind === "get") {

      console.log(
        `BASE = local ${last.local}`
      );

      const uses = localUses(
        body,
        p,
        last.local
      );

      console.log(
        `LOCAL ${last.local} USES = ${uses.length}`
      );

      for (const u of uses.slice(-20))
        console.log(`  ${u}`);

    } else {

      console.log(
        `BASE is expression ending with: ${last?.text ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
      );

      if (last) {

        const tail = ins.slice(-8);

        console.log("");
        console.log("BASE EXPRESSION TAIL");

        for (const d of tail) {
          console.log(
            `  ${hex(d.pos)} ${d.text}`
          );
        }
      }
    }

    /*
       Search nearby stores to offset +4.
    */

    console.log("");
    console.log("NEARBY STORE(+4) CANDIDATES");
    console.log("---------------------------");

    let stores = 0;

    const fromStore =
      Math.max(body.start, p - 512);

    for (
      let s = fromStore;
      s < Math.min(body.end, p + 16);
      s++
    ) {

      if (b[s] !== 0x36)
        continue;

      const a2 = readULEB(s + 1);
      if (!a2) continue;

      const o2 = readULEB(a2.end);
      if (!o2) continue;

      if (o2.value !== 4)
        continue;

      stores++;

      console.log(
        `  i32.store offset=4 @ ${hex(s)}`
      );
    }

    if (stores === 0)
      console.log("  none");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT DISPATCHES = ${exact}`);
console.log(`TARGET DISPATCHES      = ${target}`);
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TABLE_INDEX = 2053;
const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE INDIRECT INDEX 2053");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE        :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET TABLE IDX :", TARGET_TABLE_INDEX);
console.log("TARGET TYPE      :", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

// ============================================================
// LEB128
// ============================================================

function u8() {
    return buf[p++];
}

function u32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function i32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

    return result | 0;
}

function readName() {
    const n = u32();
    const start = p;
    p += n;

    return Buffer
        .from(buf.subarray(start, p))
        .toString("utf8");
}

function hex(x, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ============================================================
// SECTION PARSER
// ============================================================

p = 8;

const sections = [];

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionStart = p;

    const id = u8();
    const size = u32();

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
}

const importSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

const functionSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

const codeSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!functionSection)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

if (!codeSection)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

// ============================================================
// IMPORT FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================

let importedFunctions = 0;
const imports = [];

if (importSection) {

    p = importSection.payloadStart;

    const count = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const module = readName();
        const name = readName();

        const kind = u8();

        let typeIndex = null;

        if (kind === 0x00) {

            typeIndex = u32();

            importedFunctions++;
        }

        else if (kind === 0x01) {

            u8();

            const flags = u8();

            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x02) {

            const flags = u8();

            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x03) {

            u8();
            u8();
        }

        imports.push({
            module,
            name,
            kind,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

console.log(
    "IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :",
    importedFunctions
);

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION TYPE MAP
// ============================================================

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

const definedCount = u32();

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++)
    functionTypes.push(u32());

function getFunctionType(index) {

    if (index < importedFunctions)
        return imports[index]?.typeIndex ?? null;

    return functionTypes[
        index - importedFunctions
    ] ?? null;
}

// ============================================================
// BLOCK TYPE
// ============================================================

function skipBlockType() {

    const b = buf[p];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b
    ) {
        p++;
        return;
    }

    u32();
}

// ============================================================
// MEMORY ARGUMENT
// ============================================================

function skipMemArg() {

    u32(); // alignment
    u32(); // offset
}

// ============================================================
// INSTRUCTION DECODER
// ============================================================

function decodeInstruction() {

    const start = p;

    const op = u8();

    const x = {
        start,
        op,
        name: `op_0x${op.toString(16)}`
    };

    switch (op) {

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CONTROL
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x00:
            x.name = "unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x01:
            x.name = "nop";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            x.name = "block";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x03:
            x.name = "loop";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x04:
            x.name = "if";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x05:
            x.name = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            x.name = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c:
            x.name = "br";
            x.index = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0d:
            x.name = "br_if";
            x.index = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0e: {

            x.name = "br_table";

            const n = u32();

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
                u32();

            u32();

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            x.name = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10:

            x.name = "call";

            x.functionIndex = u32();

            break;

        case 0x11:

            x.name = "call_indirect";

            x.typeIndex = u32();
            x.tableIndex = u32();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // STACK
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x1a:
            x.name = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            x.name = "select";
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // LOCALS / GLOBALS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x20:

            x.name = "local.get";
            x.local = u32();

            break;

        case 0x21:

            x.name = "local.set";
            x.local = u32();

            break;

        case 0x22:

            x.name = "local.tee";
            x.local = u32();

            break;

        case 0x23:

            x.name = "global.get";
            x.global = u32();

            break;

        case 0x24:

            x.name = "global.set";
            x.global = u32();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // LOAD
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x28:

            x.name = "i32.load";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x29:

            x.name = "i64.load";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2a:

            x.name = "f32.load";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2b:

            x.name = "f64.load";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2c:

            x.name = "i32.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2d:

            x.name = "i32.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2e:

            x.name = "i32.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x2f:

            x.name = "i32.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x30:

            x.name = "i64.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x31:

            x.name = "i64.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x32:

            x.name = "i64.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x33:

            x.name = "i64.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x34:

            x.name = "i64.load32_s";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x35:

            x.name = "i64.load32_u";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // STORE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x36:

            x.name = "i32.store";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x37:

            x.name = "i64.store";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x38:

            x.name = "f32.store";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x39:

            x.name = "f64.store";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x3a:

            x.name = "i32.store8";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x3b:

            x.name = "i32.store16";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x3c:

            x.name = "i64.store8";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x3d:

            x.name = "i64.store16";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        case 0x3e:

            x.name = "i64.store32";
            skipMemArg();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x3f:

            x.name = "memory.size";
            u32();

            break;

        case 0x40:

            x.name = "memory.grow";
            u32();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CONSTANTS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x41:

            x.name = "i32.const";
            x.value = i32();

            break;

        case 0x42:

            x.name = "i64.const";

            while (true) {

                const b = u8();

                if (!(b & 0x80))
                    break;
            }

            break;

        case 0x43:

            x.name = "f32.const";
            p += 4;

            break;

        case 0x44:

            x.name = "f64.const";
            p += 8;

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // COMPARISONS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x45: x.name = "i32.eqz"; break;
        case 0x46: x.name = "i32.eq"; break;
        case 0x47: x.name = "i32.ne"; break;
        case 0x48: x.name = "i32.lt_s"; break;
        case 0x49: x.name = "i32.lt_u"; break;
        case 0x4a: x.name = "i32.gt_s"; break;
        case 0x4b: x.name = "i32.gt_u"; break;
        case 0x4c: x.name = "i32.le_s"; break;
        case 0x4d: x.name = "i32.le_u"; break;
        case 0x4e: x.name = "i32.ge_s"; break;
        case 0x4f: x.name = "i32.ge_u"; break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // I32
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x67: x.name = "i32.clz"; break;
        case 0x68: x.name = "i32.ctz"; break;
        case 0x69: x.name = "i32.popcnt"; break;

        case 0x6a: x.name = "i32.add"; break;
        case 0x6b: x.name = "i32.sub"; break;
        case 0x6c: x.name = "i32.mul"; break;

        case 0x6d: x.name = "i32.div_s"; break;
        case 0x6e: x.name = "i32.div_u"; break;

        case 0x6f: x.name = "i32.rem_s"; break;
        case 0x70: x.name = "i32.rem_u"; break;

        case 0x71: x.name = "i32.and"; break;
        case 0x72: x.name = "i32.or"; break;
        case 0x73: x.name = "i32.xor"; break;

        case 0x74: x.name = "i32.shl"; break;
        case 0x75: x.name = "i32.shr_s"; break;
        case 0x76: x.name = "i32.shr_u"; break;

        case 0x77: x.name = "i32.rotl"; break;
        case 0x78: x.name = "i32.rotr"; break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // I64
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x7c: x.name = "i64.add"; break;
        case 0x7d: x.name = "i64.sub"; break;
        case 0x7e: x.name = "i64.mul"; break;
        case 0x7f: x.name = "i64.div_s"; break;
        case 0x80: x.name = "i64.div_u"; break;
        case 0x81: x.name = "i64.rem_s"; break;
        case 0x82: x.name = "i64.rem_u"; break;
        case 0x83: x.name = "i64.and"; break;
        case 0x84: x.name = "i64.or"; break;
        case 0x85: x.name = "i64.xor"; break;
        case 0x86: x.name = "i64.shl"; break;
        case 0x87: x.name = "i64.shr_s"; break;
        case 0x88: x.name = "i64.shr_u"; break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CONVERSIONS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0xa7:
            x.name = "i32.wrap_i64";
            break;

        case 0xac:
            x.name = "i64.extend_i32_s";
            break;

        case 0xad:
            x.name = "i64.extend_i32_u";
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // REFERENCE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0xd0:

            x.name = "ref.null";
            u8();

            break;

        case 0xd2:

            x.name = "ref.func";
            x.functionIndex = u32();

            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // BULK MEMORY / TABLE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0xfc: {

            x.name = "prefix_fc";

            x.subopcode = u32();

            if (
                x.subopcode === 8 ||
                x.subopcode === 9
            ) {
                u32();
                u32();
            }

            else if (
                x.subopcode >= 10 &&
                x.subopcode <= 17
            ) {
                u32();
            }

            break;
        }

        default:

            x.unknown = true;

            break;
    }

    x.end = p;

    return x;
}

p = codeSection.payloadStart;

const bodyCount = u32();

console.log(
    "DEFINED FUNCTIONS :",
    definedCount
);

console.log(
    "CODE BODIES       :",
    bodyCount
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const results = [];

let failures = 0;
let totalIndirect = 0;
let type4Indirect = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodySize = u32();

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const functionIndex =
        importedFunctions + bodyIndex;

    // locals
    const localGroups = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
        u32();
        u8();
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < bodyEnd) {

        const ins = decodeInstruction();

        if (ins.unknown) {

            failures++;

            p = bodyEnd;

            break;
        }

        instructions.push(ins);

        if (ins.name === "call_indirect") {

            totalIndirect++;

            if (ins.typeIndex === TARGET_TYPE) {

                type4Indirect++;

                const idx =
                    instructions.length - 1;

                const contextStart =
                    Math.max(0, idx - 20);

                const context =
                    instructions.slice(contextStart);

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // Search backward for direct constant 2053
                // ------------------------------------------------

                let direct2053 = false;

                for (
                    let j = Math.max(0, idx - 20);
                    j < idx;
                    j++
                ) {

                    const q = instructions[j];

                    if (
                        q.name === "i32.const" &&
                        q.value === TARGET_TABLE_INDEX
                    ) {
                        direct2053 = true;
                    }
                }

                // ------------------------------------------------
                // Look for load offset 4 immediately before call
                // ------------------------------------------------

                let loadOffset4 = false;

                for (
                    let j = Math.max(0, idx - 8);
                    j < idx;
                    j++
                ) {

                    const q = instructions[j];

                    if (
                        q.name === "i32.load"
                    ) {
                        loadOffset4 = true;
                    }
                }

                results.push({
                    functionIndex,
                    functionType:
                        getFunctionType(functionIndex),
                    address: ins.start,
                    direct2053,
                    loadOffset4,
                    context
                });
            }
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}
