====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectIndexDataflow.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set([
  8,
  30,
  36,
  39,
  573,
  1974,
]);

const SELECTORS = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x0019534c, "4788"],
  [0x00195350, "4789"],
  [0x00195354, "4790"],
  [0x00195358, "4791"],
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    v |= ~0 << s;
  }

  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

interface Ins {
  pc: number;
  end: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;

  imm?: number;
  offset?: number;
  align?: number;

  typeIndex?: number;
  tableIndex?: number;
}

function name(op: number): string {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
  };

  return m[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function isLoad(op: number) {
  return op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35;
}

function isStore(op: number) {
  return op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e;
}

function decode(pc: number): Ins {
  const start = pc;
  const op = wasm[pc++];

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = uleb(pc);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: name(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
    const x = sleb(pc);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: name(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (isLoad(op) || isStore(op)) {
    const a = uleb(pc);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      name: name(op),
      align: a.value,
      offset: o.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(pc);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: "call",
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(pc);
    const table = uleb(t.next);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: table.next,
      op,
      name: "call_indirect",
      typeIndex: t.value,
      tableIndex: table.value,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {
    /*
     * blocktype
     */
    if (wasm[pc] === 0x40) {
      pc++;
    } else {
      pc = sleb(pc).next;
    }

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: pc,
      op,
      name: name(op),
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(pc);

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: name(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(pc);
    pc = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      pc = uleb(pc).next;
    }

    pc = uleb(pc).next;

    return {
      pc: start,
      end: pc,
      op,
      name: "br_table",
    };
  }

  return {
    pc: start,
    end: pc,
    op,
    name: name(op),
  };
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * Find CODE section only.
 * ============================================================
 */

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.value;

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE INDIRECT INDEX DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);
console.log(
  `TARGET FUNCS = ${[...TARGET_FUNCS].join(", ")}`
);
console.log("");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * Parse Code Section.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Here "FUNC ORDINAL" means the code-body ordinal, exactly
 * matching the numbering used by the previous successful
 * scripts.
 * ============================================================
 */

const nBodies = uleb(codeStart);
let q = nBodies.next;

interface Body {
  ordinal: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  instrStart: number;
}

const bodies = new Map<number, Body>();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < nBodies.value; ordinal++) {
  const bs = uleb(q);
  q = bs.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

  const locals = uleb(q);
  q = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    /*
     * valtype
     */
    q++;
  }

  bodies.set(ordinal, {
    ordinal,
    bodyStart,
    bodyEnd,
    instrStart: q,
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log(`CODE BODIES = ${bodies.size}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * Value model
 * ============================================================
 */

type Val =
  | {
      kind: "selector";
      addr: number;
      value: number;
      sourcePC: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "const";
      value: number;
      sourcePC: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "local";
      index: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "unknown";
    };

function valText(v: Val): string {
  if (v.kind === "selector") {
    return `SELECTOR_${v.value}@${hex(v.addr)}`;
  }

  if (v.kind === "const") {
    return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;
  }

  if (v.kind === "local") {
    return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
  }

  return "UNKNOWN";
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * Analyze one target function
 * ============================================================
 */

let totalIndirect = 0;
let selectorIndex = 0;
let unknownIndex = 0;

for (const func of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const ins: Ins[] = [];

  let x = body.instrStart;

  while (x < body.bodyEnd) {
    const i = decode(x);
    ins.push(i);

    if (i.end <= x) break;

    x = i.end;
  }

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log(
    `FUNC ${func} | BODY ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `INSTRUCTIONS = ${ins.length}`
  );
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  /*
   * Abstract stack.
   */
  const stack: Val[] = [];

  /*
   * Local values.
   */
  const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

  function pop(): Val {
    return (
      stack.pop() ?? {
        kind: "unknown",
      }
    );
  }

  function push(v: Val) {
    stack.push(v);
  }

  function resolve(v: Val): Val {
    if (v.kind !== "local") return v;

    const x = locals.get(v.index);

    if (!x) return v;

    if (x.kind === "local" && x.index === v.index) {
      return v;
    }

    return x;
  }

  /*
   * Keep a compact history of stack state.
   */
  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const a = ins[i];

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (a.op === 0x41) {
      push({
        kind: "const",
        value: a.imm! >>> 0,
        sourcePC: a.pc,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * local.get
     */
    if (a.op === 0x20) {
      const local = a.imm!;

      const v = locals.get(local);

      if (v) {
        push(v);
      } else {
        push({
          kind: "local",
          index: local,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * local.set
     */
    if (a.op === 0x21) {
      const local = a.imm!;
      const v = resolve(pop());

      locals.set(local, v);

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * local.tee
     */
    if (a.op === 0x22) {
      const local = a.imm!;

      const v =
        stack.length > 0
          ? resolve(stack[stack.length - 1])
          : {
              kind: "unknown" as const,
            };

      locals.set(local, v);

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Exact selector LOAD
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (a.op === 0x28) {
      const address = resolve(pop());

      if (address.kind === "const") {
        const effective =
          (address.value + (a.offset ?? 0)) >>> 0;

        const selector =
          SELECTORS.get(effective);

        if (selector) {
          push({
            kind: "selector",
            addr: effective,
            value: Number(selector),
            sourcePC: a.pc,
          });

          console.log(
            `SELECTOR LOAD @ ${hex(a.pc)} -> ${selector} @ ${hex(effective)}`
          );

          continue;
        }
      }

      push({
        kind: "unknown",
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * call_indirect
     *
     * At this exact point the table index is the TOP stack
     * operand.
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (a.op === 0x11) {
      totalIndirect++;

      const indexValue =
        stack.length > 0
          ? resolve(stack[stack.length - 1])
          : {
              kind: "unknown" as const,
            };

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(a.pc)}`
      );
      console.log(
        `  TYPE INDEX = ${a.typeIndex}`
      );
      console.log(
        `  TABLE      = ${a.tableIndex}`
      );
      console.log(
        `  STACK SIZE = ${stack.length}`
      );
      console.log(
        `  TABLE INDEX VALUE = ${valText(indexValue)}`
      );

      if (indexValue.kind === "selector") {
        selectorIndex++;

        console.log(
          `  >>> EXACT SELECTOR INDEX = ${indexValue.value}`
        );
        console.log(
          `      SOURCE LOAD = ${hex(indexValue.sourcePC)}`
        );
        console.log(
          `      ADDRESS     = ${hex(indexValue.addr)}`
        );
      } else {
        unknownIndex++;

        console.log(
          `  >>> INDEX NOT RESOLVED`
        );
      }

      /*
       * We cannot safely pop the exact function arguments
       * without parsing the function type signature.
       *
       * But the TOP value has already been identified as the
       * table index.
       *
       * Preserve a conservative state after the call.
       */
      stack.length = 0;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Comparisons
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (
      a.op === 0x46 ||
      a.op === 0x47 ||
      a.op === 0x45 ||
      (a.op >= 0x48 && a.op <= 0x4f)
    ) {
      const rhs =
        a.op === 0x45
          ? null
          : resolve(pop());

      const lhs = resolve(pop());

      if (
        lhs.kind === "selector" ||
        rhs?.kind === "selector"
      ) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
          `SELECTOR COMPARISON @ ${hex(a.pc)} ${a.name}`
        );
        console.log(
          `  LHS = ${valText(lhs)}`
        );

        if (rhs) {
          console.log(
            `  RHS = ${valText(rhs)}`
          );
        }
      }

      push({
        kind: "unknown",
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * DROP
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (a.op === 0x1a) {
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Simple arithmetic
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (
      a.op === 0x6a ||
      a.op === 0x6b ||
      a.op === 0x6c ||
      a.op === 0x71 ||
      a.op === 0x72 ||
      a.op === 0x73
    ) {
      const b = resolve(pop());
      const c = resolve(pop());

      /*
       * Preserve selector only for arithmetic with zero.
       */
      if (
        b.kind === "selector" &&
        c.kind === "const" &&
        c.value === 0
      ) {
        push(b);
      } else if (
        c.kind === "selector" &&
        b.kind === "const" &&
        b.value === 0
      ) {
        push(c);
      } else {
        push({
          kind: "unknown",
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Calls
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (a.op === 0x10) {
      /*
       * Unknown number of args.
       */
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Stores
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (isStore(a.op)) {
      pop();
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * ========================================================
     * Control flow
     * ========================================================
     */

    if (
      a.op === 0x02 ||
      a.op === 0x03 ||
      a.op === 0x04 ||
      a.op === 0x05 ||
      a.op === 0x0b ||
      a.op === 0x0c ||
      a.op === 0x0d ||
      a.op === 0x0e ||
      a.op === 0x0f
    ) {
      /*
       * Do not carry stack state across unknown CFG edges.
       */
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Unknown opcode:
     * invalidate stack to avoid false positives.
     */
    stack.length = 0;
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log(
  "============================================================"
);
console.log("FINAL SUMMARY");
console.log(
  "============================================================"
);
console.log(
  `CALL_INDIRECT FOUND       = ${totalIndirect}`
);
console.log(
  `EXACT SELECTOR INDEX      = ${selectorIndex}`
);
console.log(
  `UNRESOLVED INDEX          = ${unknownIndex}`
);
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectIndexSource.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const TARGETS = new Set([2053, 2101, 2612]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function i32(pos: number) {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

type Ins = {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    offset?: number;
};

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            out.push({ pos: at, op, text: "end" });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const n =
                op === 0x20
                    ? "local.get"
                    : op === 0x21
                        ? "local.set"
                        : "local.tee";

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text: `${n} ${r.value}`,
                imm: r.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text:
                    op === 0x23
                        ? `global.get ${r.value}`
                        : `global.set ${r.value}`,
                imm: r.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
                imm: r.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text: `call ${r.value}`,
                imm: r.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const r1 = uleb(p);
            p = r1.next;

            const r2 = uleb(p);
            p = r2.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text:
                    `call_indirect type=${r1.value} table=${r2.value}`,
                imm: r1.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Memory loads.
         */
        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d
        ) {
            const align = wasm[p++];
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x28: "i32.load",
                0x29: "i64.load",
                0x2a: "f32.load",
                0x2b: "f64.load",
                0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                0x2d: "i32.load8_u"
            };

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                text:
                    `${names[op]} align=${align} offset=${r.value}`,
                offset: r.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Integer arithmetic / comparisons.
         */
        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x45: "i32.eqz",
            0x46: "i32.eq",
            0x47: "i32.ne",
            0x48: "i32.lt_s",
            0x49: "i32.lt_u",
            0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
            0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
            0x4c: "i32.le_s",
            0x4d: "i32.le_u",
            0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
            0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
            0x6a: "i32.add",
            0x6b: "i32.sub",
            0x6c: "i32.mul",
            0x71: "i32.and",
            0x72: "i32.or",
            0x73: "i32.xor",
            0x74: "i32.shl",
            0x75: "i32.shr_s",
            0x76: "i32.shr_u",
            0x77: "i32.rotl",
            0x78: "i32.rotr",
            0x0f: "return",
            0x1a: "drop",
            0x1b: "select"
        };

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op,
            text: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
        });
    }

    return out;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const start = r.next;
    const end = start + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

/*
 * Decode all function bodies.
 */
const bodies: {
    globalFn: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instructions: Ins[];
}[] = [];

let q = codeStart;
const bodyCount = uleb(q);
q = bodyCount.next;

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount.value; body++) {
    const size = uleb(q);
    const bodyStart = size.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

    let x = bodyStart;

    const localGroups = uleb(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < localGroups.value; g++) {
        const n = uleb(x);
        x = n.next;

        x += 1;
    }

    const globalFn = IMPORT_COUNT + body;

    bodies.push({
        globalFn,
        start: x,
        end: bodyEnd,
        instructions: decodeBody(x, bodyEnd)
    });

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INDIRECT INDEX SOURCE TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`CODE      : ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);
console.log(`FUNCTIONS : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log();

let dispatchCount = 0;
let targetPatternCount = 0;

/*
 * We only want call_indirect(type=4).
 *
 * For each one, reconstruct the operand stack approximately.
 * The LAST stack item is the indirect-call index.
 */
for (const fn of bodies) {

    const stack: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < fn.instructions.length; i++) {

        const ins = fn.instructions[i];

        if (ins.op === 0x20) {
            stack.push(
                ins.imm! < 2
                    ? `param[${ins.imm}]`
                    : `local[${ins.imm}]`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x41) {
            stack.push(`const(${ins.imm})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x23) {
            stack.push(`global[${ins.imm}]`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x28) {
            const base = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `MEM[${base}+${ins.offset}]`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x2d) {
            const base = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `MEM8[${base}+${ins.offset}]`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x6a) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}+${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x6b) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}-${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x6c) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}*${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x71) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}&${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x72) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}|${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x73) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}^${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x74 || ins.op === 0x75 || ins.op === 0x76) {
            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `SHIFT(${a},${b})`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x10) {
            /*
             * Do not try to emulate arbitrary call results.
             * Represent result symbolically.
             */
            stack.push(`RESULT(call ${ins.imm})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (
            ins.op === 0x11 &&
            ins.imm === 4
        ) {
            dispatchCount++;

            const index =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : "UNKNOWN";

            /*
             * Look for target table-index constants
             * inside the symbolic expression.
             */
            const hit =
                [...TARGETS]
                    .filter(v =>
                        index.includes(`const(${v})`)
                    );

            if (hit.length) {
                targetPatternCount++;

                console.log(
                    `TARGET INDEX HIT @ ${hex(ins.pos)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Function : ${fn.globalFn}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Index    : ${index}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Targets  : ${hit.join(", ")}`
                );

                console.log();
            }

            /*
             * Print only potentially useful dispatches:
             * - index is a memory load
             * - or index contains a local/param
             * - or index is derived from a memory load.
             */
            if (
                index.startsWith("MEM[") ||
                index.startsWith("MEM8[") ||
                index.includes("MEM[") ||
                index.includes("MEM8[")
            ) {

                console.log(
                    `DISPATCH #${dispatchCount} @ ${hex(ins.pos)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Function : ${fn.globalFn}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  INDEX    : ${index}`
                );

                /*
                 * Show previous 10 instructions.
                 */
                const begin =
                    Math.max(0, i - 10);

                for (
                    let j = begin;
                    j <= i;
                    j++
                ) {
                    const z = fn.instructions[j];

                    console.log(
                        `    ${hex(z.pos)}  ${z.text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log();
            }

            /*
             * call_indirect consumes its arguments and index.
             * Exact arity is unknown here, so reset symbolic stack.
             * This intentionally prevents false propagation.
             */
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x22) {
            /*
             * tee leaves value on stack.
             * We don't have local-state reconstruction here;
             * retain symbolic value.
             */
            const v =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `local[${ins.imm}]=${v}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x21) {
            if (stack.length)
                stack.pop();

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Conservative handling of instructions that consume
         * unknown operands.
         */
        if (
            ins.op === 0x1a ||
            ins.op === 0x0f
        ) {
            if (stack.length)
                stack.pop();

            continue;
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(
    `CALL_INDIRECT type=4 : ${dispatchCount}`
);

console.log(
    `TARGET CONST HITS    : ${targetPatternCount}`
);

console.log();

console.log("Target table indices:");
console.log("  2053 -> Function 8952");
console.log("  2101 -> Function 8927");
console.log("  2612 -> Function 8637");

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectTarget4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 4154;
const TARGET_SLOT = 114;
const TARGET_TYPE = 0;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

type TypeDef = {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
};

type Body = {
    functionIndex: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    instrStart: number;
    instrEnd: number;
    locals: number[];
};

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm: number[];
};

type Value =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "unknown"; origin: string };

function uleb(p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 0;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= -1 << shift;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function name(p: number) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    const end = n.next + n.value;

    return {
        value: wasm.subarray(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function sections() {
    const out: any[] = [];
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        out.push({
            id,
            payload: p,
            size: n.value,
            end: p + n.value,
        });

        p += n.value;
    }

    return out;
}

function parseTypes(sec: any): TypeDef[] {
    let p = sec.payload;
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const out: TypeDef[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Bad type form ${hex(form, 2)}`);
        }

        const pc = uleb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + pc.value)];
        p += pc.value;

        const rc = uleb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + rc.value)];
        p += rc.value;

        out.push({ params, results });
    }

    return out;
}

function parseImports(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const funcs: number[] = [];
    const info: any[] = [];

    let fi = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const mod = name(p);
        p = mod.next;

        const nm = name(p);
        p = nm.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;

            funcs.push(t.value);

            info.push({
                functionIndex: fi++,
                module: mod.value,
                name: nm.value,
                typeIndex: t.value,
            });
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p++;
            const elem = uleb(p);
            p = elem.next;
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = wasm[p++];

            const min = uleb(p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = uleb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else if (kind === 4) {
            p++;
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return { funcs, info };
}

function parseFunctionTypes(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const out: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;
        out.push(t.value);
    }

    return out;
}

function parseCode(sec: any, importedCount: number): Body[] {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const out: Body[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(p);
        const bodyStart = bs.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

        p = bodyStart;

        const lc = uleb(p);
        p = lc.next;

        const locals: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < lc.value; j++) {
            const count = uleb(p);
            p = count.next;

            const type = wasm[p++];

            for (let k = 0; k < count.value; k++) {
                locals.push(type);
            }
        }

        out.push({
            functionIndex: importedCount + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart: p,
            instrEnd: bodyEnd,
            locals,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return out;
}

/*
 * Core instruction decoder.
 *
 * Important:
 * We never search raw 0x11 bytes.
 * Every 0x11 below is accepted only when decodeInstruction()
 * reaches it as an instruction boundary.
 */

function decodeInstruction(p: number, end: number): Instr {
    const start = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];
    const imm: number[] = [];

    const text = (s: string) => ({
        offset: start,
        end: p,
        op,
        text: s,
        imm,
    });

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00: return text("unreachable");
        case 0x01: return text("nop");

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const b = wasm[p++];

            if (b === 0x40) {
                return text(
                    `${op === 2 ? "block" : op === 3 ? "loop" : "if"} void`
                );
            }

            const r = sleb(p - 1);
            p = r.next;

            return text(
                `${op === 2 ? "block" : op === 3 ? "loop" : "if"} type`
            );
        }

        case 0x05:
            return text("else");

        case 0x0b:
            return text("end");

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);
            return text(`br ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);
            return text(`br_if ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                imm.push(x.value);
            }

            const d = uleb(p);
            p = d.next;
            imm.push(d.value);

            return text("br_table");
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return text("return");

        case 0x10: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);
            return text(`call ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            imm.push(t.value, table.value);

            return text(
                `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
            );
        }

        case 0x1a:
            return text("drop");

        case 0x1b:
            return text("select");

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);

            const n =
                op === 0x20
                    ? "local.get"
                    : op === 0x21
                    ? "local.set"
                    : "local.tee";

            return text(`${n} ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);

            return text(
                `${op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set"} ${r.value}`
            );
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e:
        case 0x3f: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            imm.push(a.value, o.value);

            return text(
                `memory op align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
            );
        }

        case 0x40:
            return text("memory.grow");

        case 0x41: {
            const r = sleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);
            return text(`i32.const ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = sleb(p);
            p = r.next;
            imm.push(r.value);
            return text(`i64.const ${r.value}`);
        }

        case 0x43:
            p += 4;
            return text("f32.const");

        case 0x44:
            p += 8;
            return text("f64.const");

        default:
            /*
             * Numeric/conversion instructions without immediates.
             * This intentionally accepts the standard core opcode
             * ranges. Unknown prefixed instructions are rejected.
             */
            if (
                (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0xc4)
            ) {
                return text(`op_${hex(op, 2)}`);
            }

            /*
             * 0xfc prefixed instructions have variable immediates.
             */
            if (op === 0xfc) {
                const sub = uleb(p);
                p = sub.next;

                /*
                 * saturating conversions: no additional immediate
                 */
                if (sub.value <= 7) {
                    return text(`0xfc.${sub.value}`);
                }

                /*
                 * memory/table instructions with one/two immediates.
                 * Parse conservatively.
                 */
                if (
                    sub.value === 8 ||
                    sub.value === 9 ||
                    sub.value === 10 ||
                    sub.value === 11 ||
                    sub.value === 12 ||
                    sub.value === 13 ||
                    sub.value === 14 ||
                    sub.value === 15 ||
                    sub.value === 16 ||
                    sub.value === 17
                ) {
                    const a = uleb(p);
                    p = a.next;
                    imm.push(a.value);

                    if (
                        sub.value === 8 ||
                        sub.value === 10 ||
                        sub.value === 12 ||
                        sub.value === 14 ||
                        sub.value === 16
                    ) {
                        const b = uleb(p);
                        p = b.next;
                        imm.push(b.value);
                    }

                    return text(`0xfc.${sub.value}`);
                }

                return text(`0xfc.${sub.value}`);
            }

            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported opcode ${hex(op, 2)} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
    }
}

function decodeBody(body: Body): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = body.instrStart;

    while (p < body.instrEnd) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(p, body.instrEnd);
        out.push(ins);
        p = ins.end;

        if (ins.op === 0x0b && p >= body.instrEnd) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function functionType(
    index: number,
    importedTypes: number[],
    definedTypes: number[]
) {
    if (index < importedTypes.length) {
        return importedTypes[index];
    }

    return definedTypes[index - importedTypes.length];
}

function typeSignature(
    index: number,
    types: TypeDef[],
    importedTypes: number[],
    definedTypes: number[]
) {
    const ti = functionType(index, importedTypes, definedTypes);

    if (ti === undefined || !types[ti]) {
        return { params: [], results: [] };
    }

    return types[ti];
}

function valueName(v: Value | undefined) {
    if (!v) return "EMPTY";

    if (v.kind === "const") {
        return `CONST(${v.value})`;
    }

    if (v.kind === "local") {
        return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
    }

    return "UNKNOWN";
}

function simulate(
    body: Body,
    instrs: Instr[],
    types: TypeDef[],
    importedTypes: number[],
    definedTypes: number[]
) {
    const stack: Value[] = [];

    const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

    /*
     * Parameters begin unknown.
     */
    const sig = typeSignature(
        body.functionIndex,
        types,
        importedTypes,
        definedTypes
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < sig.params.length; i++) {
        locals.set(i, {
            kind: "local",
            index: i,
            origin: `parameter ${i}`,
        });
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < body.locals.length; i++) {
        locals.set(
            sig.params.length + i,
            {
                kind: "unknown",
                origin: "local",
            }
        );
    }

    const hits: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < instrs.length; i++) {
        const ins = instrs[i];

        if (ins.op === 0x41) {
            stack.push({
                kind: "const",
                value: ins.imm[0],
                origin: hex(ins.offset),
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x20) {
            const idx = ins.imm[0];

            const v = locals.get(idx);

            stack.push(
                v ?? {
                    kind: "local",
                    index: idx,
                    origin: `local ${idx}`,
                }
            );

            continue;
        }

     if (ins.op === 0x21) {
    const idx = ins.imm[0];

    let v: Value;

    if (stack.length > 0) {
        v = stack.pop() as Value;
    } else {
        v = {
            kind: "unknown",
            origin: "empty-stack",
        } as Value;
    }

    locals.set(idx, v);
    continue;
}

             if (ins.op === 0x22) {
            const idx = ins.imm[0];

            let v: Value;

            if (stack.length > 0) {
                v = stack[stack.length - 1] as Value;
            } else {
                v = {
                    kind: "unknown",
                    origin: "empty-stack",
                } as Value;
            }

            locals.set(idx, v);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x1a) {
            stack.pop();
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x10) {
            const callee = ins.imm[0];

            const s = typeSignature(
                callee,
                types,
                importedTypes,
                definedTypes
            );

            for (let j = 0; j < s.params.length; j++) {
                stack.pop();
            }

            for (const r of s.results) {
                stack.push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    origin: `result of call ${callee}`,
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x11) {
            const typeIndex = ins.imm[0];
            const tableIndex = ins.imm[1];

            /*
             * The runtime table slot is the TOP stack value
             * after all arguments have been pushed.
             */
            const targetIndex =
                stack.length > 0
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : undefined;

            if (
                tableIndex === 0 &&
                typeIndex === TARGET_TYPE &&
                targetIndex?.kind === "const" &&
                targetIndex.value === TARGET_SLOT
            ) {
                hits.push({
                    callOffset: ins.offset,
                    targetIndex,
                    typeIndex,
                    tableIndex,
                });
            }

            /*
             * call_indirect consumes the function table index
             * plus all parameters of the referenced signature.
             */
            const s =
                types[typeIndex] ?? {
                    params: [],
                    results: [],
                };

            stack.pop();

            for (let j = 0; j < s.params.length; j++) {
                stack.pop();
            }

            for (const r of s.results) {
                stack.push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    origin: `result of call_indirect`,
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Binary i32 operations consume two values and produce one.
         * We preserve CONST only for the simplest constant case.
         */
        if (
            ins.op >= 0x6a &&
            ins.op <= 0x78
        ) {
            const b = stack.pop();
            const a = stack.pop();

            if (
                a?.kind === "const" &&
                b?.kind === "const"
            ) {
                let value = 0;

                switch (ins.op) {
                    case 0x6a:
                        value = (a.value + b.value) | 0;
                        break;
                    case 0x6b:
                        value = (a.value - b.value) | 0;
                        break;
                    case 0x6c:
                        value = Math.imul(a.value, b.value);
                        break;
                    default:
                        stack.push({
                            kind: "unknown",
                            origin: `binary ${hex(ins.op, 2)}`,
                        });
                        continue;
                }

                stack.push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value,
                    origin: `computed ${hex(ins.offset)}`,
                });
            } else {
                stack.push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    origin: `binary ${hex(ins.op, 2)}`,
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Comparisons consume operands and return i32.
         */
        if (ins.op >= 0x45 && ins.op <= 0x4f) {
            stack.pop();
            stack.pop();

            stack.push({
                kind: "unknown",
                origin: `comparison ${hex(ins.offset)}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Loads: address -> value.
         */
        if (
            (ins.op >= 0x28 && ins.op <= 0x35)
        ) {
            stack.pop();

            stack.push({
                kind: "unknown",
                origin: `load ${hex(ins.offset)}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Stores.
         */
        if (
            (ins.op >= 0x36 && ins.op <= 0x3e)
        ) {
            stack.pop();
            stack.pop();
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Other instructions are conservatively treated as
         * producing an unknown value only when required later.
         *
         * We do NOT fabricate call_indirect matches.
         */
    }

    return hits;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DEFINITIVE INDIRECT CALL TRACE -> FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log("WASM size       :", wasm.length);
    console.log("Target function :", TARGET_FUNCTION);
    console.log("Target slot     :", hex(TARGET_SLOT), "=", TARGET_SLOT);
    console.log("Target type     :", TARGET_TYPE);
    console.log();

    const sec = sections();

    const typeSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 1);
    const importSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 2);
    const functionSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 3);
    const codeSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 10);

    if (!typeSec || !importSec || !functionSec || !codeSec) {
        throw new Error("Required sections missing");
    }

    const types = parseTypes(typeSec);
    const imports = parseImports(importSec);
    const definedTypes = parseFunctionTypes(functionSec);
    const bodies = parseCode(codeSec, imports.funcs.length);

    console.log("Types          :", types.length);
    console.log("Imported funcs :", imports.funcs.length);
    console.log("Defined funcs  :", definedTypes.length);
    console.log("Code bodies    :", bodies.length);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET FUNCTION");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const targetBody = bodies.find(
        b => b.functionIndex === TARGET_FUNCTION
    );

    if (!targetBody) {
        throw new Error("Target body not found");
    }

    const targetInstr = decodeBody(targetBody);

    for (const x of targetInstr) {
        console.log(hex(x.offset), x.text);
    }

    const targetType = functionType(
        TARGET_FUNCTION,
        imports.funcs,
        definedTypes
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("Function 4154 type index:", targetType);
    console.log("Expected target type    :", TARGET_TYPE);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SCANNING ALL FUNCTIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let decodedBodies = 0;
    let indirectCount = 0;
    let targetHits = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;

    const candidates: any[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {
        let instrs: Instr[];

        try {
            instrs = decodeBody(body);
            decodedBodies++;
        } catch {
            decodeErrors++;
            continue;
        }

        for (const x of instrs) {
            if (x.op === 0x11) {
                indirectCount++;
            }
        }

        const hits = simulate(
            body,
            instrs,
            types,
            imports.funcs,
            definedTypes
        );

        for (const h of hits) {
            targetHits++;

            candidates.push({
                functionIndex: body.functionIndex,
                callOffset: h.callOffset,
                typeIndex: h.typeIndex,
                tableIndex: h.tableIndex,
            });
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("RESULT");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("Decoded bodies       :", decodedBodies);
    console.log("Decode errors        :", decodeErrors);
    console.log("Real call_indirects  :", indirectCount);
    console.log("Target matches       :", targetHits);
    console.log();

    if (candidates.length === 0) {
        console.log("NO DEFINITIVE MATCH FOUND.");
        console.log();
        console.log(
            "This means no instruction-decoded path currently"
        );
        console.log(
            "proves CONST(114) -> call_indirect(type=0, table=0)."
        );
    } else {
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("DEFINITIVE TARGET CALLERS");
        console.log("============================================================");

        for (const c of candidates) {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                "Caller function :",
                c.functionIndex
            );
            console.log(
                "call_indirect   :",
                hex(c.callOffset)
            );
            console.log(
                "type            :",
                c.typeIndex
            );
            console.log(
                "table           :",
                c.tableIndex
            );
            console.log(
                "runtime index   :",
                TARGET_SLOT
            );
            console.log(
                "=> table[0x72] -> function 4154"
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SAFETY / MODE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("No transaction sent.");
    console.log("No WASM modification.");
    console.log("Read-only / offline.");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceIndirectTargetsV22.ts

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM_PATH =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const IMPORTS = 83;

// Functions identified in the previous call graph
const TARGETS = [107, 116, 122];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readI32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            return { value, next: pos };
        }
    }
}

function readName(pos: number, end: number): string {
    const n = readU32Leb(pos);
    const start = n.next;
    const stop = start + n.value;

    if (stop > end) {
        return '';
    }

    return wasm.subarray(start, stop).toString('utf8');
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    let codeOffset = 0;
    let codeSize = 0;

    let typeOffset = 0;
    let typeSize = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const sz = readU32Leb(p);
        p = sz.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + sz.value;

        if (id === 1) {
            typeOffset = start;
            typeSize = sz.value;
        }

        if (id === 10) {
            codeOffset = start;
            codeSize = sz.value;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return {
        codeOffset,
        codeSize,
        typeOffset,
        typeSize,
    };
}

function parseCodeSection(codeOffset: number) {
    let p = codeOffset;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: {
        index: number;
        offset: number;
        size: number;
        bodyOffset: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = readU32Leb(p);
        p = size.next;

        const bodyOffset = p;
        const bodyEnd = bodyOffset + size.value;

        bodies.push({
            index: IMPORTS + i,
            offset: bodyOffset,
            size: size.value,
            bodyOffset,
            bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function readBlockType(pos: number): number {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    // value types / empty block type
    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return 1;
    }

    const r = readI32Leb(pos);
    return r.next - pos;
}

function scanFunction(
    fn: {
        index: number;
        offset: number;
        size: number;
        bodyOffset: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let p = fn.bodyOffset;

    const localGroups = readU32Leb(p);
    p = localGroups.next;

    // Skip local declarations
    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = readU32Leb(p);
        p = n.next;

        // value type
        p++;
    }

    const instructions: {
        pos: number;
        op: number;
        text: string;
        value?: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < fn.bodyEnd) {
        const pos = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        let value: number | undefined;

        switch (op) {
            case 0x41: {
                const r = readI32Leb(p);
                value = r.value;
                p = r.next;
                text = `i32.const ${value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                // i64.const
                let shift = 0;
                let v = 0n;

                while (true) {
                    const b = wasm[p++];
                    v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << BigInt(shift);

                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                        break;
                    }

                    shift += 7;
                }

                text = `i64.const ${v}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;

                if (r.value < IMPORTS) {
                    text = `call IMPORT#${r.value}`;
                } else {
                    text = `call F#${r.value}`;
                }

                value = r.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const type = readU32Leb(p);
                p = type.next;

                const table = readU32Leb(p);
                p = table.next;

                text = `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
                value = type.value;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0b:
                text = 'end';
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const n = readU32Leb(p);
                p = n.next;

                for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++) {
                    const r = readU32Leb(p);
                    p = r.next;
                }

                const def = readU32Leb(p);
                p = def.next;

                text = `br_table ${n.value} targets default=${def.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x02:
            case 0x03:
            case 0x04: {
                p += readBlockType(p);
                text =
                    op === 0x02
                        ? 'block'
                        : op === 0x03
                            ? 'loop'
                            : 'if';
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                text = 'else';
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }

        instructions.push({
            pos,
            op,
            text,
            value,
        });

        if (op === 0x0b && p >= fn.bodyEnd) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return instructions;
}

function showAround(
    instructions: ReturnType<typeof scanFunction>,
    index: number,
    radius = 10
) {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const end = Math.min(instructions.length, index + radius + 1);

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        console.log(
            `    +${(x.pos).toString().padStart(7)}  ${x.text}`
        );
    }
}

const sections = readSections();
const bodies = parseCodeSection(sections.codeOffset);

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' STON/TON SANDBOX WASM - INDIRECT TARGET ANALYZER v22');
console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE WASM');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('');
console.log(`WASM       : ${WASM_PATH}`);
console.log(`WASM size  : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Code funcs : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log(`Imports    : ${IMPORTS}`);
console.log('');

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const fn = bodies.find(x => x.index === target);

    if (!fn) {
        console.log(`F#${target}: NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(`F#${target}`);
    console.log(`offset     : ${fn.offset}`);
    console.log(`size       : ${fn.size}`);
    console.log(`bodyOffset : ${fn.bodyOffset}`);
    console.log('');

    const ins = scanFunction(fn);

    const indirect = ins
        .map((x, i) => ({ x, i }))
        .filter(x => x.x.op === 0x11);

    console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT count: ${indirect.length}`);
    console.log('');

    for (let n = 0; n < indirect.length; n++) {
        const item = indirect[n];

        console.log(
            `  [${n + 1}] CALL_INDIRECT at +${item.x.pos - fn.bodyOffset}`
        );
        console.log(`      ${item.x.text}`);

        console.log('      nearby instructions:');
        showAround(ins, item.i, 8);

        console.log('');
    }
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

FILE: scripts\traceKaDeepGraphV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const KA = 5539;
const IMPORTS = 83;

const MAX_DEPTH = 4;

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function readU32(pos: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readBytes(pos: { v: number }, n: number): Uint8Array {
    const out = bytes.slice(pos.v, pos.v + n);
    pos.v += n;
    return out;
}

interface Fn {
    index: number;
    defined: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    body: Uint8Array;
    calls: number[];
    indirect: boolean;
}

const functions = new Map<number, Fn>();

function decodeCallTarget(body: Uint8Array, start: number): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = start;

    while (p < body.length) {
        const b = body[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return result >>> 0;
}

function decodeCalls(body: Uint8Array): {
    calls: number[];
    indirect: boolean;
} {
    const calls: number[] = [];
    let indirect = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
        const op = body[i];

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            calls.push(decodeCallTarget(body, i + 1));
        }

        // call_indirect
        else if (op === 0x11) {
            indirect = true;
        }
    }

    return {
        calls: [...new Set(calls)],
        indirect,
    };
}

function parseCodeSection() {
    const pos = { v: 8 };

    while (pos.v < bytes.length) {
        const sectionId = bytes[pos.v++];
        const sectionSize = readU32(pos);

        const payload = pos.v;

        if (sectionId === 10) {
            const count = readU32(pos);

            for (let defined = 0; defined < count; defined++) {
                const size = readU32(pos);
                const offset = pos.v;
                const body = readBytes(pos, size);

                const index = IMPORTS + defined;

                const decoded = decodeCalls(body);

                functions.set(index, {
                    index,
                    defined,
                    offset,
                    size,
                    body,
                    calls: decoded.calls,
                    indirect: decoded.indirect,
                });
            }

            return;
        }

        pos.v = payload + sectionSize;
    }

    throw new Error('Code section not found');
}

function label(index: number): string {
    if (index < IMPORTS) {
        return `IMPORT#${index}`;
    }

    return `F#${index}`;
}

function printNode(index: number, depth: number, path: Set<number>) {
    const indent = '  '.repeat(depth);

    if (index < IMPORTS) {
        console.log(
            `${indent}→ ${label(index)}`
        );
        return;
    }

    const fn = functions.get(index);

    if (!fn) {
        console.log(
            `${indent}→ ${label(index)} [MISSING]`
        );
        return;
    }

    const repeated = path.has(index);

    console.log(
        `${indent}→ ${label(index)} ` +
        `defined=${fn.defined} ` +
        `offset=${fn.offset} ` +
        `size=${fn.size}` +
        (fn.indirect ? ' [CALL_INDIRECT]' : '')
    );

    if (repeated) {
        console.log(
            `${indent}  [cycle/revisit]`
        );
        return;
    }

    if (depth >= MAX_DEPTH) {
        console.log(
            `${indent}  [depth limit]`
        );
        return;
    }

    const nextPath = new Set(path);
    nextPath.add(index);

    for (const target of fn.calls) {
        printNode(target, depth + 1, nextPath);
    }
}

function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON/TON SANDBOX WASM - Ka DEEP CALL GRAPH v22');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE WASM');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`WASM       : ${WASM}`);
    console.log(`WASM size  : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log(`Ka         : ${KA}`);
    console.log(`Imports    : ${IMPORTS}`);
    console.log(`Max depth  : ${MAX_DEPTH}`);
    console.log('');

    parseCodeSection();

    const ka = functions.get(KA);

    if (!ka) {
        throw new Error('Ka function not found');
    }

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' KA GRAPH');
    console.log('============================================================');

    printNode(KA, 0, new Set());

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' CALL_INDIRECT FUNCTIONS REACHED BY DIRECT GRAPH');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const visited = new Set<number>();
    const indirect = new Set<number>();

    function scan(index: number, depth: number) {
        if (index < IMPORTS) {
            return;
        }

        if (visited.has(index)) {
            return;
        }

        visited.add(index);

        const fn = functions.get(index);

        if (!fn) {
            return;
        }

        if (fn.indirect) {
            indirect.add(index);
        }

        if (depth >= MAX_DEPTH) {
            return;
        }

        for (const target of fn.calls) {
            scan(target, depth + 1);
        }
    }

    scan(KA, 0);

    if (indirect.size === 0) {
        console.log('None reached within depth limit.');
    } else {
        for (const index of [...indirect].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
            const fn = functions.get(index)!;

            console.log(
                `F#${index} ` +
                `defined=${fn.defined} ` +
                `offset=${fn.offset} ` +
                `size=${fn.size}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DONE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

main();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceKaDefinedGraphV22.ts
