import fs from 'fs';

const WASM =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const KA_FUNCTION_INDEX = 5539;
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

const TARGET_DEFINED = [
    618,
    1481,
    1910,
    1075,
    170,
    83,
    95,
    106,
    114,
    259,
    327,
    2431,
    2485,
];

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function readU32(pos: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos.v++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readBytes(pos: { v: number }, length: number): Uint8Array {
    const out = bytes.slice(pos.v, pos.v + length);
    pos.v += length;
    return out;
}

function skipName(pos: { v: number }) {
    const len = readU32(pos);
    pos.v += len;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(4, '0');
}

function decodeCalls(body: Uint8Array): number[] {
    const calls: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
        const op = body[i];

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            let p = i + 1;
            let result = 0;
            let shift = 0;

            while (p < body.length) {
                const b = body[p++];
                result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                    break;
                }

                shift += 7;
            }

            calls.push(result >>> 0);
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {
            // We deliberately do not resolve indirect calls yet.
            // They are important later, but first we want direct calls.
        }
    }

    return calls;
}

interface FunctionBody {
    definedIndex: number;
    functionIndex: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    body: Uint8Array;
    calls: number[];
}

let codeBodies: FunctionBody[] = [];

function parseCodeSection(sectionStart: number, sectionSize: number) {
    const pos = { v: sectionStart };

    const count = readU32(pos);

    console.log(`Code section functions : ${count}`);
    console.log(`Code section offset    : ${sectionStart}`);
    console.log(`Code section size      : ${sectionSize}`);
    console.log('');

    for (let definedIndex = 0; definedIndex < count; definedIndex++) {
        const bodySize = readU32(pos);

        const bodyOffset = pos.v;

        const body = readBytes(pos, bodySize);

        const functionIndex = IMPORT_COUNT + definedIndex;

        codeBodies.push({
            definedIndex,
            functionIndex,
            offset: bodyOffset,
            size: bodySize,
            body,
            calls: decodeCalls(body),
        });
    }
}

function findCodeSection() {
    const pos = { v: 8 };

    while (pos.v < bytes.length) {
        const sectionId = bytes[pos.v++];
        const sectionSize = readU32(pos);
        const payloadStart = pos.v;

        if (sectionId === 10) {
            parseCodeSection(payloadStart, sectionSize);
            return;
        }

        pos.v += sectionSize;
    }

    throw new Error('Code section not found');
}

function describeTarget(target: number): string {
    if (target < IMPORT_COUNT) {
        return `IMPORT #${target}`;
    }

    return `DEFINED #${target - IMPORT_COUNT}`;
}

function getFunction(target: number): FunctionBody | undefined {
    if (target < IMPORT_COUNT) {
        return undefined;
    }

    return codeBodies[target - IMPORT_COUNT];
}

function printFunction(target: number) {
    const fn = getFunction(target);

    if (!fn) {
        console.log(
            `target=${target.toString().padStart(4)}  ${describeTarget(target)}`
        );
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `target=${target.toString().padStart(4)}  ` +
        `defined=${fn.definedIndex.toString().padStart(4)}  ` +
        `func=${fn.functionIndex.toString().padStart(4)}  ` +
        `offset=${fn.offset.toString().padStart(8)}  ` +
        `size=${fn.size.toString().padStart(5)}`
    );

    const unique = [...new Set(fn.calls)];

    if (unique.length === 0) {
        console.log('    calls: none');
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        '    calls:',
        unique
            .map(x => `${x}(${describeTarget(x)})`)
            .join(', ')
    );
}

function printHexPreview(fn: FunctionBody, max = 48) {
    const n = Math.min(max, fn.body.length);

    const h = [...fn.body.slice(0, n)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');

    console.log(`    bytes[0..${n - 1}]: ${h}`);
}

function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON/TON SANDBOX WASM - Ka DEFINED CALL GRAPH v22');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE WASM');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`WASM : ${WASM}`);
    console.log(`size : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()} bytes`);
    console.log(`Ka   : function index ${KA_FUNCTION_INDEX}`);
    console.log(`Ka   : defined #${KA_FUNCTION_INDEX - IMPORT_COUNT}`);
    console.log(`imports: ${IMPORT_COUNT}`);
    console.log('');

    findCodeSection();

    const ka = getFunction(KA_FUNCTION_INDEX);

    if (!ka) {
        throw new Error('Ka body not found');
    }

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' KA DIRECT CALLS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const kaCalls = [...new Set(ka.calls)];

    for (const target of kaCalls) {
        if (target < IMPORT_COUNT) {
            continue;
        }

        const fn = getFunction(target);

        if (!fn) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `target=${target} -> defined #${target - IMPORT_COUNT} ` +
            `(offset=${fn.offset}, size=${fn.size})`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET FUNCTIONS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    for (const target of TARGET_DEFINED) {
        printFunction(target);

        const fn = getFunction(target);

        if (fn) {
            printHexPreview(fn);
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ONE LEVEL DEEP - DEFINED ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const firstLevel = new Set<number>();

    for (const target of TARGET_DEFINED) {
        const fn = getFunction(target);

        if (!fn) {
            continue;
        }

        for (const call of fn.calls) {
            if (call >= IMPORT_COUNT) {
                firstLevel.add(call);
            }
        }
    }

    for (const target of [...firstLevel].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
        const fn = getFunction(target);

        if (!fn) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `target=${target} ` +
            `defined #${target - IMPORT_COUNT} ` +
            `offset=${fn.offset} ` +
            `size=${fn.size}`
        );

        const unique = [...new Set(fn.calls)];

        console.log(
            '    calls:',
            unique.length
                ? unique.map(x => `${x}(${describeTarget(x)})`).join(', ')
                : 'none'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DONE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

main();

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x1226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x126b4;

// Function 205:
// L2  = global0 - 0x200
// L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]
//
// Target:
// 0x126b4 call 87
//
// call 87 arguments:
//   arg0 = L2 - 0x40
//   arg1 = L13
//   arg2 = 0
//   arg3 = L29
//   arg4 = 0

const TARGET_CALL = 0x126b4;
const TARGET_L13_OFFSET = 0x1f0;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 L13 PRODUCER TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(
    `Range     : 0x${FUNC_START.toString(16)} .. 0x${TARGET_CALL.toString(16)}`
);
console.log();
console.log("TARGET:");
console.log("  L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]");
console.log("  L2  = global0 - 0x200");
console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DECODE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = FUNC_START;

function u8(): number {
    if (p >= wasm.length) {
        throw new Error(`EOF @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
    }
    return wasm[p++];
}

function readU32Leb(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = u8();
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid ULEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

function readS32Leb(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid SLEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readS33Leb(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();

        result |=
            BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35n) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid S33 LEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 33n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |=
            (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return result;
}

function readI64Leb(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();

        result |=
            BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid i64.const LEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |=
            (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return BigInt.asIntN(64, result);
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`;
}

function hexByte(v: number): string {
    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * WebAssembly blocktype is NOT an ordinary i32 opcode.
 *
 * Valid single-byte value types include:
 *
 *   0x40 = empty
 *   0x7f = i32
 *   0x7e = i64
 *   0x7d = f32
 *   0x7c = f64
 *   0x7b = v128
 *   0x70 = funcref
 *   0x6f = externref
 *
 * For other values it can be a signed type index (S33).
 */
function readBlockType(): string {
    const first = u8();

    switch (first) {
        case 0x40:
            return "empty";

        case 0x7f:
            return "i32";

        case 0x7e:
            return "i64";

        case 0x7d:
            return "f32";

        case 0x7c:
            return "f64";

        case 0x7b:
            return "v128";

        case 0x70:
            return "funcref";

        case 0x6f:
            return "externref";

        default: {
            let result =
                BigInt(first & 0x7f);

            let shift = 7n;
            let b = first;

            while (b & 0x80) {
                b = u8();

                result |=
                    BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

                shift += 7n;

                if (shift > 35n) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Invalid blocktype S33 @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
                    );
                }
            }

            if (shift < 33n && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return `typeidx(${result.toString()})`;
        }
    }
}

/*
 * Lightweight symbolic stack.
 *
 * We do not attempt to emulate the entire WASM.
 * The purpose is to preserve enough information to identify:
 *
 *     local.get 2
 *     i32.const 496
 *     i32.add
 *     i64.load
 *
 * and therefore:
 *
 *     L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]
 */

type Sym =
    | string
    | number
    | bigint
    | null;

const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

const stack: Sym[] = [];

interface ControlFrame {
    kind: string;
    start: number;
    depth: number;
    hasElse?: boolean;
}

const control: ControlFrame[] = [];

let i64StoreCount = 0;
let exactL13Count = 0;

const l13Candidates: Array<{
    offset: number;
    address: string;
}> = [];

function pop(): Sym {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return null;
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(v: Sym) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function localName(index: number): string {
    return `L${index}`;
}

function formatSym(v: Sym): string {
    if (typeof v === "string") return v;
    if (typeof v === "bigint") return v.toString();
    if (typeof v === "number") return `${v}`;
    return "?";
}

function normalizeAddress(
    a: Sym,
    b: Sym
): string | null {

    const sa = formatSym(a);
    const sb = formatSym(b);

    if (sa === "L2" && sb === "496") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    if (sa === "496" && sb === "L2") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    if (sa === "L2 + 0x1F0" && sb === "0") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    return null;
}

function consumeMemArg(): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
} {
    const align = readU32Leb();
    const offset = readU32Leb();

    return {
        align,
        offset
    };
}

function printInstruction(
    at: number,
    text: string
) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
    );
}

function unknownStop(
    at: number,
    opcode: number
): never {
    throw new Error(
        `UNKNOWN opcode ${hexByte(opcode)} @ ${hex(at)}`
    );
}

try {

    while (p < TARGET_CALL) {

        const at = p;
        const opcode = u8();

        switch (opcode) {

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONTROL FLOW
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x00:
                printInstruction(at, "unreachable");
                break;

            case 0x01:
                printInstruction(at, "nop");
                break;

            case 0x02: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `block ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "block",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `loop ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "loop",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                // condition
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `if ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "if",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length,
                    hasElse: false
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x05: {
                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    "else"
                );

                const f =
                    control[control.length - 1];

                if (f) {
                    f.hasElse = true;
                    stack.length = f.depth;
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x0b: {
                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    "end"
                );

                if (control.length > 0) {
                    const f = control.pop()!;

                    stack.length = Math.min(
                        stack.length,
                        f.depth
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x0c: {
                const depth = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br ${depth}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const depth = readU32Leb();

                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br_if ${depth}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const count = readU32Leb();

                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    readU32Leb();
                }

                readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br_table ${count}`
                );

                pop();

                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                printInstruction(at, "return");
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const fn = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `call ${fn}`
                );

                /*
                 * We intentionally do not model every call's
                 * stack signature here.
                 *
                 * Function 87 is the important helper and is
                 * handled separately when encountered.
                 */

                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const typeidx = readU32Leb();
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `call_indirect type=${typeidx} table=${tableidx}`
                );

                pop();

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // PARAMETRIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x1a:
                pop();
                printInstruction(at, "drop");
                break;

            case 0x1b: {
                const cond = pop();
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    cond === null
                        ? a
                        : a
                );

                printInstruction(at, "select");
                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // LOCALS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x20: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                const value =
                    locals.get(idx) ?? localName(idx);

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.get ${idx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();
                const value = pop();

                locals.set(idx, value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.set ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

                        case 0x22: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                const value = stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : null;

                locals.set(idx, value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.tee ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // GLOBALS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x23: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                if (idx === 0) {
                    push("global0");
                } else {
                    push(`global${idx}`);
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `global.get ${idx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();
                const value = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `global.set ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MEMORY LOADS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x28: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                const address = pop();

                let result: Sym = null;

                const sa = formatSym(address);

                if (
                    sa === "L2 + 0x1F0" &&
                    m.offset === 0
                ) {
                    result = "MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]";

                    exactL13Count++;

                    l13Candidates.push({
                        offset: at,
                        address: sa
                    });

                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "**************** EXACT L13 LOAD ****************"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `LOAD @ ${hex(at)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "Address : L2 + 0x1F0"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "Result  : L13 candidate"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "*************************************************"
                    );
                    console.log();
                }

                push(result);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${sa}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
    const m = consumeMemArg();
    const address = pop();

    const sa = formatSym(address);

    let result: Sym = null;

    // Exact L13:
    //
    //   local.get 2
    //   i64.load offset=496
    //
    // is equivalent to:
    //
    //   MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]

    if (
        sa === "L2" &&
        m.offset === 496
    ) {
        result = "MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]";

        exactL13Count++;

        l13Candidates.push({
            offset: at,
            address: "L2 + 0x1F0"
        });

        console.log();
        console.log(
            "**************** EXACT L13 LOAD ****************"
        );
        console.log(
            `LOAD @ ${hex(at)}`
        );
        console.log(
            "Address : L2 + 0x1F0"
        );
        console.log(
            "Memory  : MEM64[L2 + 496]"
        );
        console.log(
            "Result  : L13"
        );
        console.log(
            "*************************************************"
        );
        console.log();
    }

    push(result);

    printInstruction(
        at,
        `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${sa}`
    );

    break;
}

            case 0x2d: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x2e: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x2f: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x30: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x31: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x32: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x33: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x34: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x35: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load32_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MEMORY STORES
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x36: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                const value = pop();
                const address = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${formatSym(address)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                const value = pop();
                const address = pop();

                i64StoreCount++;

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${formatSym(address)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                if (
                    formatSym(address) === "L2 + 0x1F0"
                ) {
                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "************* WRITE TO L2 + 0x1F0 *************"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `STORE @ ${hex(at)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `VALUE : ${formatSym(value)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "***********************************************"
                    );
                    console.log();
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x38: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `f32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x39: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `f64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3a: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3b: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3c: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3d: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3e: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store32 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3f: {
                const memidx = readU32Leb();

                push("memory.size");

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `memory.size ${memidx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x40: {
                const memidx = readU32Leb();
                pop();

                push("memory.grow");

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `memory.grow ${memidx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONSTANTS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x41: {
                const value = readS32Leb();

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.const ${value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const value = readI64Leb();

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.const ${value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i32 NUMERIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x45:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.eqz");
                break;

            case 0x46: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.eq ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x47:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ne");
                break;

            case 0x48:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.lt_s");
                break;

            case 0x49:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.lt_u");
                break;

            case 0x4a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.gt_s");
                break;

            case 0x4b:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.gt_u");
                break;

            case 0x4c:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.le_s");
                break;

            case 0x4d:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.le_u");
                break;

            case 0x4e:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ge_s");
                break;

            case 0x4f:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ge_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i64 NUMERIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x50:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.eqz");
                break;

            case 0x51:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.eq");
                break;

            case 0x52:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ne");
                break;

            case 0x53:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.lt_s");
                break;

            case 0x54:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.lt_u");
                break;

            case 0x55:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.gt_s");
                break;

            case 0x56:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.gt_u");
                break;

            case 0x57:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.le_s");
                break;

            case 0x58:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.le_u");
                break;

            case 0x59:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ge_s");
                break;

            case 0x5a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ge_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i32 arithmetic
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x74: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                let result: Sym = null;

                // Preserve simple symbolic shifts when possible.
                if (
                    typeof b === "number" &&
                    typeof a === "string"
                ) {
                    result = `${a} << ${b}`;
                }

                push(result);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.shl ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }
            case 0x6a: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                if (a === "L2" && b === 496) {
                    push("L2 + 0x1F0");
                } else if (a === "L2" && b === 0x1f0) {
                    push("L2 + 0x1F0");
                } else {
                    push(null);
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.add ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x6b:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.sub");
                break;

            case 0x6c:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.mul");
                break;

            case 0x71:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.and");
                break;

            case 0x72:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.or");
                break;

            case 0x73:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.xor");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i64 arithmetic
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x7c: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.add ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x7d:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.sub");
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.mul");
                break;

            case 0x7f:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.div_s");
                break;

            case 0x80:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.div_u");
                break;

            case 0x81:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rem_s");
                break;

            case 0x82:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rem_u");
                break;

            case 0x83:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.and");
                break;

            case 0x84:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.or");
                break;

            case 0x85:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.xor");
                break;

            case 0x86:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shl");
                break;

            case 0x87:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shr_s");
                break;

            case 0x88:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shr_u");
                break;

            case 0x89:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rotl");
                break;

            case 0x8a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rotr");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONVERSIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xa7:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.wrap_i64");
                break;

            case 0xac:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend_i32_s");
                break;

            case 0xad:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend_i32_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MISC REFERENCE / SIGN EXTENSIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xc0:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.extend8_s");
                break;

            case 0xc1:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.extend16_s");
                break;

            case 0xc2:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend8_s");
                break;

            case 0xc3:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend16_s");
                break;

            case 0xc4:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend32_s");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // EXTENDED OPCODES
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xfc: {
                const sub = readU32Leb();

                /*
                 * Saturating conversions and bulk-memory.
                 *
                 * Consume their immediates conservatively.
                 */

                if (sub <= 7) {
                    pop();
                    push(null);

                    printInstruction(
                        at,
                        `0xfc.${sub} conversion`
                    );
                } else {
                    printInstruction(
                        at,
                        `0xfc.${sub}`
                    );

                    /*
                     * Bulk memory variants.
                     * We do not attempt symbolic tracking here.
                     */
                }

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // SIMD prefix
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xfd: {
                const sub = readU32Leb();

                /*
                 * We only need to avoid falsely interpreting
                 * SIMD immediate bytes as opcodes.
                 *
                 * Common SIMD memory forms consume memarg.
                 */

                if (
                    sub === 0 ||
                    sub === 1 ||
                    sub === 2 ||
                    sub === 3 ||
                    sub === 4 ||
                    sub === 5 ||
                    sub === 6 ||
                    sub === 7 ||
                    sub === 8 ||
                    sub === 9 ||
                    sub === 10 ||
                    sub === 11
                ) {
                    consumeMemArg();
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `simd 0x${sub.toString(16)}`
                );

                break;
            }
            // --------------------------------------------------
            // TABLE OPERATIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x25: {
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                const index = pop();

                // table.get consumes i32 index and produces a reference.
                // We do not need the reference symbolically for L13.
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `table.get ${tableidx} index=${formatSym(index)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x26: {
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                const value = pop();
                const index = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `table.set ${tableidx} index=${formatSym(index)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // UNKNOWN
            // --------------------------------------------------

            default:
                unknownStop(at, opcode);
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET CALL REACHED");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();
    console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(TARGET_CALL)}`);
    console.log("Function : 87");
    console.log();
    console.log("Arguments established from direct decode:");
    console.log("  arg0 = L2 - 0x40");
    console.log("  arg1 = L13");
    console.log("  arg2 = 0");
    console.log("  arg3 = L29");
    console.log("  arg4 = 0");
    console.log();

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("RESULT");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${i64StoreCount}`);
    console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exactL13Count}`);
    console.log();

    if (l13Candidates.length > 0) {
        console.log("L13 LOAD CANDIDATES:");

        for (const c of l13Candidates) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(c.offset)} -> ${c.address}`
            );
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            "No exact L13 load was identified before target CALL."
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Final decoder offset:");
    console.log(`  ${hex(p)}`);

} catch (err) {

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TRACE STOP");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `Offset : ${hex(p)}`
    );

    if (p < wasm.length) {
        console.log(
            `Opcode : ${hexByte(wasm[p])}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    if (err instanceof Error) {
        console.log(
            `Error  : ${err.message}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `Error  : ${String(err)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Current symbolic stack:");

    if (stack.length === 0) {
        console.log("  <empty>");
    } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
            console.log(
                `  [${i}] ${formatSym(stack[i])}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Current control depth:");
    console.log(`  ${control.length}`);

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("PARTIAL RESULT");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${i64StoreCount}`);
    console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exactL13Count}`);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceL13Producer_backup.ts
