import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_START = 0x1226d;
const FUNC_END   = 0x126b4;

// Function 205:
// L2  = global0 - 0x200
// L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]
//
// Target:
// 0x126b4 call 87
//
// call 87 arguments:
//   arg0 = L2 - 0x40
//   arg1 = L13
//   arg2 = 0
//   arg3 = L29
//   arg4 = 0

const TARGET_CALL = 0x126b4;
const TARGET_L13_OFFSET = 0x1f0;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 L13 PRODUCER TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(
    `Range     : 0x${FUNC_START.toString(16)} .. 0x${TARGET_CALL.toString(16)}`
);
console.log();
console.log("TARGET:");
console.log("  L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]");
console.log("  L2  = global0 - 0x200");
console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DECODE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = FUNC_START;

function u8(): number {
    if (p >= wasm.length) {
        throw new Error(`EOF @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
    }
    return wasm[p++];
}

function readU32Leb(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = u8();
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid ULEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

function readS32Leb(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid SLEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readS33Leb(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();

        result |=
            BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35n) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid S33 LEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 33n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |=
            (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return result;
}

function readI64Leb(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8();

        result |=
            BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid i64.const LEB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |=
            (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return BigInt.asIntN(64, result);
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`;
}

function hexByte(v: number): string {
    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * WebAssembly blocktype is NOT an ordinary i32 opcode.
 *
 * Valid single-byte value types include:
 *
 *   0x40 = empty
 *   0x7f = i32
 *   0x7e = i64
 *   0x7d = f32
 *   0x7c = f64
 *   0x7b = v128
 *   0x70 = funcref
 *   0x6f = externref
 *
 * For other values it can be a signed type index (S33).
 */
function readBlockType(): string {
    const first = u8();

    switch (first) {
        case 0x40:
            return "empty";

        case 0x7f:
            return "i32";

        case 0x7e:
            return "i64";

        case 0x7d:
            return "f32";

        case 0x7c:
            return "f64";

        case 0x7b:
            return "v128";

        case 0x70:
            return "funcref";

        case 0x6f:
            return "externref";

        default: {
            let result =
                BigInt(first & 0x7f);

            let shift = 7n;
            let b = first;

            while (b & 0x80) {
                b = u8();

                result |=
                    BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

                shift += 7n;

                if (shift > 35n) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Invalid blocktype S33 @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
                    );
                }
            }

            if (shift < 33n && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return `typeidx(${result.toString()})`;
        }
    }
}

/*
 * Lightweight symbolic stack.
 *
 * We do not attempt to emulate the entire WASM.
 * The purpose is to preserve enough information to identify:
 *
 *     local.get 2
 *     i32.const 496
 *     i32.add
 *     i64.load
 *
 * and therefore:
 *
 *     L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]
 */

type Sym =
    | string
    | number
    | bigint
    | null;

const locals = new Map<number, Sym>();

const stack: Sym[] = [];

interface ControlFrame {
    kind: string;
    start: number;
    depth: number;
    hasElse?: boolean;
}

const control: ControlFrame[] = [];

let i64StoreCount = 0;
let exactL13Count = 0;

const l13Candidates: Array<{
    offset: number;
    address: string;
}> = [];

function pop(): Sym {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return null;
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(v: Sym) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function localName(index: number): string {
    return `L${index}`;
}

function formatSym(v: Sym): string {
    if (typeof v === "string") return v;
    if (typeof v === "bigint") return v.toString();
    if (typeof v === "number") return `${v}`;
    return "?";
}

function normalizeAddress(
    a: Sym,
    b: Sym
): string | null {

    const sa = formatSym(a);
    const sb = formatSym(b);

    if (sa === "L2" && sb === "496") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    if (sa === "496" && sb === "L2") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    if (sa === "L2 + 0x1F0" && sb === "0") {
        return "L2 + 0x1F0";
    }

    return null;
}

function consumeMemArg(): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
} {
    const align = readU32Leb();
    const offset = readU32Leb();

    return {
        align,
        offset
    };
}

function printInstruction(
    at: number,
    text: string
) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
    );
}

function unknownStop(
    at: number,
    opcode: number
): never {
    throw new Error(
        `UNKNOWN opcode ${hexByte(opcode)} @ ${hex(at)}`
    );
}

try {

    while (p < TARGET_CALL) {

        const at = p;
        const opcode = u8();

        switch (opcode) {

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONTROL FLOW
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x00:
                printInstruction(at, "unreachable");
                break;

            case 0x01:
                printInstruction(at, "nop");
                break;

            case 0x02: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `block ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "block",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `loop ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "loop",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const bt = readBlockType();

                // condition
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `if ${bt}`
                );

                control.push({
                    kind: "if",
                    start: at,
                    depth: stack.length,
                    hasElse: false
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x05: {
                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    "else"
                );

                const f =
                    control[control.length - 1];

                if (f) {
                    f.hasElse = true;
                    stack.length = f.depth;
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x0b: {
                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    "end"
                );

                if (control.length > 0) {
                    const f = control.pop()!;

                    stack.length = Math.min(
                        stack.length,
                        f.depth
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x0c: {
                const depth = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br ${depth}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const depth = readU32Leb();

                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br_if ${depth}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const count = readU32Leb();

                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    readU32Leb();
                }

                readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `br_table ${count}`
                );

                pop();

                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                printInstruction(at, "return");
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const fn = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `call ${fn}`
                );

                /*
                 * We intentionally do not model every call's
                 * stack signature here.
                 *
                 * Function 87 is the important helper and is
                 * handled separately when encountered.
                 */

                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const typeidx = readU32Leb();
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `call_indirect type=${typeidx} table=${tableidx}`
                );

                pop();

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // PARAMETRIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x1a:
                pop();
                printInstruction(at, "drop");
                break;

            case 0x1b: {
                const cond = pop();
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    cond === null
                        ? a
                        : a
                );

                printInstruction(at, "select");
                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // LOCALS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x20: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                const value =
                    locals.get(idx) ?? localName(idx);

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.get ${idx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();
                const value = pop();

                locals.set(idx, value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.set ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

                        case 0x22: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                const value = stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : null;

                locals.set(idx, value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `local.tee ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // GLOBALS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x23: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();

                if (idx === 0) {
                    push("global0");
                } else {
                    push(`global${idx}`);
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `global.get ${idx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const idx = readU32Leb();
                const value = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `global.set ${idx} = ${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MEMORY LOADS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x28: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                const address = pop();

                let result: Sym = null;

                const sa = formatSym(address);

                if (
                    sa === "L2 + 0x1F0" &&
                    m.offset === 0
                ) {
                    result = "MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]";

                    exactL13Count++;

                    l13Candidates.push({
                        offset: at,
                        address: sa
                    });

                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "**************** EXACT L13 LOAD ****************"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `LOAD @ ${hex(at)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "Address : L2 + 0x1F0"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "Result  : L13 candidate"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "*************************************************"
                    );
                    console.log();
                }

                push(result);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${sa}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
    const m = consumeMemArg();
    const address = pop();

    const sa = formatSym(address);

    let result: Sym = null;

    // Exact L13:
    //
    //   local.get 2
    //   i64.load offset=496
    //
    // is equivalent to:
    //
    //   MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]

    if (
        sa === "L2" &&
        m.offset === 496
    ) {
        result = "MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]";

        exactL13Count++;

        l13Candidates.push({
            offset: at,
            address: "L2 + 0x1F0"
        });

        console.log();
        console.log(
            "**************** EXACT L13 LOAD ****************"
        );
        console.log(
            `LOAD @ ${hex(at)}`
        );
        console.log(
            "Address : L2 + 0x1F0"
        );
        console.log(
            "Memory  : MEM64[L2 + 496]"
        );
        console.log(
            "Result  : L13"
        );
        console.log(
            "*************************************************"
        );
        console.log();
    }

    push(result);

    printInstruction(
        at,
        `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${sa}`
    );

    break;
}

            case 0x2d: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x2e: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x2f: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x30: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x31: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x32: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x33: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load16_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x34: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load16_s align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x35: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();
                pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.load32_u align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MEMORY STORES
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x36: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                const value = pop();
                const address = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${formatSym(address)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                const value = pop();
                const address = pop();

                i64StoreCount++;

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset} address=${formatSym(address)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                if (
                    formatSym(address) === "L2 + 0x1F0"
                ) {
                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "************* WRITE TO L2 + 0x1F0 *************"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `STORE @ ${hex(at)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `VALUE : ${formatSym(value)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "***********************************************"
                    );
                    console.log();
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x38: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `f32.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x39: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `f64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3a: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3b: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3c: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3d: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store16 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3e: {
                const m = consumeMemArg();

                pop();
                pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.store32 align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x3f: {
                const memidx = readU32Leb();

                push("memory.size");

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `memory.size ${memidx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x40: {
                const memidx = readU32Leb();
                pop();

                push("memory.grow");

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `memory.grow ${memidx}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONSTANTS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x41: {
                const value = readS32Leb();

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.const ${value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const value = readI64Leb();

                push(value);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.const ${value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i32 NUMERIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x45:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.eqz");
                break;

            case 0x46: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.eq ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x47:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ne");
                break;

            case 0x48:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.lt_s");
                break;

            case 0x49:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.lt_u");
                break;

            case 0x4a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.gt_s");
                break;

            case 0x4b:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.gt_u");
                break;

            case 0x4c:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.le_s");
                break;

            case 0x4d:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.le_u");
                break;

            case 0x4e:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ge_s");
                break;

            case 0x4f:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.ge_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i64 NUMERIC
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x50:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.eqz");
                break;

            case 0x51:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.eq");
                break;

            case 0x52:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ne");
                break;

            case 0x53:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.lt_s");
                break;

            case 0x54:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.lt_u");
                break;

            case 0x55:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.gt_s");
                break;

            case 0x56:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.gt_u");
                break;

            case 0x57:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.le_s");
                break;

            case 0x58:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.le_u");
                break;

            case 0x59:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ge_s");
                break;

            case 0x5a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.ge_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i32 arithmetic
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x74: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                let result: Sym = null;

                // Preserve simple symbolic shifts when possible.
                if (
                    typeof b === "number" &&
                    typeof a === "string"
                ) {
                    result = `${a} << ${b}`;
                }

                push(result);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.shl ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }
            case 0x6a: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                if (a === "L2" && b === 496) {
                    push("L2 + 0x1F0");
                } else if (a === "L2" && b === 0x1f0) {
                    push("L2 + 0x1F0");
                } else {
                    push(null);
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i32.add ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x6b:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.sub");
                break;

            case 0x6c:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.mul");
                break;

            case 0x71:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.and");
                break;

            case 0x72:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.or");
                break;

            case 0x73:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.xor");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // i64 arithmetic
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x7c: {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `i64.add ${formatSym(a)} ${formatSym(b)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x7d:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.sub");
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.mul");
                break;

            case 0x7f:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.div_s");
                break;

            case 0x80:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.div_u");
                break;

            case 0x81:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rem_s");
                break;

            case 0x82:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rem_u");
                break;

            case 0x83:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.and");
                break;

            case 0x84:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.or");
                break;

            case 0x85:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.xor");
                break;

            case 0x86:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shl");
                break;

            case 0x87:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shr_s");
                break;

            case 0x88:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.shr_u");
                break;

            case 0x89:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rotl");
                break;

            case 0x8a:
                pop();
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.rotr");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // CONVERSIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xa7:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.wrap_i64");
                break;

            case 0xac:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend_i32_s");
                break;

            case 0xad:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend_i32_u");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // MISC REFERENCE / SIGN EXTENSIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xc0:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.extend8_s");
                break;

            case 0xc1:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i32.extend16_s");
                break;

            case 0xc2:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend8_s");
                break;

            case 0xc3:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend16_s");
                break;

            case 0xc4:
                pop();
                push(null);
                printInstruction(at, "i64.extend32_s");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // EXTENDED OPCODES
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xfc: {
                const sub = readU32Leb();

                /*
                 * Saturating conversions and bulk-memory.
                 *
                 * Consume their immediates conservatively.
                 */

                if (sub <= 7) {
                    pop();
                    push(null);

                    printInstruction(
                        at,
                        `0xfc.${sub} conversion`
                    );
                } else {
                    printInstruction(
                        at,
                        `0xfc.${sub}`
                    );

                    /*
                     * Bulk memory variants.
                     * We do not attempt symbolic tracking here.
                     */
                }

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // SIMD prefix
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0xfd: {
                const sub = readU32Leb();

                /*
                 * We only need to avoid falsely interpreting
                 * SIMD immediate bytes as opcodes.
                 *
                 * Common SIMD memory forms consume memarg.
                 */

                if (
                    sub === 0 ||
                    sub === 1 ||
                    sub === 2 ||
                    sub === 3 ||
                    sub === 4 ||
                    sub === 5 ||
                    sub === 6 ||
                    sub === 7 ||
                    sub === 8 ||
                    sub === 9 ||
                    sub === 10 ||
                    sub === 11
                ) {
                    consumeMemArg();
                }

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `simd 0x${sub.toString(16)}`
                );

                break;
            }
            // --------------------------------------------------
            // TABLE OPERATIONS
            // --------------------------------------------------

            case 0x25: {
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                const index = pop();

                // table.get consumes i32 index and produces a reference.
                // We do not need the reference symbolically for L13.
                push(null);

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `table.get ${tableidx} index=${formatSym(index)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x26: {
                const tableidx = readU32Leb();

                const value = pop();
                const index = pop();

                printInstruction(
                    at,
                    `table.set ${tableidx} index=${formatSym(index)} value=${formatSym(value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // UNKNOWN
            // --------------------------------------------------

            default:
                unknownStop(at, opcode);
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET CALL REACHED");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();
    console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(TARGET_CALL)}`);
    console.log("Function : 87");
    console.log();
    console.log("Arguments established from direct decode:");
    console.log("  arg0 = L2 - 0x40");
    console.log("  arg1 = L13");
    console.log("  arg2 = 0");
    console.log("  arg3 = L29");
    console.log("  arg4 = 0");
    console.log();

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("RESULT");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${i64StoreCount}`);
    console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exactL13Count}`);
    console.log();

    if (l13Candidates.length > 0) {
        console.log("L13 LOAD CANDIDATES:");

        for (const c of l13Candidates) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(c.offset)} -> ${c.address}`
            );
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            "No exact L13 load was identified before target CALL."
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Final decoder offset:");
    console.log(`  ${hex(p)}`);

} catch (err) {

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TRACE STOP");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `Offset : ${hex(p)}`
    );

    if (p < wasm.length) {
        console.log(
            `Opcode : ${hexByte(wasm[p])}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    if (err instanceof Error) {
        console.log(
            `Error  : ${err.message}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `Error  : ${String(err)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Current symbolic stack:");

    if (stack.length === 0) {
        console.log("  <empty>");
    } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
            console.log(
                `  [${i}] ${formatSym(stack[i])}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Current control depth:");
    console.log(`  ${control.length}`);

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("PARTIAL RESULT");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${i64StoreCount}`);
    console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exactL13Count}`);
}
// ============================================================
// CALLER SEARCH: CALL 205
// READ ONLY - OFFLINE
// ============================================================
//
// WASM encoding:
//
//   call 205
//   opcode  = 0x10
//   function index 205 = ULEB128 cd 01
//
// Therefore raw byte pattern:
//
//   10 cd 01
//
// This is only a candidate scan.
// Final verification requires function/code-section boundaries.
//

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALLER SEARCH");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log("TARGET CALL:");
console.log("  opcode : 0x10");
console.log("  index  : 205");
console.log("  ULEB   : cd 01");
console.log("  bytes  : 10 cd 01");
console.log();

let call205Count = 0;

for (let i = 0; i + 2 < wasm.length; i++) {

    if (
        wasm[i] === 0x10 &&
        wasm[i + 1] === 0xcd &&
        wasm[i + 2] === 0x01
    ) {

        call205Count++;

        console.log(
            `CALL 205 candidate #${call205Count} @ ${hex(i)}`
        );

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 24);
        const end = Math.min(wasm.length, i + 32);

        console.log(
            `  context : ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`
        );

        for (let j = start; j < end; j += 16) {

            const rowEnd = Math.min(end, j + 16);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let k = j; k < rowEnd; k++) {
                bytes.push(
                    wasm[k]
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  ${hex(j)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(
    `CALL 205 raw candidates : ${call205Count}`
);

if (call205Count === 0) {
    console.log("No raw CALL 205 candidate found.");
} else {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        "IMPORTANT: candidates are raw-byte matches."
    );
    console.log(
        "Next step: verify each candidate against WASM function boundaries."
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END CALLER SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

// ============================================================
// STRUCTURAL CALLER SEARCH: REAL CALL 205
// READ ONLY - OFFLINE
// ============================================================
//
// Goal:
//   Verify raw "10 cd 01" candidates against actual
//   WASM Code Section function boundaries.
//
// Target:
//   real function index = 205
//
// This does NOT execute the WASM.
// This does NOT modify the WASM.
// ============================================================

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 STRUCTURAL CALLER SEARCH");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

function readULEBAt(buf: Uint8Array, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Truncated ULEB @ ${hex(pos)}`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse WASM sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p205 = 8; // WASM header

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p205 < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p205++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEBAt(wasm, p205);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p205 = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p205;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = payloadStart;
        codeSectionEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p205 = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Code Section (10) not found.");
}

console.log();
console.log(`Code section : ${hex(codeSectionStart)} .. ${hex(codeSectionEnd)}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
//
// payload:
//   function_count
//   body_size
//   body_bytes
//   ...
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q205 = codeSectionStart;

const countInfo205 = readULEBAt(wasm, q205);
const functionBodyCount = countInfo205.value;
q205 = countInfo205.next;

console.log(`Function bodies : ${functionBodyCount}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Defined function index starts after imports.
//
// Earlier established:
//   import count = 83
//
// Therefore:
//   body #0 = real function 83
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const IMPORT_COUNT_205 = 83;

interface FuncBody205 {
    bodyIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    bodySize: number;
}

const functions205: FuncBody205[] = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionBodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodySizeInfo = readULEBAt(wasm, q205);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;

    const bodySizeLebEnd = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = q205;
    const codeStart = bodySizeLebEnd;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > codeSectionEnd) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function body exceeds Code Section: body #${bodyIndex}`
        );
    }

    functions205.push({
        bodyIndex,
        realIndex: IMPORT_COUNT_205 + bodyIndex,
        bodyStart,
        codeStart,
        bodyEnd,
        bodySize
    });

    q205 = bodyEnd;
}

console.log(
    `Parsed bodies : ${functions205.length}`
);

if (q205 !== codeSectionEnd) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        `WARNING: final parser offset ${hex(q205)} != Code end ${hex(codeSectionEnd)}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search only actual function code bytes
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const TARGET205 = [0x10, 0xcd, 0x01];

let verifiedCount205 = 0;

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" VERIFIED CALL 205");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const fn of functions205) {

    // We only need code bytes.
    for (
        let i = fn.codeStart;
        i + 2 < fn.bodyEnd;
        i++
    ) {

        if (
            wasm[i] === TARGET205[0] &&
            wasm[i + 1] === TARGET205[1] &&
            wasm[i + 2] === TARGET205[2]
        ) {

            verifiedCount205++;

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `CALL 205 #${verifiedCount205}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  caller real index : ${fn.realIndex}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  body index        : ${fn.bodyIndex}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  body              : ${hex(fn.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(fn.bodyEnd)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  code              : ${hex(fn.codeStart)} .. ${hex(fn.bodyEnd)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  CALL offset       : ${hex(i)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  relative          : +${i - fn.codeStart}`
            );

            const start = Math.max(fn.codeStart, i - 24);
            const end = Math.min(fn.bodyEnd, i + 32);

            console.log(
                `  context           : ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`
            );

            for (let j = start; j < end; j += 16) {

                const rowEnd = Math.min(end, j + 16);

                const bytes: string[] = [];

                for (let k = j; k < rowEnd; k++) {
                    bytes.push(
                        wasm[k]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(j)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(
    `Verified CALL 205 count : ${verifiedCount205}`
);

console.log();

if (verifiedCount205 === 0) {

    console.log(
        "No structurally verified CALL 205 found."
    );

} else {

    console.log(
        "All above matches are inside real Function bodies."
    );

    console.log(
        "Next step: identify which caller reaches Function 205"
    );

    console.log(
        "and track its frame/global setup against L2 + 0x1F0."
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END STRUCTURAL CALLER SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

                ? "MATCH"
                : "MISMATCH"
        }`

    console.log(

            fn205check.bodyStart === EXPECTED_BODY_START205
                ? "MATCH"
                : "MISMATCH"
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `  code start : ${
            fn205check.codeStart === EXPECTED_CODE_START205
                ? "MATCH"

        }`
    );

    console.log(

            fn205check.bodyEnd === EXPECTED_BODY_END205
                ? "MATCH"
                : "MISMATCH"
        }`
    );
}

FILE: scripts\traceL13ProducerV16.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const TARGET = 0x126b4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 L13 PRODUCER TRACE v16");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log();
console.log("TARGET:");
console.log("  L13 = MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0]");
console.log("  L2  = global0 - 0x200");
console.log();

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF in ULEB");
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
        }

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid SLEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF in SLEB");
}

function readS64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        if (shift > 70n) throw new Error("Invalid i64.const");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF in i64.const");
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
    next: number;
} {
    const a = readU32Leb(pos);
    const o = readU32Leb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function localName(i: number): string {
    return `L${i}`;
}

type Item =
    | { kind: "local"; n: number }
    | { kind: "const"; n: bigint }
    | { kind: "addr"; text: string }
    | { kind: "unknown"; text: string };

const stack: Item[] = [];

function pop(): Item {
    return stack.pop() ?? { kind: "unknown", text: "STACK_UNDERFLOW" };
}

function push(x: Item) {
    stack.push(x);
}

function describe(x: Item): string {
    return x.text ??
        ("n" in x ? String(x.n) : "?");
}

function addExpr(a: Item, b: Item): Item {
    const sa = describe(a);
    const sb = describe(b);

    if (a.kind === "local" && a.n === 2 &&
        b.kind === "const" && b.n === 496n) {
        return {
            kind: "addr",
            text: "L2 + 0x1F0"
        };
    }

    if (b.kind === "local" && b.n === 2 &&
        a.kind === "const" && a.n === 496n) {
        return {
            kind: "addr",
            text: "L2 + 0x1F0"
        };
    }

    return {
        kind: "addr",
        text: `(${sa} + ${sb})`
    };
}

function fmtItem(x: Item): string {
    return describe(x);
}

let p = START;

let stores = 0;
let exact = 0;

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SEQUENTIAL DECODE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

while (p < TARGET) {
    const at = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    try {
        switch (op) {

            case 0x0b: // end
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  end`);
                break;

            case 0x20: { // local.get
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                push({ kind: "local", n: r.value });
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  local.get ${localName(r.value)}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: { // local.set
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                const v = pop();
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  local.set ${localName(r.value)} <- ${fmtItem(v)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: { // local.tee
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                const v = pop();
                push(v);
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${localName(r.value)} = ${fmtItem(v)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: { // global.get
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `G${r.value}`
                });
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  global.get ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: { // global.set
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                const v = pop();
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  global.set ${r.value} <- ${fmtItem(v)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: { // i32.const
                const r = readS32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                push({
                    kind: "const",
                    n: BigInt(r.value)
                });
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: { // i64.const
                const r = readS64Leb(p);
                p = r.next;
                push({
                    kind: "const",
                    n: r.value
                });
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x6a: { // i32.add
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "addr",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} + ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.add    ${fmtItem(a)} + ${fmtItem(b)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x6b: { // i32.sub
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "addr",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} - ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.sub    ${fmtItem(a)} - ${fmtItem(b)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x7c: { // i64.add
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} + ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.add    ${fmtItem(a)} + ${fmtItem(b)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x7d: { // i64.sub
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} - ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.sub    ${fmtItem(a)} - ${fmtItem(b)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: { // i64.load
                const m = readMemArg(p);
                p = m.next;

                const addr = pop();

                const text =
                    `MEM64[${fmtItem(addr)} + ${m.offset}]`;

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.load offset=${m.offset}  <- ${fmtItem(addr)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x37: { // i64.store
                const m = readMemArg(p);
                p = m.next;

                const value = pop();
                const addr = pop();

                stores++;

                const addressText = fmtItem(addr);

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    `>>> REAL i64.store #${stores} @ ${hex(at)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `    address = ${addressText} + ${m.offset}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `    value   = ${fmtItem(value)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `    align   = ${m.align}`
                );

                if (
                    addressText === "L2 + 0x1F0" &&
                    m.offset === 0
                ) {
                    exact++;

                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "!!! EXACT L13 PRODUCER FOUND !!!"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "!!! MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0] WRITE !!!"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `store @ ${hex(at)}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `value = ${fmtItem(value)}`
                    );
                    console.log();
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x10: { // call
                const r = readU32Leb(p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  call ${r.value}`);

                // Calls can radically alter the symbolic stack.
                // Keep only items that existed below the current
                // call arguments conservatively impossible to know.
                break;
            }

            case 0x6c: { // i32.mul
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} * ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.mul`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x7e: { // i64.mul
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} * ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.mul`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x7f: { // i64.div_s
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} / ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.div_s`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x84: { // i64.extend_i32_u
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `u32(${fmtItem(a)})`
                });

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.extend_i32_u`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x85: { // i64.extend_i32_s
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `s32(${fmtItem(a)})`
                });

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.extend_i32_s`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x86: { // i64.extend32_s
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `extend32(${fmtItem(a)})`
                });

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.extend32_s`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x56: { // i64.shr_u
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push({
                    kind: "unknown",
                    text: `(${fmtItem(a)} >>> ${fmtItem(b)})`
                });

                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.shr_u`);
                break;
            }

            case 0xad: { // i64.extend_i32_s (prefix/extended decoding may vary)
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  op=0xad`
                );
                break;
            }

            default:
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                );

                // Stop deliberately: we don't want to cross an
                // unknown instruction and corrupt the symbolic stack.
                throw new Error(
                    `Decoder stopped at ${hex(at)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
                );
        }
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TRACE STOP");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Offset : ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`Reason : ${e.message}`);
        console.log();
        console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${stores}`);
        console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exact}`);
        console.log();
        break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`REAL i64.store count : ${stores}`);
console.log(`EXACT L13 producers  : ${exact}`);
console.log();

if (exact === 0) {
    console.log("No exact MEM64[L2 + 0x1F0] store decoded before CALL #26.");
    console.log("Next step: trace the memory value through the frame initialization");
    console.log("or through a call that populates L2+0x1F0.");
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

const TARGET_OPCODES = [
    {
        name: 'ADD_LIQUIDITY',
        hex: '50c6a654',
    },
    {
        name: 'PROVIDE_LP',
        hex: '37c096df',
    },
    {
        name: 'LP_TRANSFER',
        hex: '178d4519',
    },
    {
        name: 'PTON_TRANSFER',
        hex: '01f3835d',
    },
    {
        name: 'ROOSTER_PATH',
        hex: '0f8a7ea5',
    },
];

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function bitsToHex(cell: Cell): string {
    const bits = cell.bits;
    const bytes = Math.ceil(bits.length / 8);

    if (bytes === 0) {
        return '';
    }

    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(bytes);

    for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) {
        if (bits.at(i)) {
            buffer[Math.floor(i / 8)] |=
                1 << (7 - (i % 8));
        }
    }

    return buffer.toString('hex').toUpperCase();
}

function first32BitsHex(cell: Cell): string {
    if (cell.bits.length < 32) {
        return bitsToHex(cell).slice(0, 8);
    }

    let value = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
        value += cell.bits.at(i) ? '1' : '0';
    }

    let hex = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i += 4) {
        hex += parseInt(value.slice(i, i + 4), 2)
            .toString(16);
    }

    return hex.toUpperCase();
}

function containsOpcode(cell: Cell, opcodeHex: string): boolean {
    const hex = bitsToHex(cell).toLowerCase();
    return hex.includes(opcodeHex.toLowerCase());
}

function scanCell(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    visited: Set<string>,
    results: string[]
) {
    const hash = cell.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase();

    if (visited.has(hash)) {
        return;
    }

    visited.add(hash);

    const bitsHex = bitsToHex(cell);
    const first32 = first32BitsHex(cell);

    console.log();
    console.log(`Cell ${path}`);
    console.log(`Hash:      ${hash}`);
    console.log(`Bits:      ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`Refs:      ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`First32:   ${first32}`);
    console.log(`Bits hex:  ${bitsHex.slice(0, 160)}`);

    if (hash === TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX) {
        const msg =
            `TARGET CELL FOUND at ${path} -> ${hash}`;

        console.log();
        console.log('!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!');
        console.log(msg);
        console.log('!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!');

        results.push(msg);
    }

    for (const opcode of TARGET_OPCODES) {
        if (containsOpcode(cell, opcode.hex)) {
            const msg =
                `OPCODE ${opcode.name} (${opcode.hex}) FOUND at ${path} in cell ${hash}`;

            console.log();
            console.log('*** OPCODE MATCH ***');
            console.log(msg);

            results.push(msg);
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        scanCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref${i}`,
            visited,
            results
        );
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIBRARY OPCODE TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`LiteServer: ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
            LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
            'hex'
        );

        console.log('Requesting Library...');
        console.log(`Hash: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);
        console.log();

        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            libraryHash,
        ]);

        console.log(
            `Library entries returned: ${result.result.length}`
        );

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'Library was not returned by LiteServer'
            );
        }

        const entry = result.result.find(
            item =>
                Buffer.from(item.hash)
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase() === LIBRARY_HASH_HEX
        );

        if (!entry) {
            throw new Error(
                'Requested Library hash was not found in result'
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('Library entry found.');
        console.log(
            `Entry hash: ${Buffer.from(entry.hash)
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );
        console.log(`BOC bytes: ${entry.data.length}`);

        const root = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(entry.data)
        )[0];

        const calculatedHash =
            root.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase();

        console.log(
            `Root cell hash: ${calculatedHash}`
        );

        if (calculatedHash !== LIBRARY_HASH_HEX) {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                'WARNING: Root hash does not match requested hash.'
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                'Root hash verification: OK'
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' RECURSIVE CELL TRACE');
        console.log('========================================================================');

        const visited = new Set<string>();
        const results: string[] = [];

        scanCell(
            root,
            'root',
            visited,
            results
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' SUMMARY');
        console.log('========================================================================');

        console.log(
            `Unique cells scanned: ${visited.size}`
        );

        if (results.length === 0) {
            console.log(
                'No target opcode or target cell hash found.'
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                `Matches found: ${results.length}`
            );

            for (const result of results) {
                console.log(`- ${result}`);
            }
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}
