const sleep = (ms: number) =>
    new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

function shortAddress(value: unknown): string {
    const text = String(value ?? 'unknown');

    if (text.length <= 28) {
        return text;
    }

    return `${text.slice(0, 12)}...${text.slice(-10)}`;
}

function getExitCode(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.description?.compute_ph?.exit_code ??
        tx?.description?.compute_phase?.exit_code ??
        tx?.description?.compute?.exit_code ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getComputeSuccess(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.description?.compute_ph?.success ??
        tx?.description?.compute_phase?.success ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getActionSuccess(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.description?.action?.success ??
        tx?.description?.action_phase?.success ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getActionResult(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.description?.action?.result_code ??
        tx?.description?.action_phase?.result_code ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getHash(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.transaction_id?.hash ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getLt(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx?.transaction_id?.lt ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getTime(tx: any): string {

    const utime =
        Number(tx?.utime);

    if (!Number.isFinite(utime)) {
        return 'unknown';
    }

    return new Date(
        utime * 1000
    ).toISOString();
}

function getFee(tx: any): string {

    const fee =
        tx?.fee ??
        tx?.total_fees?.coins;

    if (
        fee === undefined ||
        fee === null
    ) {
        return 'unknown';
    }

    return String(fee);
}

function getMessages(tx: any): any[] {

    if (
        Array.isArray(tx?.out_msgs)
    ) {
        return tx.out_msgs;
    }

    return [];
}

function getDestination(msg: any): string {

    return String(
        msg?.destination ??
        msg?.dst ??
        msg?.destination_address ??
        msg?.address ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getValue(msg: any): string {

    const value =
        msg?.value?.coins ??
        msg?.value ??
        msg?.amount;

    if (
        value === undefined ||
        value === null
    ) {
        return 'unknown';
    }

    return String(value);
}

function printTransaction(
    tx: any,
    index: number
) {

    const messages =
        getMessages(tx);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `---------------- TRANSACTION ${index} ----------------`
    );

    console.log(
        `Hash            : ${getHash(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `LT              : ${getLt(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Time            : ${getTime(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Fee             : ${getFee(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Compute success : ${getComputeSuccess(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Compute exit    : ${getExitCode(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Action success  : ${getActionSuccess(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Action result   : ${getActionResult(tx)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Outgoing msgs   : ${messages.length}`
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < messages.length;
        i++
    ) {

        const msg =
            messages[i];

        console.log(
            `  Message ${i + 1}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    Destination: ${shortAddress(
                getDestination(msg)
            )}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    Value      : ${getValue(msg)}`
        );

        if (
            msg?.message_content?.body
        ) {

            console.log(
                '    Body       : present'
            );
        }
    }
}

async function getTransactions(
    address: string
): Promise<any[]> {

    const url =
        `${API_BASE}/getTransactions` +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(address)}` +
        `&limit=5`;

    const response =
        await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {

        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const json =
        await response.json();

    if (!json?.ok) {

        throw new Error(
            json?.error ??
            'TON Center returned ok=false'
        );
    }

    if (
        !Array.isArray(json?.result)
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'Unexpected TON Center response'
        );
    }

    return json.result;
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const API = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const USER =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_HASHES = [
    'yahs7oxwg9624eIgSCqu/yvho9ZlHOMjaEySpupB6do=',
    '/pyWeeTVHT5CJqLG0I4u4OJF0L3Qv2ro6mnPwNsDZ5U=',
    'mPHUbHn8N68UPhyQAtu5P8nabFFfOpqRszhb3oFMLR4=',
    'Ia2y2HJbueUFlilQdEqzvjVblc3Sd1gtpulayzDfk5M=',
    '3kdI6EWpP9m1d7JKO808HpzEADbzgSX37/3ufQGKyI8=',
];

function printObject(label: string, value: unknown) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`----- ${label} -----`);

    try {
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
        );
    } catch {
        console.log(String(value));
    }
}

async function getJson(path: string): Promise<any> {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    return await response.json();
}

async function getTransactions(
    address: string
): Promise<any[]> {
    const data = await getJson(
        `/transactions?account=${encodeURIComponent(address)}&limit=50&offset=0&sort=desc`
    );

    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        return data;
    }

    return data.transactions ?? [];
}

function hashOf(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx.hash ??
        tx.transaction_hash ??
        tx.tx_hash ??
        ''
    );
}

function addressOf(msg: any): string {
    return String(
        msg.destination ??
        msg.dst ??
        msg.destination_address ??
        msg.source ??
        msg.src ??
        msg.source_address ??
        ''
    );
}

function valueOf(msg: any): string {
    return String(
        msg.value ??
        msg.value_nanoton ??
        'unknown'
    );
}

function getAllMessages(tx: any): any[] {
    const result: any[] = [];

    if (tx.in_msg) {
        result.push({
            direction: 'IN',
            message: tx.in_msg,
        });
    }

    if (Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)) {
        for (const msg of tx.out_msgs) {
            result.push({
                direction: 'OUT',
                message: msg,
            });
        }
    }

    if (Array.isArray(tx.messages)) {
        for (const msg of tx.messages) {
            result.push({
                direction: 'MESSAGE',
                message: msg,
            });
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function bodyOf(msg: any): unknown {
    return (
        msg.message_content ??
        msg.msg_data ??
        msg.body ??
        msg.message?.body ??
        null
    );
}

function decodeBody(body: unknown): {
    type: string;
    raw: string;
} {
    if (body === null || body === undefined) {
        return {
            type: 'NO_BODY',
            raw: '',
        };
    }

    if (typeof body === 'string') {
        return {
            type: 'STRING',
            raw: body,
        };
    }

    try {
        return {
            type: typeof body,
            raw: JSON.stringify(body),
        };
    } catch {
        return {
            type: typeof body,
            raw: String(body),
        };
    }
}

async function inspectTransaction(
    tx: any,
    accountName: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(`ACCOUNT: ${accountName}`);
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log(`HASH: ${hashOf(tx)}`);
    console.log(`LT: ${tx.lt ?? 'unknown'}`);
    console.log(
        `NOW: ${tx.now ?? tx.utime ?? 'unknown'}`
    );

    printObject(
        'TRANSACTION KEYS',
        Object.keys(tx)
    );

    printObject(
        'DESCRIPTION',
        tx.description ??
        tx.transaction_description ??
        null
    );

    const messages = getAllMessages(tx);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TOTAL MESSAGE OBJECTS: ${messages.length}`
    );

    let counter = 0;

    for (const item of messages) {
        counter++;

        const msg = item.message;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `******** MESSAGE ${counter} (${item.direction}) ********`
        );

        console.log(
            `Source: ${
                msg.source ??
                msg.src ??
                msg.source_address ??
                'unknown'
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `Destination: ${
                msg.destination ??
                msg.dst ??
                msg.destination_address ??
                'unknown'
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `Value: ${valueOf(msg)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Message type: ${
                msg.msg_type ??
                msg.message_type ??
                'unknown'
            }`
        );

        const decoded = decodeBody(
            bodyOf(msg)
        );

        console.log(
            `Body type: ${decoded.type}`
        );

        if (decoded.raw) {
            console.log(
                `Body: ${decoded.raw}`
            );
        }

        const destination = addressOf(msg);

        if (
            destination.includes(
                POOL.slice(0, 20)
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                '*** THIS MESSAGE TARGETS THE POOL ***'
            );
        }

        if (
            destination.includes(
                ROUTER.slice(0, 20)
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                '*** THIS MESSAGE TARGETS THE STON ROUTER ***'
            );
        }

        if (
            destination.includes(
                PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.slice(0, 20)
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                '*** THIS MESSAGE TARGETS THE PTON ROUTER WALLET ***'
            );
        }

        if (
            destination.includes(
                ROOSTER_WALLET.slice(0, 20)
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                '*** THIS MESSAGE TARGETS THE ROOSTER WALLET ***'
            );
        }
    }
}

async function inspectAccount(
    name: string,
    address: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `\n################ ${name} ################`
    );
    console.log(`Address: ${address}`);

    try {
        const transactions =
            await getTransactions(address);

        console.log(
            `Transactions downloaded: ${transactions.length}`
        );

        for (const tx of transactions) {
            if (
                TARGET_HASHES.includes(
                    hashOf(tx)
                )
            ) {
                await inspectTransaction(
                    tx,
                    name
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            `ERROR: ${
                error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : String(error)
            }`
        );
    }
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '       ROOSTER DEEP LIQUIDITY TRACE'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TARGET POOL: ${POOL}`
    );

    await inspectAccount(
        'USER',
        USER
    );

    await inspectAccount(
        'ROOSTER WALLET',
        ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    await inspectAccount(
        'PTON ROUTER WALLET',
        PTON_ROUTER_WALLET
    );

    await inspectAccount(
        'STON ROUTER',
        ROUTER
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const SOURCE_TIME = 1790714445;

const ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

function opcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'NO_32BIT_OPCODE';
        }

        return (
            '0x' +
            s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    } catch {
        return 'UNREADABLE';
    }
}

function outputs(tx: any): any[] {
    const result: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < tx.outMessagesCount; i++) {
        result.push(tx.outMessages.get(i));
    }

    return result;
}

function printMessage(
    message: any,
    index: number
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`MESSAGE ${index}`);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    if (!message) {
        console.log('NULL');
        return;
    }

    console.log('TYPE:', message.info.type);

    if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
        console.log(
            'FROM:',
            message.info.src.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'TO:',
            message.info.dest.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'VALUE:',
            message.info.value.coins.toString(),
            'nanoTON'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        opcode(message.body)
    );

    try {
        console.log(
            'BODY BOC:',
            message.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('BODY BOC: UNAVAILABLE');
    }
}

async function inspect(
    client: TonClient4,
    label: string,
    address: Address
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('ADDRESS:', address.toString());

    const last = await client.getLastBlock();

    const account = await client.getAccount(
        last.last.seqno,
        address
    );

    if (!account.account.last) {
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION HISTORY');
        return;
    }

    const latestLt = BigInt(account.account.last.lt);

    const latestHash = Buffer.from(
        account.account.last.hash,
        'base64'
    );

    const txs = await client.getAccountTransactions(
        address,
        latestLt,
        latestHash
    );

    let count = 0;

    for (const item of txs) {
        const tx: any = item.tx;

        if (Number(tx.now) < SOURCE_TIME) {
            continue;
        }

        count++;

        console.log('');
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
        console.log('TRANSACTION');
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        console.log(
            'LT:',
            tx.lt.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'HASH:',
            tx.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log(
            'TIME:',
            tx.now
        );

        console.log(
            'BLOCK SEQNO:',
            item.block.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'TOTAL FEES:',
            tx.totalFees.coins.toString(),
            'nanoTON'
        );

        console.log(
            'OUT MESSAGES:',
            tx.outMessagesCount
        );

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');

            console.log(
                'TYPE:',
                tx.inMessage.info.type
            );

            if (
                tx.inMessage.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'FROM:',
                    tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'TO:',
                    tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'VALUE:',
                    tx.inMessage.info.value.coins.toString(),
                    'nanoTON'
                );
            }

            console.log(
                'OPCODE:',
                opcode(tx.inMessage.body)
            );

            try {
                console.log(
                    'BODY BOC:',
                    tx.inMessage.body
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );
            } catch {
                console.log(
                    'BODY BOC: UNAVAILABLE'
                );
            }
        }

        const out = outputs(tx);

        for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) {
            printMessage(out[i], i + 1);
        }
    }

    if (count === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'NO TRANSACTION AT OR AFTER SOURCE TIME.'
        );
    }
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 DEEP LIQUIDITY TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'SOURCE TIME:',
        SOURCE_TIME
    );

    console.log(
        'USER:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    );

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DEEP TRACE COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY.');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');

    void provider;

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const API = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const USER =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const POOL =
    'EQB2Ijt3RHMCfzYBUH44pPD04IYZOj8Ol4RsyNDgQhWwMvd_';

const TARGET_HASHES = new Set([
    'yahs7oxwg9624eIgSCqu/yvho9ZlHOMjaEySpupB6do=',
    '/pyWeeTVHT5CJqLG0I4u4OJF0L3Qv2ro6mnPwNsDZ5U=',
    'mPHUbHn8N68UPhyQAtu5P8nabFFfOpqRszhb3oFMLR4=',
    'Ia2y2HJbueUFlilQdEqzvjVblc3Sd1gtpulayzDfk5M=',
    '3kdI6EWpP9m1d7JKO808HpzEADbzgSX37/3ufQGKyI8=',
]);

function short(value: unknown, length = 36): string {
    const s = String(value ?? '');
    return s.length <= length
        ? s
        : `${s.slice(0, 16)}...${s.slice(-16)}`;
}

function getHash(tx: any): string {
    return String(
        tx.hash ??
        tx.transaction_hash ??
        tx.tx_hash ??
        ''
    );
}

function getMessages(tx: any): any[] {
    const candidates = [
        tx.messages,
        tx.out_msgs,
        tx.out_messages,
        tx.out_msgs_list,
    ];

    for (const value of candidates) {
        if (Array.isArray(value)) {
            return value;
        }
    }

    return [];
}

function getBody(msg: any): unknown {
    return (
        msg.message_content ??
        msg.msg_data ??
        msg.body ??
        msg.message?.body ??
        null
    );
}

function decodeOpcode(body: unknown): string {
    if (!body) {
        return 'NO_BODY';
    }

    try {
        let boc: string | null = null;

        if (typeof body === 'string') {
            boc = body;
        } else if (typeof body === 'object') {
            const b = body as Record<string, unknown>;

            if (typeof b.body === 'string') {
                boc = b.body;
            } else if (typeof b.boc === 'string') {
                boc = b.boc;
            } else if (typeof b.value === 'string') {
                boc = b.value;
            }
        }

        if (!boc) {
            return 'BODY_FORMAT_UNKNOWN';
        }

        const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        if (slice.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'BODY_LT_32_BITS';
        }

        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        return '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_ERROR';
    }
}

async function api(path: string): Promise<any> {
    const response = await fetch(`${API}${path}`);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    return await response.json();
}

async function getTransactions(
    address: string
): Promise<any[]> {
    const data = await api(
        `/transactions?account=${encodeURIComponent(address)}&limit=50&offset=0&sort=desc`
    );

    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        return data;
    }

    return Array.isArray(data.transactions)
        ? data.transactions
        : [];
}

async function inspectAccount(
    name: string,
    address: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `================ ${name} ================`
    );
    console.log(`Address: ${address}`);

    try {
        const transactions =
            await getTransactions(address);

        const selected = transactions.filter(
            (tx) => TARGET_HASHES.has(getHash(tx))
        );

        console.log(
            `Transactions checked: ${transactions.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Target transactions found: ${selected.length}`
        );

        for (const tx of selected) {
            const hash = getHash(tx);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '--------------------------------------------'
            );
            console.log(`HASH: ${hash}`);
            console.log(`LT: ${tx.lt ?? 'unknown'}`);

            const description =
                tx.description ??
                tx.transaction_description ??
                {};

            const compute =
                description.compute_phase ??
                tx.compute_phase ??
                {};

            const action =
                description.action_phase ??
                tx.action_phase ??
                {};

            console.log(
                `Compute success: ${compute.success ?? 'unknown'}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Compute exit: ${
                    compute.exit_code ??
                    compute.exitCode ??
                    'unknown'
                }`
            );

            console.log(
                `Action success: ${action.success ?? 'unknown'}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Action result: ${
                    action.result_code ??
                    action.resultCode ??
                    'unknown'
                }`
            );

            const messages = getMessages(tx);

            console.log(
                `Outgoing messages: ${messages.length}`
            );

            let index = 0;

            for (const msg of messages) {
                index++;

                const source =
                    msg.source ??
                    msg.src ??
                    msg.source_address ??
                    '';

                const destination =
                    msg.destination ??
                    msg.dst ??
                    msg.destination_address ??
                    '';

                const value =
                    msg.value ??
                    msg.value_nanoton ??
                    'unknown';

                console.log('');
                console.log(`MESSAGE ${index}`);
                console.log(`From: ${short(source)}`);
                console.log(`To: ${short(destination)}`);
                console.log(`Value: ${value}`);

                const opcode =
                    decodeOpcode(getBody(msg));

                console.log(`Opcode: ${opcode}`);

                if (
                    String(destination)
                        .startsWith(POOL.slice(0, 12))
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        '*** DESTINATION MATCHES POOL ***'
                    );
                }

                if (
                    String(destination)
                        .startsWith(ROUTER.slice(0, 12))
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        '*** DESTINATION MATCHES ROUTER ***'
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            `Error: ${
                error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : String(error)
            }`
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceLocal0Origin6618.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const BODY_START = 0x0022ccb5;
const BODY_END   = 0x0022d0a1;

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE LOCAL0 ORIGIN - FUNC 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(BODY_START)} .. ${hx(BODY_END)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let local0Writes = [];
let local0Reads = [];
let fieldUses = [];

let p = BODY_START;

// Skip local declarations
const groups = u32(p);
p = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
  const n = u32(p);
  p = n.next;
  p++; // valtype
}

while (p < BODY_END) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (x.value === 0) {
      local0Reads.push(off);
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} local.get 0`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (x.value === 0) {
      local0Writes.push(off);

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `*** LOCAL0 SET @ ${hx(off)} ***`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (x.value === 0) {
      local0Writes.push(off);

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `*** LOCAL0 TEE @ ${hx(off)} ***`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    if (o.value === 40 || o.value === 56 || o.value === 16) {

      fieldUses.push({
        address: off,
        kind: "load",
        offset: o.value
      });

      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i32.load offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    if (o.value === 40 || o.value === 56 || o.value === 16) {

      fieldUses.push({
        address: off,
        kind: "store",
        offset: o.value
      });

      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i32.store offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i64.load
  if (op === 0x29) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    if (o.value === 40 || o.value === 56 || o.value === 16) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i64.load offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i64.store
  if (op === 0x37) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    if (o.value === 40 || o.value === 56 || o.value === 16) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i64.store offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (
      x.value === 446 ||
      x.value === 248 ||
      x.value === 6618
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = u32(p);
    p = t.next;

    const table = u32(p);
    p = table.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    continue;
  }

  // control / misc operands
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    // block type
    if (p < BODY_END && wasm[p] === 0x40)
      p++;
    else {
      // signed/valtype byte
      p++;
    }
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b) {
    continue;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `LOCAL0 SET/TEE COUNT : ${local0Writes.length}`
);

console.log(
  `LOCAL0 GET COUNT     : ${local0Reads.length}`
);

if (local0Writes.length === 0) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("RESULT:");
  console.log("LOCAL0 IS NEVER REASSIGNED IN THIS FUNCTION.");
  console.log("Therefore local0 is a function parameter.");
} else {
  console.log("");
  console.log("RESULT:");
  console.log("LOCAL0 IS REASSIGNED.");
  console.log("Inspect the SET/TEE addresses above.");
}

console.log("");
console.log("FIELD OPERATIONS:");
for (const x of fieldUses) {
  console.log(
    `${hx(x.address)} ${x.kind} offset=${x.offset}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const BODY_START = 0x0022ccb5;
const BODY_END   = 0x0022d0a1;
const TARGET     = 0x0022ce75;

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE LOCAL0 + 56");
console.log("FUNC 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(BODY_START)} .. ${hx(BODY_END)}`);
console.log(`TARGET CALL : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search for:
//
// local.get 0
// i32.const 56
// i32.add
//
// and also:
//
// local.get 0
// i32.const 16
// i32.add
// ...
// load/store offset 40
//
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let p = BODY_START; p < BODY_END - 10; p++) {

  // local.get 0
  if (wasm[p] !== 0x20 || wasm[p + 1] !== 0x00)
    continue;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Direct local0 + constant
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (wasm[p + 2] === 0x41) {

    const c = i32(p + 3);

    if (c.value === 56 || c.value === 16) {

      const q = c.next;

      if (wasm[q] === 0x6a) {

        hits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`HIT ${hits}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        console.log(`${hx(p)} local.get 0`);

        console.log(
          `${hx(p + 2)} i32.const ${c.value}`
        );

        console.log(
          `${hx(q)} i32.add`
        );

        console.log(
          `EXPRESSION : local0 + ${c.value}`
        );

        // show following bytes/instructions raw
        let r = q + 1;

        console.log("FOLLOWING BYTES:");

        for (let k = 0; k < 16 && r < BODY_END; k++, r++) {

          console.log(
            `  ${hx(r)} ${wasm[r].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search specifically for load/store offset 56.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT OFFSET-56 MEMORY OPERATIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

let offsetHits = 0;

for (let p = BODY_START; p < BODY_END - 5; p++) {

  const op = wasm[p];

  // i32.load / i32.store
  if (op !== 0x28 && op !== 0x36)
    continue;

  let q = p + 1;

  const align = u32(q);
  q = align.next;

  const off = u32(q);
  q = off.next;

  if (off.value === 56) {

    offsetHits++;

    console.log(
      `${hx(p)} ${op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"} offset=56`
    );
  }
}

const b = TARGET + 24;

  );

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x95e67;
const TARGET = 0x963d1;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function sleb64(p: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];

    v |= BigInt(x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7n;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (s < 64n && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    next: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

const names: Record<number,string> = {
  0x20:"local.get",
  0x21:"local.set",
  0x22:"local.tee",
  0x23:"global.get",
  0x24:"global.set",

  0x28:"i32.load",
  0x29:"i64.load",

  0x2c:"i32.load8_s",
  0x2d:"i32.load8_u",

  0x30:"i64.load8_s",
  0x31:"i64.load8_u",
  0x32:"i64.load16_s",
  0x33:"i64.load16_u",
  0x34:"i64.load32_s",
  0x35:"i64.load32_u",

  0x36:"i32.store",
  0x37:"i64.store",
  0x3e:"i64.store32",

  0x41:"i32.const",
  0x42:"i64.const",

  0x46:"i32.eq",
  0x47:"i32.ne",
  0x48:"i32.lt_s",
  0x49:"i32.lt_u",
  0x4a:"i32.gt_s",
  0x4b:"i32.gt_u",
  0x4e:"i32.ge_s",
  0x4f:"i32.ge_u",

  0x6a:"i32.add",
  0x6b:"i32.sub",

  0x71:"i32.and",
  0x72:"i32.or",
  0x73:"i32.xor",
  0x74:"i32.shl",
  0x75:"i32.shr_s",
  0x76:"i32.shr_u",

  0x7c:"i64.add",
  0x7d:"i64.sub",
  0x7e:"i64.mul",

  0x83:"i64.and",
  0x84:"i64.or",
  0x85:"i64.xor",
  0x86:"i64.shl",
  0x87:"i64.shr_s",
  0x88:"i64.shr_u",

  0xac:"i64.extend_i32_s",
  0xad:"i64.extend_i32_u",

  0x10:"call",
  0x1a:"drop",
  0x03:"loop",
  0x04:"if",
  0x05:"else",
  0x0b:"end",
  0x0c:"br",
  0x0d:"br_if"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL13 BACKWARD TRACE V20");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Function range : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`TARGET         : ${hx(TARGET)} local.get 13`);
console.log();

let p = START;
let refs: { at:number; kind:string }[] = [];

while (p < TARGET) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let name =
    names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;

    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    if (r.value === 13) {
      refs.push({
        at,
        kind:
          op === 0x20 ? "get" :
          op === 0x21 ? "set" :
          "tee"
      });
    }
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb64(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (
    op >= 0x28 &&
    op <= 0x35
  ) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra = ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (
    op >= 0x36 &&
    op <= 0x3e
  ) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra = ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x10 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` blocktype=${r.value}`;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL13 REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const r of refs) {
  console.log(
    `${hx(r.at)}  local.${r.kind} 13`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LAST PRODUCER BEFORE 0x963d1");
console.log("============================================================");

const producers =
  refs.filter(r => r.at < TARGET &&
    (r.kind === "set" || r.kind === "tee"));

if (producers.length) {

  const last = producers[producers.length - 1];

  console.log(
    `${hx(last.at)}  local.${last.kind} 13`
  );

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `Distance to target: ${TARGET - last.at} bytes`
  );

} else {

  console.log("NO local13 producer found");

}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V20");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x96520;
const END   = 0x96600;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

FILE: scripts\traceLocal4Source.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 0x0022ce73;

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = codeStart;

const count = u32(q);
q = count.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < count.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const sz = u32(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + sz.value;

  if (TARGET >= bodyStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {

    targetBody = {
      bodyIndex,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd
    };

    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetBody) {
  throw new Error("Target address is not inside a function body");
}

const actualFunction = 83 + targetBody.bodyIndex;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE SOURCE OF LOCAL4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`BODY INDEX     : ${targetBody.bodyIndex}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION       : ${actualFunction}`);
console.log(`BODY START     : ${hx(targetBody.bodyStart)}`);
console.log(`BODY END       : ${hx(targetBody.bodyEnd)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Skip local declaration vector
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = targetBody.bodyStart;

const localGroups = u32(p);
p = localGroups.next;

console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS   : ${localGroups.value}`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

  const n = u32(p);
  p = n.next;

  // value type
  p++;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Symbolic stack
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const stack = [];
const locals = new Map();

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop() : "<?>"; 
}

function getLocal(n) {
  return locals.has(n)
    ? locals.get(n)
    : `local${n}`;
}

function setLocal(n, e, addr) {

  locals.set(n, e);

  if (n === 4) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("************************************************************");
    console.log("LOCAL4 DEFINITION");
    console.log("************************************************************");
    console.log(`ADDRESS : ${hx(addr)}`);
    console.log(`local4  = ${e}`);
    console.log("************************************************************");
    console.log("");
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode
// ------------------------------------------------------------

while (p < targetBody.bodyEnd) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // end
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} end`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = getLocal(x.value);

    push(e);

    if (x.value === 4) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} local.get 4 -> ${e}  <=== LOCAL4`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    setLocal(x.value, e, off);

    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    setLocal(x.value, e, off);

    push(e);

    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(String(x.value));

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const e =
      o.value === 0
        ? `MEM[${addr}]`
        : `MEM[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

    push(e);

    continue;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const value = pop();
    const addr = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.store offset=${o.value} : ${addr} = ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a}+${b})`);

    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a}-${b})`);

    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`
    );

    // We do not know the exact signature here.
    // Preserve symbolic return for calls that can define local4.
    push(`RET[${x.value}]`);

    continue;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {

    const type = u32(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = u32(p);
    p = table.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
    );

    push(`RET[INDIRECT:type${type.value}]`);

    continue;
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {

    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a}==0)`);

    continue;
  }

  // i32.eq
  if (op === 0x46) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a}==${b})`);

    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    pop();

    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    pop();

    continue;
  }

  // i32.wrap_i64
  if (op === 0xa7) {

    const a = pop();

    push(`wrap32(${a})`);

    continue;
  }

  // unknown opcode
  console.log(
    `${hx(off)} UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL LOCAL DEFINITIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [n, e] of locals.entries()) {
  console.log(`local${n} = ${e}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceLocal6Mem68V21.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x95e67;
const TARGET = 0x96265;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

const names: Record<number,string> = {
  0x20:"local.get",
  0x21:"local.set",
  0x22:"local.tee",

  0x28:"i32.load",
  0x29:"i64.load",

  0x34:"i64.load32_s",
  0x35:"i64.load32_u",

  0x36:"i32.store",
  0x37:"i64.store",
  0x3e:"i64.store32",

  0x41:"i32.const",
  0x42:"i64.const",

  0x6a:"i32.add",
  0x6b:"i32.sub",

  0x7c:"i64.add",
  0x7d:"i64.sub",
  0x7e:"i64.mul",
  0x83:"i64.and",
  0x85:"i64.xor",
  0x87:"i64.shr_s",
  0x88:"i64.shr_u",

  0xac:"i64.extend_i32_s",
  0xad:"i64.extend_i32_u",

  0x10:"call",
  0x1a:"drop",
  0x03:"loop",
  0x04:"if",
  0x05:"else",
  0x0b:"end",
  0x0c:"br",
  0x0d:"br_if"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL6 + 68 MEMORY PRODUCER V21");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Range : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`Target: ${hx(TARGET)} local.get 6 + i64.load32_s offset=68`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < TARGET) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let name =
    names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x42) {
    // skip signed LEB64
    while (p < TARGET) {
      const x = b[p++];
      if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    }
  }

  else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra = ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra = ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x10 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` blocktype=${r.value}`;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET CONTEXT");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0x96250; i < 0x96275; i += 16) {

  const e = Math.min(i + 16, 0x96275);
  const bytes: string[] = [];

  for (let j = i; j < e; j++)
    bytes.push(b[j].toString(16).padStart(2,"0"));

  console.log(`${hx(i)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V21");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceLocal6Offset68ProducerV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x95e67;
const END   = 0x96265;

function readU32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, q = p;
  while (q < b.length) {
    const x = b[q++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: q };
    s += 7;
  }
  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readS32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, q = p, x = 0;
  while (q < b.length) {
    x = b[q++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: q };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = START;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL6 + 68 MEMORY PRODUCER V22");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`Range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET: MEM[G-368+68] = MEM[G-300]");
console.log();

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = readU32(p); p = r.next;
    if (r.value === 6)
      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get 6`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = readU32(p); p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = readU32(p); p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readS32(p); p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    let q = p;
    let x = 0, s = 0, c = 0;
    while (q < b.length) {
      const z = b[q++];
      c++;
      x |= (z & 0x7f) << s;
      s += 7;
      if (!(z & 0x80)) break;
    }
    p = q;
    continue;
  }

  // memory load/store family
  if (
    op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d || op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 || op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
    op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
    op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 || op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b || op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
    op === 0x3e
  ) {
    const a = readU32(p); p = a.next;
    const o = readU32(p); p = o.next;

    if (o.value === 68 || o.value === 64 || o.value === 60 ||
        o.value === 56 || o.value === 52 || o.value === 48 ||
        o.value === 44 || o.value === 40 || o.value === 36 ||
        o.value === 32 || o.value === 28 || o.value === 24 ||
        o.value === 20 || o.value === 16 || o.value === 12 ||
        o.value === 8 || o.value === 4 || o.value === 0) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} memory op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }
    continue;
  }

  // calls
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readU32(p); p = r.next;
    if (r.value === 116 || r.value === 136 || r.value === 87 ||
        r.value === 205 || r.value === 3425) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} call ${r.value}`);
    }
    continue;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = readU32(p); p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (b[p] === 0x40) p++;
    else {
      const r = readS32(p); p = r.next;
    }
    continue;
  }

  // end / else / drop / arithmetic / comparisons etc.
  if (
    op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 || op === 0x1a ||
    (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x66) ||
    (op >= 0x67 && op <= 0x91) ||
    (op >= 0xa7 && op <= 0xbf)
  ) {
    continue;
  }

  // memory.copy / memory.fill
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = readU32(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (r.value === 8 || r.value === 10) {
      const a = readU32(p); p = a.next;
      const c = readU32(p); p = c.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.value} imm=${a.value},${c.value}`);
    }
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V22");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x0e1147;
const END   = 0x0e1181;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v: v >>> 0, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (-1 << s);

  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    if (r.v === 2 || r.v === 3 || r.v === 8)
      console.log(`${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`);

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD"} ` +
      `op=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
      `align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

    if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.p;

      x = uleb(p);
      p = x.p;
    }

    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log("END V32");

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000014n;

const RPC =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function addr(a: Address | null): string {
    return a ? a.toString() : 'null';
}

function opcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) return 'NONE';

        return '0x' +
            s.loadUint(32)
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function boc(body: any): string {
    try {
        return body.toBoc().toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return 'ERROR';
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP MINT TRANSACTION');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nLP WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        LP_WALLET,
        { limit: 30 }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        x => BigInt(x.lt.toString()) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TX NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\nHASH:');
    console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('\nUTIME:');
    console.log(tx.now);

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // IN
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    const inMsg: any = tx.inMessage;

    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('INCOMING');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    if (inMsg) {

        const info: any = inMsg.info;

        if (info.type === 'internal') {

            console.log('\nSOURCE:');
            console.log(addr(info.src));

            console.log('\nDESTINATION:');
            console.log(addr(info.dest));

            console.log('\nTON VALUE:');
            console.log(info.value.coins.toString());

            console.log('\nOPCODE:');
            console.log(opcode(inMsg.body));

            console.log('\nBODY BOC HEX:');
            console.log(boc(inMsg.body));

        } else {
            console.log('\nINCOMING IS NOT INTERNAL');
        }

    } else {
        console.log('\nNO INCOMING MESSAGE');
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // OUT
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('OUTGOING');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    let i = 0;

    for (const [, msg] of tx.outMessages) {

        i++;

        const info: any = msg.info;

        console.log(`\nOUT #${i}`);

        if (info.type === 'internal') {

            console.log('SOURCE:');
            console.log(addr(info.src));

            console.log('DESTINATION:');
            console.log(addr(info.dest));

            console.log('TON VALUE:');
            console.log(info.value.coins.toString());

            console.log('TON / 1e9:');
            console.log(
                (Number(info.value.coins) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
            );
        }

        console.log('OPCODE:');
        console.log(opcode(msg.body));

        console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');
        console.log(boc(msg.body));
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\traceLpWalletLastV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99811882000005n;

const RPC = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function shortAddress(address: Address | null): string {
    return address ? address.toString() : 'null';
}

function getOpcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'NONE';
        }

        return '0x' + s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function getBodyHex(body: any): string {
    try {
        return body.toBoc().toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return 'ERROR';
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET LAST TRANSACTION TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nLP WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('\nFetching LP Wallet transactions...');

    try {

        const txs = await client.getTransactions(
            LP_WALLET,
            {
                limit: 20,
            }
        );

        const tx = txs.find(
            (x) => BigInt(x.lt.toString()) === TARGET_LT
        );

        if (!tx) {
            console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
            console.log('Returned transactions:');

            for (const x of txs) {
                console.log(
                    x.lt.toString(),
                    x.hash().toString('hex')
                );
            }

            return;
        }

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log('\nLT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('\nHASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

        console.log('\nUTIME:');
        console.log(tx.now);

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('INCOMING MESSAGES');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        let incomingCount = 0;

        for (const [key, msg] of tx.inMessage?.info?.type === 'internal'
            ? [['in', tx.inMessage] as const]
            : []) {

            incomingCount++;

            const info: any = msg.info;

            console.log(`\nINCOMING #${incomingCount}`);

            console.log('SOURCE:');
            console.log(shortAddress(info.src));

            console.log('DESTINATION:');
            console.log(shortAddress(info.dest));

            console.log('TON VALUE:');
            console.log(
                info.value.coins.toString()
            );

            console.log('TON / 1e9:');
            console.log(
                (Number(info.value.coins) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
            );

            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(getOpcode(msg.body));

            console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');
            console.log(getBodyHex(msg.body));
        }

        if (incomingCount === 0) {
            console.log('\nNO INTERNAL INCOMING MESSAGE');
        }

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('OUTGOING MESSAGES');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        const outMessages: any[] = [];

        for (const [, msg] of tx.outMessages) {
            outMessages.push(msg);
        }

        console.log('\nOUTGOING COUNT:');
        console.log(outMessages.length);

        let index = 0;

        for (const msg of outMessages) {

            index++;

            const info: any = msg.info;

            console.log(`\nOUTGOING #${index}`);

            if (info.type === 'internal') {

                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(shortAddress(info.src));

                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(shortAddress(info.dest));

                console.log('TON VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    info.value.coins.toString()
                );

                console.log('TON / 1e9:');
                console.log(
                    (Number(info.value.coins) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
                );
            }

            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(getOpcode(msg.body));

            console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');
            console.log(getBodyHex(msg.body));
        }

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
        console.log('TRACE COMPLETE');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

    } catch (e: any) {

        console.log('\nERROR:');

        if (e?.response?.data?.result) {
            console.log(e.response.data.result);
        } else {
            console.log(e?.message || String(e));
        }
    }
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const TARGET_LT = '99491281000001';

const TARGET_HASH =
    'iGqT9ftOmAjasbHcEntrKYL4acopbvdJrUnoiGmYE9c=';

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const STON_ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

function opcode(cell: Cell | null | undefined): string {
    if (!cell) return 'NONE';

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
        return 'NO_32BIT_OPCODE';
    }

    return (
        '0x' +
        s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
}

function boc(cell: Cell | null | undefined): string {
    if (!cell) return 'NONE';

    return cell.toBoc().toString('base64');
}

function printMessage(
    label: string,
    message: any
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('TYPE:');
    console.log(message.info.type);

    if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
        console.log('SOURCE:');
        console.log(message.info.src.toString());

        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(message.info.dest.toString());

        console.log('TON VALUE:');
        console.log(
            message.info.value.coins.toString()
        );
    }

    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(opcode(message.body));

    console.log('BODY BOC:');
    console.log(boc(message.body));
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');

    console.log('');

    console.log(TARGET_HASH);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('STON ROUTER:');
    console.log(STON_ROUTER.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Connected to TON Center Testnet.');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching exact target transaction...');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        USER,
        {
            limit: 10,
            lt: TARGET_LT,
            hash: TARGET_HASH,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TRANSACTIONS RETURNED: ${txs.length}`
    );

    if (txs.length === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TARGET TRANSACTION WAS NOT RETURNED.'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Trying nearby transactions around target LT...'
        );

        const nearby = await client.getTransactions(
            USER,
            {
                limit: 10,
                to_lt: (
                    BigInt(TARGET_LT) + 100n
                ).toString(),
            }
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `NEARBY TRANSACTIONS: ${nearby.length}`
        );

        for (const tx of nearby) {
            console.log(
                `${tx.lt} | ${new Date(
                    tx.now * 1000
                ).toISOString()} | ${tx.hash().toString('base64')}`
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
        );

        return;
    }

    let txIndex = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        txIndex++;

        console.log('');
        console.log('==============================================');
        console.log(`TARGET RESULT #${txIndex}`);
        console.log('==============================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIME UTC:');
        console.log(
            new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.hash().toString('base64')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('IN MESSAGE:');

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            printMessage(
                'IN MESSAGE DETAILS',
                tx.inMessage
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `OUT MESSAGE COUNT: ${tx.outMessagesCount}`
        );

        let outIndex = 0;

        for (const message of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            outIndex++;

            printMessage(
                `OUT MESSAGE #${outIndex}`,
                message
            );

            if (
                message.info.type === 'internal' &&
                message.info.dest.equals(
                    ROOSTER_WALLET
                )
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '*** ROOSTER WALLET MESSAGE FOUND ***'
                );
            }

            if (
                message.info.type === 'internal' &&
                message.info.dest.equals(
                    STON_ROUTER
                )
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '*** STON ROUTER MESSAGE FOUND ***'
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' TRACE COMPLETE');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceMappingDispatch.ts
