import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNCS = [39, 1974];

function readU32LE(p: number): number {
  return (
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number } {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
      return {
        start: sectionStart,
        end: sectionEnd,
      };
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function parseCodeBodies(): {
  ordinal: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  instrStart: number;
}[] {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const localCountInfo = readULEB(p);
    const localCount = localCountInfo.value;
    p = localCountInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      p += 1;
    }

    const instrStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      instrStart,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.xor",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeOne(p: number): { next: number; text: string } {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(p);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(p);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = readULEB(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = readULEB(p);
    p = table.next;

    return {
      next: p,
      text: `${opcodeName(op)} type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x36
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    return {
      next: p,
      text: `${opcodeName(op)} align=${align.value} offset=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x6a ||
    op === 0x6b ||
    op === 0x45 ||
    op === 0x46 ||
    op === 0x47 ||
    op === 0x71 ||
    op === 0x0b ||
    op === 0x0f ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    return {
      next: p,
      text: opcodeName(op),
    };
  }

  return {
    next: p,
    text: `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  };
}

const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

console.log("TRACE MAPPING DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNCTIONS = ${TARGET_FUNCS.join(", ")}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log("");

for (const ordinal of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  const body = bodies[ordinal];

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ORDINAL ${ordinal}: NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`FUNCTION ORDINAL ${ordinal}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = 0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.bodyEnd
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
  console.log(
    `INSTR = 0x${body.instrStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = body.instrStart;
  let depth = 0;

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const addr = p;

    let decoded;

    try {
      decoded = decodeOne(p);
    } catch {
      console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} DECODE_ERROR`
      );
      break;
    }

    const bytes = wasm.subarray(addr, decoded.next);

    const interesting =
      decoded.text.includes("4788") ||
      decoded.text.includes("4789") ||
      decoded.text.includes("4790") ||
      decoded.text.includes("4791") ||
      decoded.text.includes("call_indirect") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.load") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.add") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.sub") ||
      decoded.text.includes("local.get") ||
      decoded.text.includes("local.set") ||
      decoded.text.includes("local.tee") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.eq") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.ne") ||
      decoded.text.includes("i32.const");

    if (interesting) {
      console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${decoded.text}`
      );
      console.log(
        `    BYTES = ${Array.from(bytes)
          .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")}`
      );
    }

    if (wasm[addr] === 0x02 || wasm[addr] === 0x03 || wasm[addr] === 0x04) {
      depth++;
    }

    if (wasm[addr] === 0x0b && depth > 0) {
      depth--;
    }

    p = decoded.next;
  }

  console.log("");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceMappingValues.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_VALUES = new Set([
    4788,
    4789,
    4790,
    4791
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("TRACE MAPPING VALUES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const fc = uleb(q);
q = fc.next;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", fc.value);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < fc.value; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(q);
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

    /*
     * Search for i32.const 4788..4791.
     *
     * These are the actual mapping values, not addresses.
     */
    let x = bodyStart;

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const at = x;
        const op = wasm[x++];

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = uleb(x);
            x = r.next;

            const value = r.value >>> 0;

            if (TARGET_VALUES.has(value)) {

                console.log(
                    `FUNC ORDINAL ${ordinal} @ ${hex(at)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  i32.const ${value}`
                );

                /*
                 * Show local byte context.
                 */
                const from = Math.max(
                    bodyStart,
                    at - 12
                );

                const to = Math.min(
                    bodyEnd,
                    at + 20
                );

                const bytes: string[] = [];

                for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
                    bytes.push(
                        wasm[i]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    "  BYTES =",
                    bytes.join(" ")
                );

                hits++;
                console.log("");
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Skip immediate operands for the most common
         * instructions that contain ULEB/SLEB values.
         *
         * This is deliberately conservative: the purpose
         * is only to find literal mapping values.
         */

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24 ||
            op === 0x10
        ) {
            const r = uleb(x);
            x = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39
        ) {
            const a = uleb(x);
            x = a.next;

            const o = uleb(x);
            x = o.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x04 ||
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x06
        ) {
            const r = uleb(x);
            x = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = uleb(x);
            x = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const t = uleb(x);
            x = t.next;

            const table = uleb(x);
            x = table.next;
        }
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LITERAL MAPPING VALUE HITS =", hits);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGET VALUES:");
console.log("  4788 -> FUNC122");
console.log("  4789 -> FUNC656");
console.log("  4790 -> FUNC91");
console.log("  4791 -> FUNC787");
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceMemory19534C.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: v, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function signedLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return { value: value | 0, next: p };
        }
    }
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("TRACE MEMORY ADDRESS 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND ALL RAW i32.const 0x19534C
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("-".repeat(72));
console.log("RAW ADDRESS REFERENCES");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

let rawHits = [];

for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length - 1; i++) {

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x41) continue;

    try {
        const r = uleb(i + 1);

        if (r.value === TARGET) {
            rawHits.push(i);
            console.log(`${hx(i)}  i32.const 0x19534C`);
        }
    } catch {}
}

console.log();
console.log(`RAW REFERENCES : ${rawHits.length}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PARSE CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const IMPORTS = 83;

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = uleb(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;

        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {

            const b = uleb(p);
            const size = b.value;
            p = b.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            const func = IMPORTS + ordinal;

            let hits = [];

            for (let q = start; q < bodyEnd; q++) {

                if (wasm[q] !== 0x41) continue;

                try {
                    const r = uleb(q + 1);

                    if (r.value === TARGET) {
                        hits.push(q);
                    }
                } catch {}
            }

            if (hits.length === 0) {
                p = bodyEnd;
                continue;
            }

            console.log("=".repeat(72));
            console.log(`FUNCTION ${func}`);
            console.log(`BODY : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
            console.log(`REFERENCES : ${hits.length}`);
            console.log("-".repeat(72));

            for (const hit of hits) {

                console.log(`ADDRESS REFERENCE @ ${hx(hit)}`);

                // Show ~40 bytes before and ~100 after
                const from = Math.max(start, hit - 40);
                const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, hit + 110);

                console.log();
                console.log("DECODED CONTEXT:");

                let q = from;

                while (q < to) {

                    const op = wasm[q];

                    // i32.const
                    if (op === 0x41) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i32.load
                    if (op === 0x28) {

                        const a = uleb(q + 1);
                        const o = uleb(a.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  i32.load ` +
                            `align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}`
                        );

                        q = o.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // i32.store
                    if (op === 0x36) {

                        const a = uleb(q + 1);
                        const o = uleb(a.next);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  i32.store ` +
                            `align=${a.value} offset=${hx(o.value)}`
                        );

                        q = o.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.get
                    if (op === 0x20) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.set
                    if (op === 0x21) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // local.tee
                    if (op === 0x22) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call
                    if (op === 0x10) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                        );

                        q = r.next;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // call_indirect
                    if (op === 0x11) {

                        const r = uleb(q + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${r.value} table=${wasm[r.next]}`
                        );

                        q = r.next + 1;
                        continue;
                    }

                    // return
                    if (op === 0x0f) {

                        console.log(
                            `${hx(q)}  RETURN`
                        );

                        q++;
                        continue;
                    }

                    q++;
                }

                console.log();
            }

            console.log();

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceMemoryIndex4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_SLOT = 114;
const TARGET_TYPE = 0;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0;

type Instr = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm: number[];
    text: string;
};

type Body = {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    bytes: Buffer;
};

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= -1 << shift;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function readVec(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { count: number; next: number } {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);

    return {
        count: r.value,
        next: r.next,
    };
}

function readName(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { name: string; next: number } {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);

    const end = r.next + r.value;

    return {
        name: buf
            .subarray(r.next, end)
            .toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function parseSections(wasm: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    let typeSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let importSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let funcSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let codeSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);

        p = s.next;

        const start = p;

        if (id === 1) {
            typeSec = {
                start,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        if (id === 2) {
            importSec = {
                start,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        if (id === 3) {
            funcSec = {
                start,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        if (id === 10) {
            codeSec = {
                start,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    return {
        typeSec,
        importSec,
        funcSec,
        codeSec,
    };
}

function parseImports(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
): number {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);

    p = v.next;

    let importedFuncs = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const mod = readName(wasm, p);
        p = mod.next;

        const name = readName(wasm, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);

            p = r.next;

            importedFuncs++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p++;

            const flags = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }
    }

    return importedFuncs;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
): number[] {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);

    p = v.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const r = uleb(wasm, p);

        result.push(r.value);

        p = r.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function parseBodies(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number },
    importedFuncs: number
): Body[] {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);

    p = v.next;

    const result: Body[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const s = uleb(wasm, p);

        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        result.push({
            func: importedFuncs + i,
            start,
            end,
            bytes: wasm.subarray(start, end),
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return result;
}

function decode(
    bytes: Buffer,
    base: number
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const start = p;
        const off = base + p;
        const op = bytes[p++];

        const imm: number[] = [];

        let text =
            `op=0x${op
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, "0")}`;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text =
                op === 0x20
                    ? `local.get ${r.value}`
                    : op === 0x21
                    ? `local.set ${r.value}`
                    : `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `call ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = b.next;

            imm.push(a.value, b.value);

            text =
                `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x30 ||
            op === 0x31 ||
            op === 0x32 ||
            op === 0x33 ||
            op === 0x34 ||
            op === 0x35 ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b ||
            op === 0x3c ||
            op === 0x3d ||
            op === 0x3e ||
            op === 0x3f ||
            op === 0x40
        ) {
            const align = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = offset.next;

            imm.push(
                align.value,
                offset.value
            );

            const name =
                op === 0x28
                    ? "i32.load"
                    : op === 0x29
                    ? "i64.load"
                    : op === 0x2c
                    ? "i32.load8_s"
                    : op === 0x2d
                    ? "i32.load8_u"
                    : op === 0x2e
                    ? "i32.load16_s"
                    : op === 0x2f
                    ? "i32.load16_u"
                    : "memory";

            text =
                `${name} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x25) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `table.get ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x26) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `table.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            if (p >= bytes.length) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Missing blocktype"
                );
            }

            const first = bytes[p++];

            if (
                first !== 0x40 &&
                first >= 0x80
            ) {
                let b = first;
                let value = first & 0x7f;
                let shift = 7;

                while (b & 0x80) {
                    if (p >= bytes.length) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Blocktype overflow"
                        );
                    }

                    b = bytes[p++];

                    value |=
                        (b & 0x7f) << shift;

                    shift += 7;
                }

                imm.push(value);
            } else {
                imm.push(first);
            }

            text =
                op === 0x02
                    ? "block"
                    : op === 0x03
                    ? "loop"
                    : "if";
        } else if (op === 0x05) {
            text = "else";
        } else if (op === 0x0b) {
            text = "end";
        } else if (op === 0x0f) {
            text = "return";
        } else if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text =
                op === 0x0c
                    ? `br ${r.value}`
                    : `br_if ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x1a) {
            text = "drop";
        } else if (
            op >= 0x45 &&
            op <= 0x4f
        ) {
            text =
                `i32.compare 0x${op
                    .toString(16)}`;
        } else if (
            op >= 0x6a &&
            op <= 0x78
        ) {
            text =
                `i32.arith 0x${op
                    .toString(16)}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = uleb(bytes, p);

            p = n.next;

            for (
                let i = 0;
                i < n.value + 1;
                i++
            ) {
                const r = uleb(bytes, p);

                p = r.next;

                imm.push(r.value);
            }

            text = "br_table";
        }

        if (p <= start) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder stalled @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        out.push({
            off,
            op,
            imm,
            text,
        });
    }

    return out;
}

function showWindow(
    instrs: Instr[],
    index: number,
    radius: number
) {
    const from = Math.max(
        0,
        index - radius
    );

    const to = Math.min(
        instrs.length,
        index + radius + 1
    );

    for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
        const mark =
            i === index
                ? " >>>"
                : "    ";

        console.log(
            `${mark} 0x${instrs[i].off
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}  ${instrs[i].text}`
        );
    }
}

export async function run() {
    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " MEMORY INDEX INVENTORY -> FUNCTION 4154"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `WASM size       : ${wasm.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target slot     : ${TARGET_SLOT} (0x${TARGET_SLOT
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")})`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target type     : ${TARGET_TYPE}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target table    : ${TARGET_TABLE}`
    );

    console.log();

    const sections = parseSections(wasm);

    if (
        !sections.typeSec ||
        !sections.importSec ||
        !sections.funcSec ||
        !sections.codeSec
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Required sections missing"
        );
    }

    const importedFuncs = parseImports(
        wasm,
        sections.importSec
    );

    const funcTypes = parseFunctionTypes(
        wasm,
        sections.funcSec
    );

    const bodies = parseBodies(
        wasm,
        sections.codeSec,
        importedFuncs
    );

    console.log(
        `Imported funcs : ${importedFuncs}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Defined funcs  : ${funcTypes.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Code bodies    : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log();

    let decodedBodies = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;

    let sites = 0;
    let loadPaths = 0;

    const histogram = new Map<
        number,
        number
    >();

    const samples: {
        func: number;
        callOff: number;
        loadOff: number;
        loadOp: number;
        align: number;
        offset: number;
        distance: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {
        let instrs: Instr[];

        try {
            instrs = decode(
                body.bytes,
                body.start
            );

            decodedBodies++;
        } catch {
            decodeErrors++;
            continue;
        }

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < instrs.length;
            i++
        ) {
            const ci = instrs[i];

            if (
                ci.op !== 0x11 ||
                ci.imm[0] !== TARGET_TYPE ||
                ci.imm[1] !== TARGET_TABLE
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            sites++;

            /*
             * Find the nearest memory load that
             * appears to produce the table index.
             *
             * This is inventory only; no value is
             * assumed yet.
             */
            let loadIndex = -1;

            for (
                let j = i - 1;
                j >= 0 && j >= i - 12;
                j--
            ) {
                const x = instrs[j];

                if (
                    x.op >= 0x28 &&
                    x.op <= 0x3e
                ) {
                    loadIndex = j;
                    break;
                }

                /*
                 * Stop when crossing a call or
                 * another indirect call.
                 */
                if (
                    x.op === 0x10 ||
                    x.op === 0x11
                ) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (loadIndex < 0) {
                continue;
            }

            loadPaths++;

            const load = instrs[loadIndex];

            const offset =
                load.imm.length >= 2
                    ? load.imm[1]
                    : -1;

            const align =
                load.imm.length >= 1
                    ? load.imm[0]
                    : -1;

            const distance =
                i - loadIndex;

            histogram.set(
                offset,
                (histogram.get(offset) ?? 0) + 1
            );

            if (samples.length < 80) {
                samples.push({
                    func: body.func,
                    callOff: ci.off,
                    loadOff: load.off,
                    loadOp: load.op,
                    align,
                    offset,
                    distance,
                });
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "SUMMARY"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `Decoded bodies     : ${decodedBodies}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Decode errors      : ${decodeErrors}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Type-0/table-0     : ${sites}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Memory-load paths  : ${loadPaths}`
    );

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "LOAD OFFSET HISTOGRAM"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const hist = Array.from(
        histogram.entries()
    ).sort(
        (a, b) => b[1] - a[1]
    );

    for (
        const [offset, count] of hist.slice(0, 50)
    ) {
        console.log(
            `offset=${offset
                .toString()
                .padStart(8, " ")}  count=${count}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "FIRST 80 MEMORY-LOAD PATHS"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    for (const s of samples) {
        console.log(
            `func=${s.func} ` +
            `load=0x${s.loadOff.toString(16)} ` +
            `call_indirect=0x${s.callOff.toString(16)} ` +
            `align=${s.align} ` +
            `offset=${s.offset} ` +
            `distance=${s.distance}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "DETAILED WINDOWS"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    /*
     * Show the first 10 paths with actual
     * instruction windows.
     */
    let shown = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        if (shown >= 10) break;

        let instrs: Instr[];

        try {
            instrs = decode(
                body.bytes,
                body.start
            );
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < instrs.length;
            i++
        ) {
            const ci = instrs[i];

            if (
                ci.op !== 0x11 ||
                ci.imm[0] !== TARGET_TYPE ||
                ci.imm[1] !== TARGET_TABLE
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            let loadIndex = -1;

            for (
                let j = i - 1;
                j >= 0 && j >= i - 12;
                j--
            ) {
                const x = instrs[j];

                if (
                    x.op >= 0x28 &&
                    x.op <= 0x3e
                ) {
                    loadIndex = j;
                    break;
                }

                if (
                    x.op === 0x10 ||
                    x.op === 0x11
                ) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (loadIndex < 0) {
                continue;
            }

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `--- FUNCTION ${body.func} ---`
            );

            console.log(
                `LOAD @ 0x${instrs[loadIndex].off.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${ci.off.toString(16)}`
            );

            showWindow(
                instrs,
                i,
                8
            );

            shown++;
        }
    }

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "SAFETY / MODE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "No transaction sent."
    );

    console.log(
        "No WASM modification."
    );

    console.log(
        "Read-only / offline."
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceMutableGlobals.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

const IMPORTED = 83;

// ============================================================
// GLOBAL DEFINITIONS
// ============================================================

const globalSec = section(6);

let gp = globalSec.start;

const globalCount = uleb(gp);
gp = globalCount.next;

interface GlobalInfo {
    index: number;
    type: number;
    mutable: boolean;
    init: string;
}

const globals: GlobalInfo[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < globalCount.value; i++) {

    const type = wasm[gp++];

    const mut = wasm[gp++];

    let init = "UNKNOWN";

    const op = wasm[gp++];

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = sleb(gp);
        gp = x.next;

        init =
            `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`;

    } else if (op === 0x42) {

        const x = sleb(gp);
        gp = x.next;

        init =
            `i64.const ${x.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x43) {

        gp += 4;

        init = "f32.const";

    } else if (op === 0x44) {

        gp += 8;

        init = "f64.const";

    } else if (op === 0x23) {

        const x = uleb(gp);
        gp = x.next;

        init =
            `global.get ${x.value}`;

    } else {

        init = `opcode ${hex(op)}`;

        while (
            gp < globalSec.end &&
            wasm[gp] !== 0x0b
        ) {
            gp++;
        }
    }

    if (wasm[gp] === 0x0b) {
        gp++;
    }

    globals.push({
        index: i,
        type,
        mutable: mut === 1,
        init
    });
}

// ============================================================
// FIND CODE SECTION
// ============================================================

const codeSec = section(10);

let cp = codeSec.start;

const bodyCount = uleb(cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

interface Body {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount.value; ordinal++) {

    const size = uleb(cp);
    cp = size.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size.value;

    let p = start;

    const localCount = uleb(p);
    p = localCount.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {

        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: p
    });

    cp = end;
}

// ============================================================
// MUTABLE GLOBALS
// ============================================================

const mutable = globals.filter(g => g.mutable);

console.log("TRACE MUTABLE GLOBALS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`TOTAL GLOBALS = ${globals.length}`);
console.log(`MUTABLE GLOBALS = ${mutable.length}`);
console.log("");

console.log("MUTABLE GLOBAL DEFINITIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const g of mutable) {

    console.log(
        `GLOBAL ${g.index} | TYPE=${g.type} | INIT=${g.init}`
    );
}

console.log("");

// ============================================================
// SET COUNTS
// ============================================================

const setCounts = new Map<number, number>();

for (const g of mutable) {
    setCounts.set(g.index, 0);
}

// ============================================================
// GLOBAL.GET / SET BY FUNCTION
// ============================================================

interface Ref {
    func: number;
    offset: number;
    kind: "GET" | "SET";
    global: number;
}

const refs: Ref[] = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

    let p = body.instrStart;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const offset = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // global.get
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x23) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            refs.push({
                func: body.func,
                offset,
                kind: "GET",
                global: x.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // global.set
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x24) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            refs.push({
                func: body.func,
                offset,
                kind: "SET",
                global: x.value
            });

            if (setCounts.has(x.value)) {
                setCounts.set(
                    x.value,
                    setCounts.get(x.value)! + 1
                );
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Skip immediates for instructions we know.
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {
            p = sleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            p = sleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            p += 4;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            p += 8;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            p = uleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            p = uleb(p).next;
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            p = uleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            p = uleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const n = uleb(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                p = uleb(p).next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Memory instructions.
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            p = uleb(p).next;
            p = uleb(p).next;

            continue;
        }

        // memory.size / memory.grow
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        // prefixed instruction
        if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd) {
            p = uleb(p).next;
            continue;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// PRINT COUNTS
// ============================================================

console.log("GLOBAL.SET COUNTS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const g of mutable) {

    console.log(
        `GLOBAL ${g.index} SET COUNT = ${setCounts.get(g.index)}`
    );
}

console.log("");

    const sets = refs.filter(

            r.global === g.index &&

    const gets = refs.filter(

}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceOffset128Producers.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_OFFSET = 128;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(pos) {
  const x = readULEB(pos);
  return { next: x.next + x.value };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Sections
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;

let importSection;
let functionSection;
let codeSection;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 2)
    importSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 3)
    functionSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSection = { start, end };

  p = end;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Imported function count
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = importSection.start;

let x = readULEB(p);
const importCount = x.value;
p = x.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

  p = readName(p).next;
  p = readName(p).next;

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {

    p = readULEB(p).next;
    importedFunctions++;

  } else if (kind === 1) {

    p++;
    const flags = wasm[p++];

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;

  } else if (kind === 2) {

    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;

  } else if (kind === 3) {

    p++;
    p++;
  }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Function section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = functionSection.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = fc.value;
p = fc.next;

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {

  const t = readULEB(p);

  functionTypes.push(t.value);

  p = t.next;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Code bodies
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = codeSection.start;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const bodySize = bs.value;
  const bodyStart = bs.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  bodies.push({
    func: importedFunctions + i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd
  });

  p = bodyEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Decoder
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function decodeOne(pos) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text:
        (op === 0x20 ? "local.get " :
         op === 0x21 ? "local.set " :
                        "local.tee ") + x.value,
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = readSLEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      value: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text: `call ${x.value}`,
      func: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const t = readULEB(pos);
    const table = readULEB(t.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: table.next,
      op,
      text:
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      type: t.value
    };
  }

  /*
   * Loads
   */

  if (
    op >= 0x28 &&
    op <= 0x35
  ) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      text:
        `load opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      offset: o.value
    };
  }

  /*
   * Stores
   */

  if (
    op >= 0x36 &&
    op <= 0x3e
  ) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      text:
        `store opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      offset: o.value,
      isStore: true
    };
  }

  /*
   * Control flow
   */

  if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text:
        (op === 0x0c ? "br " : "br_if ") +
        x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {

    const n = readULEB(pos);

    pos = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;

    pos = readULEB(pos).next;

    return {
      start,
      end: pos,
      op,
      text: "br_table"
    };
  }

  /*
   * Memory
   */

  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {

    return {
      start,
      end: pos + 1,
      op,
      text:
        op === 0x3f ?
        "memory.size" :
        "memory.grow"
    };
  }

  /*
   * Everything else
   */

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text:
      `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Decode selected function
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function decodeFunction(body) {

  let p = body.start;

  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    p++;
  }

  const code = [];

  while (p < body.end) {

    const ins = decodeOne(p);

    code.push(ins);

    p = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }

  return code;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Find all offset=128 stores
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const results = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

  const code = decodeFunction(body);

  for (let i = 0; i < code.length; i++) {

    const ins = code[i];

    if (
      ins.isStore &&
      ins.offset === TARGET_OFFSET
    ) {

      results.push({
        func: body.func,
        index: i,
        code
      });
    }
  }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Output
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE OFFSET 128 PRODUCERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET OFFSET :", TARGET_OFFSET);
console.log("HITS :", results.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * First priority:
 *
 * functions close to the target dispatch region,
 * then older functions.
 */

results.sort((a, b) => {

  const priority = f => {

    if (f.func >= 8940)
      return 0;

    if (f.func >= 9000)
      return 1;

    return 2;
  };

  return priority(a) - priority(b) ||
         a.func - b.func ||
         a.index - b.index;
});

/*
 * Limit contextual output to 20 instructions
 * around each hit.
 */

for (const r of results) {

  const ins = r.code[r.index];

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    `FUNC ${r.func} @ ${hex(ins.start)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `STORE: ${ins.text}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const start = Math.max(0, r.index - 16);
  const end = Math.min(
    r.code.length,
    r.index + 2
  );

  let has2053 = false;

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {

    const x = r.code[i];

    let marker = "  ";

    if (i === r.index)
      marker = ">>";

    if (
      x.op === 0x41 &&
      x.value === 2053
    ) {
      marker = "!!";
      has2053 = true;
    }

    console.log(
      marker,
      hex(x.start),
      x.text
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "2053 IN LOCAL CONTEXT :",
    has2053 ? "YES" : "NO"
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceOffset4Stores.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    v += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, s);

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {

      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return {
        value: v | 0,
        next: p
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" +
    x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(p, end) {

  if (p >= end)
    return null;

  const start = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  const r = {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.local = a.value;

    r.text =
      op === 0x20
        ? `local.get local=${a.value}`
        : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set local=${a.value}`
          : `local.tee local=${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.callTarget = a.value;
    r.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isLoad = true;

    r.text =
      `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x29) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isLoad = true;

    r.text =
      `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isLoad = true;

    r.text =
      `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isStore = true;

    r.text =
      `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const a = sleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.value = a.value;

    r.text =
      `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    r.text = "i32.add";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    r.text = "i32.sub";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x72) {
    r.text = "i32.or";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x73) {
    r.text = "i32.xor";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x71) {
    r.text = "i32.and";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x74) {
    r.text = "i32.shl";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x75) {
    r.text = "i32.shr_s";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x76) {
    r.text = "i32.shr_u";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    r.text = "end";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    r.text = "else";
    return r;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {

    if (p >= end)
      return null;

    p++;

    r.end = p;

    r.text =
      op === 0x02
        ? "block"
        : op === 0x03
          ? "loop"
          : "if";

    return r;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;

    r.text =
      op === 0x0c
        ? `br ${a.value}`
        : `br_if ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  return r;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const section = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  if (!s)
    throw new Error("bad section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (section === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("code section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

const bodyCount = count.value;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE OFFSET +4 STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("BODY COUNT:", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET    : i32.store offset=4");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;
let localValueStores = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {

  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);
  if (!sizeInfo)
    break;

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  q = bodyEnd;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);
  if (!localInfo)
    continue;

  x = localInfo.next;

  for (
    let g = 0;
    g < localInfo.value;
    g++
  ) {

    const n = uleb(x);
    if (!n) {
      x = bodyEnd;
      break;
    }

    x = n.next + 1;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const z = decode(x, bodyEnd);

    if (!z)
      break;

    ins.push(z);
    x = z.end;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (
      !ins[i].isStore ||
      ins[i].offset !== 4
    )
      continue;

    total++;

    /*
     * In WASM stack order:
     *
     * address
     * value
     * i32.store
     *
     * Therefore inspect preceding instruction.
     */

    const valueIns = ins[i - 1];

    if (
      !valueIns ||
      !valueIns.text.startsWith("local.get")
    )
      continue;

    const valueLocal = valueIns.local;

    localValueStores++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("MATCH");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC :", func);
    console.log("STORE:", hex(ins[i].start));
    console.log("VALUE LOCAL:", valueLocal);

    console.log("");
    console.log("CONTEXT:");

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);

    for (let k = from; k <= i; k++) {

      console.log(
        " ",
        hex(ins[k].start),
        ins[k].text
      );
    }

    /*
     * Search backwards for the most recent definition
     * of the value local.
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("BACKWARD DEFINITION:");

    for (
      let k = i - 2;
      k >= Math.max(0, i - 100);
      k--
    ) {

      if (
        ins[k].local === valueLocal &&
        (
          ins[k].text.startsWith("local.set") ||
          ins[k].text.startsWith("local.tee")
        )
      ) {

        console.log(
          " ",
          hex(ins[k].start),
          ins[k].text
        );

        /*
         * Show instructions before definition.
         */

        const dfrom = Math.max(0, k - 8);

        for (let z = dfrom; z < k; z++) {

          console.log(
            "   ",
            hex(ins[z].start),
            ins[z].text
          );
        }

        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL +4 STORES       :", total);
console.log("LOCAL-DERIVED STORES  :", localValueStores);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceP28.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNC = 6618;
const START = 0x22ccba;
const END   = 0x22d0a1;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE MEM[0x28] -> FUNC 446 ARGUMENT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FUNC :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("TARGET MEM  : 0x28");
console.log("CALL 446    : 0x22d262");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;
    let x = 0;
    do {
        x = b[pos++];
        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;
    } while (x & 0x80);
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
}

// Find code section.
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = p;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = codeStart;

const countR = readULEB(p);
const count = countR.value;
p = countR.next;

let funcStart = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const bodyR = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodyR.value;
    p = bodyR.next;

    const bodyBegin = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const localsR = readULEB(p);
    const localGroups = localsR.value;
    p = localsR.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < localGroups; j++) {
        const n = readULEB(p).value;
        p += 1;
    }

    if (i === TARGET_FUNC - 83) {
        funcStart = bodyBegin;
        console.log("BODY START :", "0x" + bodyBegin.toString(16));
        console.log("BODY END   :", "0x" + bodyEnd.toString(16));
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!funcStart) throw new Error("FUNC 6618 NOT FOUND");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SEARCHING WRITES TO ADDRESS 0x28");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = funcStart; i < funcStart + (END - START); i++) {
    if (b[i] === 0x41) {
        const r = readULEB(i + 1);

        if (r.value === 0x28) {
            console.log(
                "i32.const 0x28 @",
                "0x" + i.toString(16)
            );
            hits++;
        }
    }

    // i32.store
    if (b[i] === 0x36) {
        const align = b[i + 1];
        const r = readULEB(i + 2);

        console.log(
            "i32.store @",
            "0x" + i.toString(16),
            "offset=" + r.value
        );
    }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CONST 0x28 HITS :", hits);
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000001n;

function opHex(body: any): string {
    try {
        if (body.bits.length < 32) return 'NO_OPCODE';
        return '0x' + body.beginParse().loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_ERROR';
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL TRACE AFTER PROVIDE_LP');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL   :', POOL.toString());
    console.log('ROUTER :', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('TARGET LT:', TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reading Pool transactions...');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(POOL, {
        limit: 50,
    });

    console.log('Transactions returned:', txs.length);
    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < txs.length; i++) {
        const tx: any = txs[i];

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt < TARGET_LT - 100000000n) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log('--------------------------------------------------------------');
        console.log('TX #' + (i + 1));
        console.log('LT:', lt.toString());
        console.log('HASH:', tx.hash().toString('base64'));

        const inMsg: any = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMsg) {
            console.log('No incoming message');
            continue;
        }

        if (inMsg.info?.type !== 'internal') {
            console.log('Incoming message is not internal');
            continue;
        }

        const src = inMsg.info.src;
        const value = inMsg.info.value?.coins ?? 0n;
        const body = inMsg.body;

        console.log('SOURCE :', src.toString());
        console.log('VALUE  :', value.toString());
        console.log('OPCODE :', opHex(body));

        if (src.equals(ROUTER)) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('*** ROUTER -> POOL MESSAGE FOUND ***');

            try {
                const boc = body.toBoc();

                console.log('BODY BOC BYTES :', boc.length);
                console.log('BODY BOC HEX   :', boc.toString('hex'));
                console.log('BODY BOC BASE64:');
                console.log(boc.toString('base64'));
            } catch (e) {
                console.log('BOC extraction error:', e);
            }

            console.log('');
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('TRACE FINISHED');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const URL =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

const TARGET_LTS = new Set([
    '99809465000008',
    '99809465000009',
    '99809465000010',
    '99809465000011',
    '99809465000012',
]);

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL LT TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('\nPOOL:');
    console.log(POOL);

    const response = await fetch(
        `${URL}?address=${POOL}&limit=20`
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
        console.log('\nHTTP ERROR:', response.status);
        console.log(await response.text());
        return;
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        console.log('\nTONCENTER ERROR:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(json, null, 2));
        return;
    }

    const transactions = json.result ?? [];

    console.log(
        `\nFetched ${transactions.length} transactions.`
    );

    const selected = transactions.filter((tx: any) =>
        TARGET_LTS.has(
            String(tx.transaction_id?.lt ?? '')
        )
    );

    selected.sort(
        (a: any, b: any) =>
            BigInt(a.transaction_id.lt) >
            BigInt(b.transaction_id.lt) ? 1 : -1
    );

    console.log(
        `Matched ${selected.length} target transactions.`
    );

    for (const tx of selected) {
        const lt = String(tx.transaction_id.lt);

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
        console.log(`LT: ${lt}`);
        console.log('HASH:', tx.transaction_id.hash);
        console.log('='.repeat(80));

        console.log('\nINCOMING');

        const input = tx.in_msg;

        if (!input) {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
        } else {
            console.log('Source:', input.source ?? 'null');
            console.log('Destination:', input.destination ?? 'null');
            console.log('Value:', input.value ?? '0');
            console.log('Type:', input.msg_type ?? '');

            const body =
                input.message_content?.body;

            console.log('BODY:');
            console.log(body ?? 'null');
        }

        console.log('\nOUTGOING');

        const outputs = tx.out_msgs ?? [];

        if (outputs.length === 0) {
            console.log('No outgoing messages.');
        }

        let i = 0;

        for (const msg of outputs) {
            i++;

            console.log(`\nOUT #${i}`);
            console.log('Source:', msg.source ?? 'null');
            console.log('Destination:', msg.destination ?? 'null');
            console.log('Value:', msg.value ?? '0');
            console.log('Type:', msg.msg_type ?? '');

            console.log('BODY:');
            console.log(
                msg.message_content?.body ?? 'null'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('TRACE COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
}
