import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE =
    '0x37c096df';

const ADD_LIQUIDITY_OPCODE =
    '0x50c6a654';

const POOL_TXS = [
    {
        name: 'POOL TX #6',
        hash: 'Aoam+QisUbVya0dK8c4cvZYNDte4V1m8+K08QpXayZU=',
        inMsgHash:
            'wOv69fdHU3O/g3rN+3mYEJvc/HRzq6xM+gmGyt9OB+Q=',
        outMsgHash:
            'frDBIfFjE82SSyMArnZCvttMz3Nh4ljg+g+m4MByvW8=',
    },
    {
        name: 'POOL TX #7',
        hash: 'ECvOzvsGYKdAlpGss+AHAkHaLUPKQb3TTbUKkAudYEw=',
        inMsgHash:
            'xOoEn8vtg71Crtx+pf9uLfeaaC0EgvEHd+d+mohK2GM=',
        outMsgHash:
            'iX0jSxgB4qXSTXX0h+Jz53XAyc/7WpwNOu8fEitjEic=',
    },
];

const DELAY_MS = 1300;

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function getJson(
    url: string,
    label: string
): Promise<any> {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt++) {

        const response = await fetch(url);

        if (response.ok) {
            return await response.json();
        }

        if (response.status === 429) {
            const wait =
                DELAY_MS * attempt;

            console.log(
                `429 for ${label}; waiting ${wait} ms...`
            );

            await sleep(wait);
            continue;
        }

        throw new Error(
            `${label}: HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    throw new Error(
        `${label}: too many 429 responses`
    );
}

function txUrlByMessage(
    msgHash: string,
    direction: 'in' | 'out'
): string {

    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    params.set('msg_hash', msgHash);
    params.set('direction', direction);
    params.set('limit', '10');
    params.set('offset', '0');

    return `${TONCENTER}/transactionsByMessage?${params}`;
}

function printTx(
    tx: any,
    label: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `----- ${label} -----`
    );

    console.log(
        'account:',
        tx.account ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'tx hash:',
        tx.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'lt:',
        tx.lt ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'trace_id:',
        tx.trace_id ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'total_fees:',
        tx.total_fees ?? 'N/A'
    );

    if (tx.description?.compute_ph) {
        console.log(
            'compute success:',
            tx.description.compute_ph.success
        );

        console.log(
            'exit_code:',
            tx.description.compute_ph.exit_code
        );
    }

    if (tx.in_msg) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('IN MESSAGE');

        console.log(
            'hash:',
            tx.in_msg.hash ?? 'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'source:',
            tx.in_msg.source ?? 'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'destination:',
            tx.in_msg.destination ?? 'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'opcode:',
            tx.in_msg.opcode ??
            tx.in_msg.decoded_opcode ??
            'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'decoded_opcode:',
            tx.in_msg.decoded_opcode ?? 'N/A'
        );
    }

    if (Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'OUT MESSAGES:',
            tx.out_msgs.length
        );

        for (let i = 0;
             i < tx.out_msgs.length;
             i++) {

            const msg = tx.out_msgs[i];

            console.log(
                `OUT #${i + 1}`
            );

            console.log(
                '  hash:',
                msg.hash ?? 'N/A'
            );

            console.log(
                '  source:',
                msg.source ?? 'N/A'
            );

            console.log(
                '  destination:',
                msg.destination ?? 'N/A'
            );

            console.log(
                '  opcode:',
                msg.opcode ??
                msg.decoded_opcode ??
                'N/A'
            );

            console.log(
                '  decoded:',
                msg.decoded_opcode ?? 'N/A'
            );
        }
    }
}

async function traceMessage(
    msgHash: string,
    name: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `================================================`
    );

    console.log(
        `${name}`
    );

    console.log(
        `message hash: ${msgHash}`
    );

    console.log(
        `================================================`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Looking for IN transaction...'
    );

    const inData =
        await getJson(
            txUrlByMessage(msgHash, 'in'),
            `${name} IN`
        );

    const inTxs =
        inData.transactions ?? [];

    console.log(
        'IN matches:',
        inTxs.length
    );

    for (const tx of inTxs) {
        printTx(
            tx,
            `${name} / IN`
        );
    }

    await sleep(DELAY_MS);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Looking for OUT transaction...'
    );

    const outData =
        await getJson(
            txUrlByMessage(msgHash, 'out'),
            `${name} OUT`
        );

    const outTxs =
        outData.transactions ?? [];

    console.log(
        'OUT matches:',
        outTxs.length
    );

    for (const tx of outTxs) {
        printTx(
            tx,
            `${name} / OUT`
        );
    }

    return {
        inTxs,
        outTxs,
    };
}

    console.log(

        PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    );

    console.log(
        'add_liquidity opcode:',
        ADD_LIQUIDITY_OPCODE
    );

    for (const item of POOL_TXS) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '################################################'

        console.log(
            item.name
        );

        console.log(
            'Pool transaction:',

        );

        console.log(

        await traceMessage(

        );

        await sleep(DELAY_MS);

        console.log('');

        await traceMessage(

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceReads19534c.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE ALL READS OF 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 10; p++) {

  if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

  const c = uleb(p + 1);

  if (c.value !== TARGET) continue;

  const from = Math.max(0, p - 16);
  const to = Math.min(wasm.length, p + 45);

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CONST : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RANGE : 0x${from.toString(16)} .. 0x${to.toString(16)}`);

  let q = from;

  while (q < to) {

    const pos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let imm = "";

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x10 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x41 ||
      op === 0x42
    ) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      imm = ` ${x.value}`;
      q = x.next;
    }
    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.next;

      imm = ` align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`;
    }

    console.log(
      `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${imm}`
    );
  }

  hits++;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL READ LOCATIONS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 122;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += sz.value;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {

  const bs = uleb(q);
  q = bs.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + bs.value;
  const realFunc = IMPORTS + body;

  if (realFunc !== TARGET) {
    q = end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("REAL FUNC 122");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`BODY INDEX : ${body}`);
  console.log(`BODY START : 0x${start.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`BODY END   : 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`BODY SIZE  : ${bs.value}`);
  console.log("");
  console.log("CALLS:");

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const r = uleb(i + 1);

    console.log(
      `  @ 0x${i.toString(16)} -> FUNC ${r.value}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("RAW:");
  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
    console.log(
      `0x${i.toString(16)}  ${wasm[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  break;
  
  q = end;
}

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x8e7d4;

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;
const count = u();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC CONTAINING 0x8E7D4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
  const bodySize = u();
  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const groups = u();

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  if (TARGET >= bodyStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {
    console.log("FUNC :", f + 91);
    console.log("BODY :", "0x" + bodyStart.toString(16),
                         ".. 0x" + bodyEnd.toString(16));
    console.log("TARGET:", "0x" + TARGET.toString(16));
    console.log("");

    const s = Math.max(bodyStart, TARGET - 80);
    const e = Math.min(bodyEnd, TARGET + 120);

    for (let q = s; q < e; q += 16) {
      const z = Math.min(e, q + 16);

      console.log(
        "0x" + q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        [...b.slice(q, z)]
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }

    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceRefField946x.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

const TARGETS = new Set([9368, 9462, 9467, 9472]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(type) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionType = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionType === type)
            return { start: p, end: p + size };

        p += size;
    }

    return null;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
    let p = start;

    const locals = uleb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    let localCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        p++; // valtype

        localCount += n.value;
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = sleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2a: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `f32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2b: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `f64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2c:
            case 0x2d: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `${op === 0x2c ? "i32.load8_s" : "i32.load8_u"} align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2e:
            case 0x2f: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `${op === 0x2e ? "i32.load16_s" : "i32.load16_u"} align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x3a:
            case 0x3b: {
                const align = uleb(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = uleb(p);
                p = off.next;

                text = `${op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" : "i32.store16"} align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const type = uleb(p);
                p = type.next;

                const table = uleb(p);
                p = table.next;

                text = `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x71:
                text = "i32.and";
                break;

            case 0x72:
                text = "i32.or";
                break;

            case 0x73:
                text = "i32.xor";
                break;

            case 0x04:
                text = "if";
                break;

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x02:
            case 0x03: {
                const blockType = wasm[p++];
                text = `${op === 0x02 ? "block" : "loop"} type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                break;
            }

            default:
                // Keep unknown opcode, but stop decoding operands.
                break;
        }

        instructions.push({
            addr,
            op,
            text
        });

        if (op === 0x0b && p === end)
            break;
    }

    return instructions;
}

function findCodeSection() {
    const sec = findSection(10);
    if (!sec) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        bodies.push({
            bodyIndex: i,
            globalFunc: IMPORT_COUNT + i,
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex(x) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const bodies = findCodeSection();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE +4 FIELD POINTER SOURCES : 9368 / 9462 / 9467 / 9472");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("FUNCTION BODIES :", bodies.length);
console.log("IMPORT COUNT :", IMPORT_COUNT);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const body of bodies) {

    if (!TARGETS.has(body.globalFunc))
        continue;

    const ins = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${body.globalFunc} BODY ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
    );
    console.log("BODY INDEX :", body.bodyIndex);
    console.log("INSTRUCTIONS :", ins.length);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        const x = ins[i];

        /*
         * Find:
         *
         * local.get N
         * i32.load offset=4
         *
         * This is the pointer source feeding the +4 field.
         */
        if (
            x.text.startsWith("local.get ") &&
            i + 1 < ins.length &&
            ins[i + 1].text.includes("i32.load") &&
            ins[i + 1].text.includes("offset=4")
        ) {
            const local = x.text.split(" ")[1];

            console.log("");
            console.log("FIELD +4 READ");
            console.log("ADDRESS :", hex(x.addr));
            console.log("LOCAL   :", local);
            console.log("LOAD    :", ins[i + 1].text);

            const a = Math.max(0, i - 6);
            const b = Math.min(ins.length, i + 8);

            for (let j = a; j < b; j++) {

                const mark = j === i ? ">>" : "  ";

                console.log(
                    `${mark} ${hex(ins[j].addr)} ${ins[j].text}`
                );
            }

            /*
             * Look backwards for the nearest definition of this local.
             */
            let foundDef = false;

            for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0; j--) {

                const t = ins[j].text;

                if (
                    t === `local.set ${local}` ||
                    t === `local.tee ${local}`
                ) {
                    console.log("");
                    console.log("NEAREST LOCAL DEFINITION");
                    console.log("LOCAL :", local);
                    console.log("AT    :", hex(ins[j].addr));
                    console.log("OP    :", t);

                    const a2 = Math.max(0, j - 5);
                    const b2 = Math.min(ins.length, j + 3);

                    for (let k = a2; k < b2; k++) {
                        const mark = k === j ? ">>" : "  ";
                        console.log(
                            `${mark} ${hex(ins[k].addr)} ${ins[k].text}`
                        );
                    }

                    foundDef = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!foundDef) {
                console.log("NEAREST LOCAL DEFINITION : NOT FOUND");
            }
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("TARGETS : 9368, 9462, 9467, 9472");
console.log("FOCUS   : SOURCE OF POINTER USED BY FIELD +4");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const TARGET_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;

const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_JETTON_NOTIFY = 0x7362d09c;
const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    separator('CURRENT v2.2 ROOSTER LEG TRACE');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET ROOSTER AMOUNT:');
    console.log(TARGET_AMOUNT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        ROOSTER_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS FETCHED: ${txs.length}`);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        if (!tx.inMessage) {
            continue;
        }

        const body = tx.inMessage.body;
        const op = getOpcode(body);

        if (op !== OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const s = body.beginParse();

            s.loadUint(32);

            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

            const amount = s.loadCoins();

            if (amount !== TARGET_AMOUNT) {
                continue;
            }

            found++;

            separator('MATCHING ROOSTER TRANSFER');

            console.log('LT:');
            console.log(tx.lt.toString());

            console.log('HASH:');
            console.log(
                tx.hash().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('TIME:');
            console.log(
                new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
            );

            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opHex(op));

            console.log('QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            console.log('ROOSTER AMOUNT RAW:');
            console.log(amount.toString());

            console.log('ROOSTER AMOUNT:');
            console.log(ton(amount));

            if (tx.inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('TON VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    ton(tx.inMessage.info.value.coins)
                );
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER AMOUNT:');
            console.log(s.remainingBits);

            console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
            console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            console.log('');
            console.log('FULL TRANSFER BODY BOC:');
            console.log(
                body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('');

            if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
                console.log('PAYLOAD PARSE:');

                try {
                    const customPayload = s.loadMaybeRef();

                    if (customPayload) {
                        console.log(
                            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD REF: YES'
                        );

                        console.log(
                            customPayload
                                .toBoc()
                                .toString('base64')
                        );
                    } else {
                        console.log(
                            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD REF: NO'
                        );
                    }
                } catch {
                    console.log(
                        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD: DECODE FAILED'
                    );
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('TRANSFER DECODE ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    separator('RESULT');

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log(
            'TARGET ROOSTER TRANSFER NOT FOUND.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `MATCHES FOUND: ${found}`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');

    separator('END');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_CREATED_LT = '99491281000002';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

async function getTransactions(
    client: TonClient,
    address: Address,
    limit = 20
) {
    return await client.getTransactions(address, {
        limit,
    });
}

function opcodeFromBody(body: string): string {
    try {
        const raw = Buffer.from(body, 'base64');

        if (raw.length < 4) {
            return 'N/A';
        }

        return '0x' + raw.readUInt32BE(0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'N/A';
    }
}

function printMessage(label: string, msg: any) {
    if (!msg) {
        return;
    }

    const body = msg.msg_data?.body ?? '';

    console.log('');
    console.log(`----- ${label} -----`);

    console.log('HASH:');
    console.log(msg.hash);

    console.log('SOURCE:');
    console.log(msg.source || '(external)');

    console.log('DESTINATION:');
    console.log(msg.destination);

    console.log('VALUE:');
    console.log(msg.value);

    console.log('CREATED LT:');
    console.log(msg.created_lt);

    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(opcodeFromBody(body));

    console.log('BODY LENGTH:');
    console.log(body.length);

    if (body) {
        console.log('BODY BOC:');
        console.log(body);
    }
}

function inspectTransactions(
    title: string,
    transactions: any[],
    sourceAddress?: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(title);
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRANSACTIONS:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx: any = transactions[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`################ TRANSACTION ${i + 1} ################`);

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt);

        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash);

        console.log('UTIME:');
        console.log(tx.now);

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            const msg: any = tx.inMessage;

            if (
                !sourceAddress ||
                msg.info?.src?.toString?.() === sourceAddress
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('IN MESSAGE:');
                console.log(
                    JSON.stringify(
                        {
                            source: msg.info?.src?.toString?.(),
                            destination: msg.info?.dest?.toString?.(),
                            value: msg.info?.value?.coins?.toString?.(),
                            body:
                                msg.body?.toBoc?.().toString('base64') ??
                                '',
                        },
                        null,
                        2
                    )
                );
            }
        }

        if (tx.outMessages) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('OUT MESSAGE COUNT:');
            console.log(tx.outMessages.size);

            let n = 0;

            for (const [, msg] of tx.outMessages) {
                n++;

                console.log('');
                console.log(`OUT MESSAGE ${n}:`);

                console.log(
                    JSON.stringify(
                        {
                            destination:
                                msg.info?.dest?.toString?.(),
                            value:
                                msg.info?.value?.coins?.toString?.(),
                            body:
                                msg.body?.toBoc?.().toString('base64') ??
                                '',
                        },
                        null,
                        2
                    )
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER -> POOL TRACE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET CREATED LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_CREATED_LT);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER,
    });

    // ============================================================
    // 1. ROUTER
    // ============================================================

    const routerTxs = await getTransactions(client, ROUTER, 20);

    inspectTransactions(
        '1. ROUTER RECENT TRANSACTIONS',
        routerTxs
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' SEARCHING ROUTER FOR TARGET CREATED LT');
    console.log('================================================');

    let routerMatch: any = null;

    for (const tx of routerTxs as any[]) {
        const inMsg: any = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMsg) {
            continue;
        }

        const createdLt =
            inMsg.info?.createdLt?.toString?.() ??
            '';

        if (createdLt === TARGET_CREATED_LT) {
            routerMatch = tx;

            console.log('');
            console.log('*** ROUTER TARGET FOUND ***');

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROUTER TX LT:');
            console.log(tx.lt);

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROUTER TX HASH:');
            console.log(tx.hash);

            console.log('');
            console.log('IN MESSAGE SOURCE:');
            console.log(inMsg.info?.src?.toString?.());

            console.log('');
            console.log('IN MESSAGE DESTINATION:');
            console.log(inMsg.info?.dest?.toString?.());

            console.log('');
            console.log('IN MESSAGE VALUE:');
            console.log(
                inMsg.info?.value?.coins?.toString?.()
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('IN MESSAGE BODY:');
            console.log(
                inMsg.body?.toBoc?.().toString('base64')
            );

            break;
        }
    }

    if (!routerMatch) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'ROUTER TARGET NOT FOUND IN LATEST 20 TRANSACTIONS.'
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 2. POOL
    // ============================================================

    const poolTxs = await getTransactions(client, POOL, 30);

    inspectTransactions(
        '2. POOL RECENT TRANSACTIONS',
        poolTxs
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 3. ROOSTER WALLET
    // ============================================================

    const roosterTxs =
        await getTransactions(client, ROOSTER_WALLET, 20);

    inspectTransactions(
        '3. ROOSTER JETTON WALLET RECENT TRANSACTIONS',
        roosterTxs
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 4. SUMMARY
    // ============================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' TRACE SUMMARY');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        routerMatch
            ? 'ROUTER MESSAGE: FOUND'
            : 'ROUTER MESSAGE: NOT FOUND'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET:');
    console.log(
        '99491281000002 -> ROUTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceRuntimeWriters.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    {
        name: "Function 3621 dispatch memory",
        address: 2428392,
        hex: "0x00250de8",
    },
    {
        name: "Function 3658 root pointer",
        address: 1837608,
        hex: "0x001c0a28",
    },
];

const TARGET_INDEX = 7969;

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    next: number;
};

type Body = {
    func: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    codeStart: number;
};

function readULEB(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB EOF");
}

function readSLEB(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (-1 << shift);

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findSection(
    wasm: Buffer,
    idWanted: number
): {
    payloadStart: number;
    payloadEnd: number;
} | null {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const size = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const start = size.next;
        const end = start + size.value;

        if (id === idWanted)
            return {
                payloadStart: start,
                payloadEnd: end,
            };

        p = end;
    }

    return null;
}

function parseBodies(wasm: Buffer): Body[] {
    const sec = findSection(wasm, 10);

    if (!sec)
        throw new Error("Code section not found");

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: Body[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);

        const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

        let q = bodyStart;

        const localInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
        q = localInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localInfo.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = n.next;

            // valtype
            q++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: q,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decodeOne(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number,
    end: number
): Instr | null {
    if (p >= end)
        return null;

    const start = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // i64.const
    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // global.get
    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // global.set
    if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // i32.load / variants
    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2d ||
        op === 0x2e ||
        op === 0x2f
    ) {
        if (p >= end)
            return null;

        const align = wasm[p++];

        const off = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = off.next;

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `load opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${align} offset=${off.value}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    // i32.store / store8 / store16
    if (
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b
    ) {
        if (p >= end)
            return null;

        const align = wasm[p++];

        const off = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = off.next;

        const name =
            op === 0x36
                ? "i32.store"
                : op === 0x3a
                    ? "i32.store8"
                    : "i32.store16";

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `${name} align=${align} offset=${off.value}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    // i64.store
    if (op === 0x37) {
        if (p >= end)
            return null;

        const align = wasm[p++];

        const off = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = off.next;

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `i64.store align=${align} offset=${off.value}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    // memory.copy
    if (op === 0xfc) {
        if (p >= end)
            return null;

        const sub = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = sub.next;

        if (sub.value === 10) {
            const a = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = b.next;

            return {
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: `memory.copy dstmem=${a.value} srcmem=${b.value}`,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `0xfc sub=${sub.value}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(wasm, p);

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            next: x.next,
        };
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
        const type = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = type.next;

        const table = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = table.next;

        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    // common zero-operand instructions
    const zeroOps = new Set([
        0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04,
        0x05, 0x0b, 0x0f, 0x1a, 0x1b,
        0x45, 0x46, 0x47, 0x48, 0x49,
        0x4a, 0x4b, 0x4c, 0x4d, 0x4e,
        0x4f, 0x50, 0x51, 0x52, 0x53,
        0x54, 0x55, 0x56, 0x57, 0x58,
        0x59, 0x5a, 0x5b, 0x5c, 0x5d,
        0x5e, 0x5f, 0x60, 0x61, 0x62,
        0x63, 0x64, 0x65, 0x66, 0x67,
        0x68, 0x69, 0x6a, 0x6b, 0x6c,
        0x6d, 0x6e, 0x6f, 0x70, 0x71,
        0x72, 0x73, 0x74, 0x75, 0x76,
        0x77, 0x78, 0x79, 0x7a, 0x7b,
        0x7c, 0x7d, 0x7e, 0x7f, 0x80,
        0x81, 0x82, 0x83, 0x84, 0x85,
        0x86, 0x87, 0x88, 0x89, 0x8a,
        0x8b, 0x8c, 0x8d, 0x8e, 0x8f,
        0x90, 0x91, 0x92, 0x93, 0x94,
        0x95, 0x96, 0x97, 0x98, 0x99,
        0x9a, 0x9b, 0x9c, 0x9d, 0x9e,
        0x9f, 0xa0, 0xa1, 0xa2, 0xa3,
        0xa4, 0xa5, 0xa6, 0xa7, 0xa8,
        0xa9, 0xaa, 0xab, 0xac, 0xad,
        0xae, 0xaf, 0xb0, 0xb1, 0xb2,
        0xb3, 0xb4, 0xb5, 0xb6, 0xb7,
        0xb8, 0xb9, 0xba, 0xbb, 0xbc,
        0xbd, 0xbe, 0xbf, 0xc0, 0xc1,
        0xc2, 0xc3, 0xc4, 0xc5, 0xc6,
        0xc7, 0xc8, 0xc9, 0xca, 0xcb,
        0xcc, 0xcd, 0xce, 0xcf, 0xd0,
        0xd1, 0xd2, 0xd3, 0xd4, 0xd5,
        0xd6, 0xd7, 0xd8, 0xd9, 0xda,
        0xdb, 0xdc, 0xdd, 0xde, 0xdf,
        0xe0, 0xe1, 0xe2, 0xe3, 0xe4,
        0xe5, 0xe6, 0xe7, 0xe8, 0xe9,
        0xea, 0xeb, 0xec, 0xed, 0xee,
        0xef, 0xf0, 0xf1, 0xf2, 0xf3,
        0xf4, 0xf5, 0xf6, 0xf7, 0xf8,
        0xf9, 0xfa, 0xfb,
    ]);

    if (zeroOps.has(op)) {
        return {
            offset: start,
            opcode: op,
            text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`,
            next: p,
        };
    }

    return {
        offset: start,
        opcode: op,
        text: `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16)}`,
        next: p,
    };
}

function decodeWindow(
    wasm: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number,
    maxInstr = 40
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];

    let p = start;

    for (let i = 0; i < maxInstr && p < end; i++) {
        const ins = decodeOne(wasm, p, end);

        if (!ins)
            break;

        out.push(ins);

        p = ins.next;

        if (
            ins.opcode === 0x0b ||
            ins.opcode === 0x0f
        )
            break;
    }

    return out;
}

function isStore(op: number): boolean {
    return (
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39 ||
        op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b
    );
}

function findConstHits(
    wasm: Buffer,
    bodies: Body[],
    target: number
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` SEARCH ADDRESS ${hex(target)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    let total = 0;
    let possibleWriters = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const insns = decodeWindow(
            wasm,
            body.codeStart,
            body.bodyEnd,
            100000
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < insns.length; i++) {
            const ins = insns[i];

            if (
                ins.opcode !== 0x41 ||
                !ins.text.startsWith("i32.const ")
            )
                continue;

            const value = Number(
                ins.text.substring("i32.const ".length)
            );

            if ((value >>> 0) !== (target >>> 0))
                continue;

            total++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `Function ${body.func} @ ${hex(ins.offset)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  ${ins.text}`
            );

            const window = insns.slice(i, i + 20);

            for (const x of window) {
                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.text}`
                );

                if (isStore(x.opcode)) {
                    possibleWriters++;
                    console.log(
                        "  >>> POSSIBLE STORE AFTER TARGET ADDRESS"
                    );
                    break;
                }

                if (
                    x.opcode === 0x0b ||
                    x.opcode === 0x0f
                )
                    break;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("SUMMARY");
    console.log(`  CONST hits          : ${total}`);
    console.log(`  possible store paths: ${possibleWriters}`);
}

function searchRawAddressBytes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    target: number
) {
    const b = Buffer.alloc(4);

    b.writeUInt32LE(target >>> 0, 0);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` RAW LITTLE-ENDIAN SEARCH ${hex(target)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `bytes: ${[...b]
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")}`
    );

    let hits = 0;

    for (let p = 0; p <= wasm.length - 4; p++) {
        if (
            wasm[p] === b[0] &&
            wasm[p + 1] === b[1] &&
            wasm[p + 2] === b[2] &&
            wasm[p + 3] === b[3]
        ) {
            hits++;

            if (hits <= 50) {
                console.log(
                    `  raw hit @ ${hex(p)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(`Total raw hits: ${hits}`);
}

function searchTargetIndex(wasm: Buffer) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` RAW SEARCH TABLE INDEX ${TARGET_INDEX}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const b = Buffer.alloc(4);
    b.writeUInt32LE(TARGET_INDEX >>> 0, 0);

    console.log(
        `i32 bytes: ${[...b]
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")}`
    );

    let hits = 0;

    for (let p = 0; p <= wasm.length - 4; p++) {
        if (
            wasm[p] === b[0] &&
            wasm[p + 1] === b[1] &&
            wasm[p + 2] === b[2] &&
            wasm[p + 3] === b[3]
        ) {
            hits++;

            if (hits <= 80) {
                console.log(
                    `  raw hit @ ${hex(p)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(`Total raw hits: ${hits}`);
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RUNTIME DISPATCH WRITER TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const bodies = parseBodies(wasm);

    console.log(
        `Defined functions : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Function range    : ${bodies[0].func} .. ${bodies.at(-1)!.func}`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `Target Function 4815 table index : ${TARGET_INDEX}`
    );

    for (const t of TARGETS) {
        console.log("");

            `${t.name}`
        );
        console.log(
            `  decimal : ${t.address}`
        );
        console.log(
            `  hex     : ${t.hex}`
        );

        findConstHits(
            wasm,
            bodies,
            t.address
        );

        searchRawAddressBytes(
            wasm,
            t.address
        );
    }

    searchTargetIndex(wasm);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function sections() {
  let p = 8, out = [];
  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;
    out.push({ id, start:p, end:p+z.value });
    p += z.value;
  }
  return out;
}

const sec = sections().find(x => x.id === 10);
let p = sec.start;

const n = uleb(p);
p = n.next;

const funcs = [];

for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.next;

  funcs.push({
    func: i + 83,
    start: p,
    end: p + z.value
  });

  p += z.value;
}

function targetConst(q) {
  if (b[q] !== 0x41) return false;
  const x = sleb(q + 1);
  return (x.value >>> 0) === TARGET;
}

function localIndex(q) {
  const x = uleb(q + 1);
  return { index:x.value, next:x.next };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("S1 ADDRESS ALIAS TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET : 0x19534c");
console.log("");

let found = 0;

for (const fn of funcs) {

  for (let q = fn.start; q < fn.end; q++) {

    if (!targetConst(q)) continue;

    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${fn.func}`);
    console.log(
      `TARGET CONST @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log("############################################################");

    // inspect next 80 bytes
    let end = Math.min(fn.end, q + 100);

    for (let x = q; x < end; x++) {

      // local.set
      if (b[x] === 0x21) {
        const l = localIndex(x);
        console.log(
          `0x${x.toString(16)}  local.set L${l.index}`
        );
        x = l.next - 1;
        continue;
      }

      // local.tee
      if (b[x] === 0x22) {
        const l = localIndex(x);
        console.log(
          `0x${x.toString(16)}  local.tee L${l.index}`
        );
        x = l.next - 1;
        continue;
      }

      // local.get
      if (b[x] === 0x20) {
        const l = localIndex(x);
        console.log(
          `0x${x.toString(16)}  local.get L${l.index}`
        );
        x = l.next - 1;
        continue;
      }

      // i32.store
      if (b[x] === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(x+1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        console.log(
          `0x${x.toString(16)}  i32.store ` +
          `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        );

        x = o.next - 1;
        continue;
      }

      // i32.load
      if (b[x] === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(x+1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        console.log(
          `0x${x.toString(16)}  i32.load ` +
          `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        );

        x = o.next - 1;
        continue;
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    found++;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ALIAS SITES : ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const SEG412_ADDR = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_SIZE = 14145;

const SEG413_ADDR = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_SIZE = 18810;

const SEG412_FILE = 0x004a6695;

const TARGETS = [2053, 2101, 2612];

const SEG412_END = SEG412_ADDR + SEG412_SIZE;
const SEG413_END = SEG413_ADDR + SEG413_SIZE;

function u32(p: number): number {
  return (
    (wasm[p] |
      (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
      (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
      (wasm[p + 3] << 24)) >>>
    0
  );
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function runtimeToFile(addr: number): number {
  return SEG412_FILE + (addr - SEG412_ADDR);
}

function classify(v: number): string {
  if (v >= SEG412_ADDR && v < SEG412_END) {
    return "SEG412_PTR";
  }

  if (v >= SEG413_ADDR && v < SEG413_END) {
    return "SEG413_PTR";
  }

  if (TARGETS.includes(v)) {
    return "TARGET_INDEX";
  }

  return "";
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`SEG412 runtime : ${hex(SEG412_ADDR)} .. ${hex(SEG412_END - 1)}`);
console.log(`SEG412 file    : ${hex(SEG412_FILE)} .. ${hex(SEG412_FILE + SEG412_SIZE - 1)}`);
console.log(`SEG413 runtime : ${hex(SEG413_ADDR)} .. ${hex(SEG413_END - 1)}`);
console.log("");

console.log("TARGET RESOLUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (let addr = SEG412_ADDR; addr <= SEG412_END - 4; addr += 4) {
    const file = runtimeToFile(addr);

    if (file + 4 > wasm.length) continue;

    if (u32(file) === target) {
      hits.push(addr);
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`TARGET ${target}`);

  if (hits.length === 0) {
    console.log("  NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  for (const addr of hits) {
    const file = runtimeToFile(addr);

    console.log(
      `  runtime : ${hex(addr)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  file    : ${hex(file)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");

let count412 = 0;
let count413 = 0;

for (let addr = SEG412_ADDR; addr <= SEG412_END - 4; addr += 4) {
  const file = runtimeToFile(addr);
  const value = u32(file);

  const type = classify(value);

  if (!type) continue;

  if (type === "SEG412_PTR") count412++;
  if (type === "SEG413_PTR") count413++;

  console.log(
    `${hex(addr)}  file=${hex(file)}  value=${hex(value)}  ${type}`
  );

console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const locations: number[] = [];

  for (let addr = SEG412_ADDR; addr <= SEG412_END - 4; addr += 4) {
    const file = runtimeToFile(addr);

    if (u32(file) === target) {
      locations.push(addr);
    }
  }

  for (const targetAddr of locations) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(`TARGET ${target} @ ${hex(targetAddr)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const start = Math.max(
      SEG412_ADDR,
      targetAddr - 32
    );

    const end = Math.min(
      SEG412_END - 4,
      targetAddr + 96
    );

    for (let addr = start; addr <= end; addr += 4) {
      const file = runtimeToFile(addr);
      const value = u32(file);

      let mark = "";

      if (addr === targetAddr) {
        mark += " <<< TARGET";
      }

      const type = classify(value);

      if (type) {
        mark += ` <<< ${type}`;
      }

      console.log(
        `${hex(addr)}  file=${hex(file)}  ${hex(value)}${mark}`
      );
    }
  }
}

FILE: scripts\traceSegment413ToDispatch.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [2053, "Function 8952"],
    [2101, "Function 8927"],
    [2612, "Function 8637"]
]);

const ROOTS = [
    0x0018efc4,
    0x0018eec4,
    0x0018ee88,
    0x0018efdc,
    0x0018f068,
    0x0018ef50,
    0x0018ef38,
    0x0018acbc
];

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readU32(addr: number) {
    if (addr < 0 || addr + 4 > memory.length)
        return null;

    return memory.readUInt32LE(addr);
}

/*
 * Locate data section.
 */
let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!dataStart)
    throw new Error("Data section not found");

/*
 * Build linear memory.
 */
const memory = Buffer.alloc(0x02000000);

p = dataStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

type Segment = {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    size: number;
    fileStart: number;
};

const segments: Segment[] = [];

for (let id = 0; id < count.value; id++) {

    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;

    let memStart = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        memStart = off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

    } else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const memidx = uleb(p);
        p = memidx.next;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        memStart = off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

    } else if (flags.value === 1) {

        const sz = uleb(p);
        p = sz.next + sz.value;

        continue;

    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported data flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const fileStart = p;

    if (memStart + sz.value <= memory.length) {

        wasm.copy(
            memory,
            memStart,
            fileStart,
            fileStart + sz.value
        );
    }

    segments.push({
        id,
        start: memStart,
        size: sz.value,
        fileStart
    });

    p += sz.value;
}

function segmentOf(addr: number) {

    return segments.find(
        s =>
            addr >= s.start &&
            addr < s.start + s.size
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SEGMENT 413 → DISPATCH POINTER GRAPH");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(
    `Segment 412 : ${hex(SEG412_START)} .. ${hex(SEG412_END)}`

console.log("============================================================");

    addr += 4

    const v = readU32(addr);

    if (
        v !== null &&
        v >= SEG412_START &&
        v < SEG412_END
    ) {

        direct++;

        console.log(
            `${hex(addr)} -> ${hex(v)} ` +
            `(relative 412 = ${hex(v - SEG412_START)})`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log(`TOTAL DIRECT POINTERS = ${direct}`);
console.log();

/*
 * 2. Find every occurrence of target indices
 *    inside Segment 413.
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET INDICES INSIDE SEGMENT 413");
console.log("============================================================");

for (
    let addr = SEG413_START;
    addr + 4 <= SEG413_END;
    addr += 4
) {

    const v = readU32(addr);

    if (v !== null && TARGETS.has(v)) {

        console.log(
            `${hex(addr)} -> ${v} ` +
            `${TARGETS.get(v)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();

/*
 * 3. For each known static root, follow up to 4 pointer levels.
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ROOT POINTER CHAINS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const root of ROOTS) {

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `ROOT ${hex(root)}`
    );

    let cur = root;

    for (let depth = 0; depth < 5; depth++) {

        const value = readU32(cur);

        if (value === null) {
            console.log(
                `  depth ${depth}: invalid`
            );
            break;
        }

        const seg = segmentOf(value);

        let tag = "";

        if (
            value >= SEG412_START &&
            value < SEG412_END
        ) {
            tag =
                "  *** INTO SEGMENT 412 ***";
        }

        if (
            value >= SEG413_START &&
            value < SEG413_END
        ) {
            tag =
                "  -> Segment 413";
        }

        if (TARGETS.has(value)) {
            tag =
                `  *** TARGET ${TARGETS.get(value)} ***`;
        }

        console.log(
            `  depth ${depth}: ` +
            `${hex(cur)} -> ${value} (${hex(value)})${tag}`
        );

        /*
         * Stop on function indices / small integers.
         */
        if (
            value < 0x10000 &&
            !TARGETS.has(value)
        ) {
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Continue only when value is a valid memory pointer.
         */
        if (!seg)
            break;

        cur = value;
    }
}

/*
 * 4. Search Segment 413 for pointers that land
 *    immediately around the three target records.
 */
console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("POINTERS NEAR TARGET RECORDS");
console.log("============================================================");

const targetAddresses = [
    0x00189dec,
    0x00189f9c,
    0x0018a8b0
];

for (const target of targetAddresses) {

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET RECORD ${hex(target)}`
    );

    for (
        let addr = SEG413_START;
        addr + 4 <= SEG413_END;
        addr += 4
    ) {

        const v = readU32(addr);

        if (v === null)
            continue;

        /*
         * Look for exact target and nearby record addresses.
         */
        if (
            v >= target - 0x40 &&
            v <= target + 0x40
        ) {

            console.log(
                `${hex(addr)} -> ${hex(v)} ` +
                `(delta ${v - target})`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*

 *    For every pointer in Segment 413, show if
 *    it points into the three target neighborhoods.
 */
console.log();

    let hits = 0;

    for (

        addr + 4 <= SEG413_END;

            v >= target &&

        ) {

            hits++;

            console.log(

    console.log(

    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSegment669ConsumersV22.ts

import fs from "fs";

function hx(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function uleb(n: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    while (n >= 0x80) {
        out.push((n & 0x7f) | 0x80);
        n >>>= 7;
    }

    out.push(n);
    return out;
}

function findAll(buf: Buffer, pattern: Buffer): number[] {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i <= buf.length - pattern.length; i++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (ok) hits.push(i);
    }

    return hits;
}

function dump(buf: Buffer, pos: number, radius = 96) {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
    const end = Math.min(buf.length, pos + radius + 16);

    console.log(
        `\n--- context @ 0x${hx(pos)} ---`
    );

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < 16 && p + j < end; j++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[p + j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${hx(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

function isFunctionBodyStart(buf: Buffer, pos: number): boolean {
    // Typical beginning of a WASM function body:
    // local decl count followed by local declarations.
    //
    // We don't assume this is definitive. This helper only
    // detects nearby structural candidates.

    if (pos < 1 || pos >= buf.length) return false;

    const b = buf[pos];

    return (
        b === 0x00 ||
        b === 0x01 ||
        b === 0x02 ||
        b === 0x03 ||
        b === 0x04 ||
        b === 0x05 ||
        b === 0x06 ||
        b === 0x07 ||
        b === 0x08
    );
}

function findNearestFunctionLikeStart(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number,
    maxBack = 4096
): number {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - maxBack);

    for (let p = pos - 1; p >= start; p--) {
        // Function bodies frequently contain:
        // local declaration vector followed by local types.
        //
        // This is only a candidate marker.
        if (buf[p] === 0x00 && p + 1 < buf.length) {
            const next = buf[p + 1];

            if (
                next === 0x0b ||
                next === 0x20 ||
                next === 0x41 ||
                next === 0x02 ||
                next === 0x03 ||
                next === 0x04
            ) {
                return p;
            }
        }
    }

    return start;
}

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function u32le(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    if (p < 0 || p + 4 > buf.length) return 0;
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function dump(buf: Buffer, pos: number, radius = 48) {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
    const end = Math.min(buf.length, pos + radius + 16);

    console.log(
        `\n--- hit file=0x${hex(pos)} (${pos}) range=0x${hex(start)}..0x${hex(end)} ---`
    );

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < 16 && p + j < end; j++) {
            bytes.push(buf[p + j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

function findAll(buf: Buffer, pattern: Buffer): number[] {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i <= buf.length - pattern.length; i++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (ok) hits.push(i);
    }

    return hits;
}

function p32(n: number): Buffer {
    const b = Buffer.alloc(4);
    b.writeUInt32LE(n >>> 0, 0);
    return b;
}

function p16(n: number): Buffer {
    const b = Buffer.alloc(2);
    b.writeUInt16LE(n & 0xffff, 0);
    return b;
}

async function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" WASM SEGMENT 669 REFERENCE TRACE v22");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const root = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin";

    const candidates = [
        path.join(root, "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"),
        path.join(root, "emulator.wasm"),
    ];

    let wasmPath = "";

    for (const p of candidates) {
        if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
            wasmPath = p;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!wasmPath) {
        throw new Error("WASM file not found");
    }

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

    console.log(`\nWASM: ${wasmPath}`);
    console.log(`SIZE: ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    const addresses = [
        0x00195348,

    ];

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET VALUES 4788..4791");

    const values = [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791, 58423];

    for (const value of values) {
        const pattern = p32(value);
        const hits = findAll(wasm, pattern);

        console.log(
            `\nvalue=${value}  hex=0x${hex(value, 8)}  hits=${hits.length}`
        );

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 40)) {
            console.log(`  file=0x${hex(h)} (${h})`);
        }

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 10)) {
            dump(wasm, h, 32);
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" 16-BIT TARGET SEARCH");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const value of [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791]) {
        const pattern = p16(value);

        console.log(

        );

        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" NEARBY MEMORY-ADDRESS BYTE SEARCH");
    console.log("============================================================");

    // Search the low 3-byte forms as additional clues.
    for (const addr of [0x195348, 0x19534c, 0x195350, 0x195354, 0x195358]) {
        const full = p32(addr);
        const low3 = full.subarray(0, 3);

        const hits = findAll(wasm, low3);

        console.log(
            `addr=0x${hex(addr)} low3=${low3.toString("hex")} hits=${hits.length}`
        );

        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x37, 0xe4, 0x00, 0x00,
        0x04,
    ]);

    const segHits = findAll(wasm, segment669);

    console.log(`Segment 669 exact raw pattern hits: ${segHits.length}`);

    for (const h of segHits) {
        console.log(`  file=0x${hex(h)} (${h})`);
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no TON connection");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no signing");
}

main().catch((e) => {
