import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("TRACE SELECTOR 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

let pos = 8;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const section = wasm[pos++];

    const s = uleb(pos);
    pos = s.next;

    const end = pos + s.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        let p = pos;
        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {

            const b = uleb(p);
            const size = b.value;
            p = b.next;

            const start = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + size;

            let hits = [];

            for (let q = start; q < bodyEnd; q++) {

                if (wasm[q] !== 0x41) continue;

                try {
                    const r = uleb(q + 1);

                    if (r.value === TARGET) {
                        hits.push(q);
                    }
                } catch {}
            }

            if (hits.length) {

                const func = 83 + ordinal;

                console.log();
                console.log("-".repeat(72));
                console.log(`FUNCTION ${func}`);
                console.log(`BODY : ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
                console.log(`HITS : ${hits.length}`);
                console.log("-".repeat(72));

                for (const hit of hits) {

                    console.log(`SELECTOR AT ${hx(hit)}`);

                    const from = Math.max(start, hit - 24);
                    const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, hit + 80);

                    console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

                    let line = "";

                    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

                        line +=
                            wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

                        if ((q - from + 1) % 16 === 0) {
                            console.log(
                                `${hx(from + Math.floor(q - from + 1 - 16))}  ${line}`
                            );
                            line = "";
                        }
                    }

                    if (line.length) {
                        console.log(line);
                    }

                    console.log();
                    console.log("DECODED NEAR SELECTOR:");

                    let q = hit;

                    while (q < to) {

                        const op = wasm[q];

                        if (op === 0x41) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`
                            );

                            q = r.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x20) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  local.get ${r.value}`
                            );

                            q = r.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x21) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  local.set ${r.value}`
                            );

                            q = r.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x22) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  local.tee ${r.value}`
                            );

                            q = r.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x10) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  CALL FUNC ${r.value}`
                            );

                            q = r.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x11) {
                            const r = uleb(q + 1);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${r.value} table=${wasm[r.next]}`
                            );

                            q = r.next + 1;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {

                            const a = uleb(q + 1);
                            const o = uleb(a.next);

                            console.log(
                                `${hx(q)}  ${op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"} ` +
                                `offset=${hx(o.value)}`
                            );

                            q = o.next;
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (op === 0x6a) {
                            console.log(`${hx(q)}  i32.add`);
                        }

                        if (op === 0x6b) {
                            console.log(`${hx(q)}  i32.sub`);
                        }

                        if (op === 0x45) {
                            console.log(`${hx(q)}  i32.eqz`);
                        }

                        q++;
                    }
                }
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x0000eece, 0x00093e6b];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR 0x19534C - LOCAL CONTEXT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  const from = Math.max(0, target - 80);
  const to = Math.min(wasm.length, target + 120);

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TARGET OFFSET : 0x${target.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`WINDOW        : 0x${from.toString(16)} .. 0x${to.toString(16)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let p = from; p < to; p++) {

    const b = wasm[p];

    let extra = "";

    // i32.const
    if (b === 0x41) {
      let q = p + 1;
      let value = 0;
      let shift = 0;

      while (q < to) {
        const x = wasm[q++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
      }

      extra = ` i32.const=${value >>> 0} (0x${(value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
    }

    // local.get / set / tee
    if (b === 0x20 || b === 0x21 || b === 0x22) {
      let q = p + 1;
      let value = 0;
      let shift = 0;

      while (q < to) {
        const x = wasm[q++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
      }

      const name =
        b === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        b === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      extra = ` ${name} local=${value >>> 0}`;
    }

    // call
    if (b === 0x10) {
      let q = p + 1;
      let value = 0;
      let shift = 0;

      while (q < to) {
        const x = wasm[q++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
      }

      extra = ` call func=${value >>> 0}`;
    }

    // i32.store / i64.store / etc.
    if (b >= 0x36 && b <= 0x3e) {
      extra = " MEMORY_STORE";
    }

    // comparison
    if (b >= 0x45 && b <= 0x5f) {
      extra = " COMPARISON";
    }

    console.log(
      `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${extra}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\traceSelector5311.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;
const count = u();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE TYPE-1 INDIRECT SELECTORS");
console.log("TARGET TABLE INDEX : 5311");
console.log("TARGET FUNC        : 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
  const bodySize = u();
  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const groups = u();

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  const vals: string[] = [];

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const pos = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const v = u();
      vals.push(`CONST(${v})`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const l = u();
      vals.push(`L${l}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const l = u();
      vals.push(`TEE${l}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      u();
      const off = u();
      vals.push(`LOAD(+${off})`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const fn = u();
      vals.push(`CALL(${fn})`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = u();
      const table = u();

      if (type === 1 && table === 0) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
          `FUNC ${f + 91} @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log("STACK TAIL:");
        console.log(
          vals.slice(-12).join(" -> ")
        );
      }

      vals.length = Math.max(0, vals.length - 2);
      continue;
    }

    // common instructions which consume stack values
    if (
      op === 0x6a || // add
      op === 0x6b || // sub
      op === 0x6c || // mul
      op === 0x47 || // lt_u
      op === 0x46 || // eq
      op === 0x45    // eqz
    ) {
      vals.pop();
      vals.pop();
      vals.push("OP");
      continue;
    }

    // memory stores
    if (
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a ||
      op === 0x3b
    ) {
      u();
      u();
      vals.pop();
      vals.pop();
      continue;
    }

    // branches / blocks
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      u();
      continue;
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
      const l = u();
      vals.pop();
      continue;
    }

    // drop
    if (op === 0x1a) {
      vals.pop();
      continue;
    }

    // other constants
    if (op === 0x42) {
      u();
      vals.push("I64CONST");
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x195350,
    0x195351,
    0x195352,
    0x195353,
    0x195354,
    0x195355,
    0x195356,
    0x195357,
]);

const TARGET_START = 0x195350;
const TARGET_END = 0x195358;

const STORE_OPS = new Set([
    0x36, // i32.store
    0x37, // i64.store
    0x38, // f32.store
    0x39, // f64.store
    0x3a, // i32.store8
    0x3b, // i32.store16
    0x3c, // i64.store8
    0x3d, // i64.store16
    0x3e, // i64.store32
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

const IMPORTED = 83;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const funcSec = section(3);

let fp = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(fp);
    fp = x.next;
    functionTypes.push(x.value);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const codeSec = section(10);

let cp = codeSec.start;

const bc = uleb(cp);
cp = bc.next;

interface Body {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bc.value; ordinal++) {

    const size = uleb(cp);
    cp = size.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size.value;

    let p = start;

    const localDecls = uleb(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {

        const count = uleb(p);
        p = count.next;

        // valtype
        p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: p
    });

    cp = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// VALUE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Value =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number; origin: number }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number; origin: number }
    | { kind: "global"; index: number; origin: number }
    | { kind: "expr"; text: string; origin: number }
    | { kind: "unknown"; origin: number };

function unknown(origin: number): Value {
    return {
        kind: "unknown",
        origin
    };
}

function describe(v: Value): string {

    switch (v.kind) {

        case "const":
            return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;

        case "local":
            return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;

        case "global":
            return `GLOBAL(${v.index})`;

        case "expr":
            return v.text;

        default:
            return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

function isConst(v: Value): v is Extract<Value, {kind: "const"}> {
    return v.kind === "const";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// INTERESTING CONSTANT PAIRS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const interesting: {
    value: number;
    func: number;
    offset: number;
}[] = [];

let arithmeticMatches = 0;
let exactStores = 0;
let literalStoreValues = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR ADDRESS ARITHMETIC");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(
    `TARGET = ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END - 1)}`
);

console.log(
    "LOOKING FOR: CONST + ADD/SUB -> STORE ADDRESS"
);

console.log("");

for (const body of bodies) {

    let p = body.instrStart;

    const stack: Value[] = [];

    const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

    function pop(): Value {
        return stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : unknown(p);
    }

    function push(v: Value) {
        stack.push(v);
    }

    while (p < body.end) {

        const opOffset = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.const
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            const value = x.value >>> 0;

            push({
                kind: "const",
                value,
                origin: opOffset
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // local.get
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push(
                locals.get(x.value) ?? {
                    kind: "local",
                    index: x.value,
                    origin: opOffset
                }
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // local.set
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x21) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            locals.set(x.value, pop());

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // local.tee
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x22) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : unknown(opOffset);

            locals.set(x.value, v);

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // global.get
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x23) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push({
                kind: "global",
                index: x.value,
                origin: opOffset
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.add
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x6a) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (isConst(a) && isConst(b)) {

                push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: (a.value + b.value) >>> 0,
                    origin: opOffset
                });

            } else {

                push({
                    kind: "expr",
                    text: `(${describe(a)} + ${describe(b)})`,
                    origin: opOffset
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.sub
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x6b) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (isConst(a) && isConst(b)) {

                push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: (a.value - b.value) >>> 0,
                    origin: opOffset
                });

            } else {

                push({
                    kind: "expr",
                    text: `(${describe(a)} - ${describe(b)})`,
                    origin: opOffset
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // LOAD
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            const address = pop();

            // A load destroys the exact value provenance.
            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // STORE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (STORE_OPS.has(op)) {

            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const memOffset = uleb(p);
            p = memOffset.next;

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            // -----------------------------------------------
            // CASE 1: EXACT CONSTANT ADDRESS
            // -----------------------------------------------

            if (isConst(address)) {

                const effective =
                    (address.value + memOffset.value) >>> 0;

                if (
                    effective >= TARGET_START &&
                    effective < TARGET_END
                ) {

                    exactStores++;

                    console.log("");
                    console.log("============================================================");
                    console.log("EXACT ARITHMETIC TARGET STORE");
                    console.log("============================================================");

                    console.log(
                        `FUNC       = ${body.func}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `STORE      = ${hex(opOffset)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `BASE       = ${hex(address.value)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `MEM OFFSET = ${hex(memOffset.value)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `EFFECTIVE  = ${hex(effective)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `VALUE      = ${describe(value)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `VALUE SRC  = ${hex(value.origin)}`
                    );

                    console.log("");
                }
            }

            // -----------------------------------------------
            // CASE 2: symbolic arithmetic expression
            // -----------------------------------------------

            if (
                address.kind === "expr" &&
                (
                    address.text.includes("0x001953") ||
                    address.text.includes("LOCAL") ||
                    address.text.includes("GLOBAL")
                )
            ) {

                arithmeticMatches++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("POTENTIAL SYMBOLIC STORE");
                console.log("--------------------------------------------");
                console.log(
                    `FUNC       = ${body.func}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `STORE      = ${hex(opOffset)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `ADDRESS    = ${describe(address)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `OFFSET     = ${hex(memOffset.value)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `VALUE      = ${describe(value)}`
                );
                console.log("");
            }

            // -----------------------------------------------
            // CASE 3: literal selector value being stored
            // -----------------------------------------------

            if (
                isConst(value) &&
                (
                    value.value === 4789 ||
                    value.value === 4790
                )
            ) {

                literalStoreValues++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log("SELECTOR VALUE STORE");
                console.log("============================================================");

                console.log(
                    `FUNC       = ${body.func}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `STORE      = ${hex(opOffset)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `ADDRESS    = ${describe(address)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `OFFSET     = ${hex(memOffset.value)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `VALUE      = ${value.value}`
                );

                console.log("");
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CALL
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            // Unknown signature.
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CALL INDIRECT
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            // table index immediate
            p++;

            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // DROP
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1a) {

            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // SELECT
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1b) {

            pop();
            pop();
            pop();

            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // BRANCH / CONTROL
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03) {

            const blockType = wasm[p++];

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x04) {

            const blockType = wasm[p++];

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const n = uleb(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // COMPARISONS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x45 ||
            op === 0x46 ||
            op === 0x47 ||
            op === 0x48 ||
            op === 0x49 ||
            op === 0x4a ||
            op === 0x4b ||
            op === 0x4c ||
            op === 0x4d ||
            op === 0x4e ||
            op === 0x4f
        ) {

            if (op === 0x45) {
                pop();
            } else {
                pop();
                pop();
            }

            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32 unary
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x67 ||
            op === 0x68 ||
            op === 0x69
        ) {

            pop();

            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32 binary arithmetic
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op >= 0x6c &&
            op <= 0x78
        ) {

            pop();
            pop();

            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY SIZE / GROW
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {

            p++;

            push(
                unknown(opOffset)
            );

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // MISC PREFIXES
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            // Conservative reset.
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FINAL
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL CLASSIFICATION");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `EXACT TARGET STORES             = ${exactStores}`
);

console.log(
    `SYMBOLIC ADDRESS CANDIDATES     = ${arithmeticMatches}`
);

console.log(
    `LITERAL 4789/4790 VALUE STORES  = ${literalStoreValues}`
);

console.log("");

if (exactStores === 0) {

    console.log(
        "NO ARITHMETICALLY RESOLVED STORE TO 0x195350..0x195357."
    );
}

if (literalStoreValues === 0) {

    console.log(
        "NO DIRECT STORE OF LITERAL 4789/4790 FOUND."
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorBulkCallers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set([480, 1907, 6908]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function readBlockType(pos: number) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (b === 0x40)
        return { next: pos + 1 };

    if (
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c
    ) {
        return { next: pos + 1 };
    }

    return readSLEB(pos);
}

function parseCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next;

        const sectionStart = p;
        const sectionEnd = p + size.value;

        if (id !== 10) {
            p = sectionEnd;
            continue;
        }

        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        const bodies: {
            ordinal: number;
            start: number;
            end: number;
            codeStart: number;
        }[] = [];

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
            const bodySize = readULEB(p);
            p = bodySize.next;

            const start = p;
            const end = start + bodySize.value;

            const localCount = readULEB(p);
            p = localCount.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;

                p += 1;
            }

            bodies.push({
                ordinal,
                start,
                end,
                codeStart: p,
            });

            p = end;
        }

        return {
            start: sectionStart,
            end: sectionEnd,
            bodies,
        };
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = parseCodeSection();

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR BULK CALLERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(code.start)} .. ${hex(code.end)}`);
console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${code.bodies.length}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGET BULK FUNCTIONS");
console.log("  FUNC480");
console.log("  FUNC1907");
console.log("  FUNC6908");
console.log("============================================================");

let totalCalls = 0;

for (const body of code.bodies) {
    let p = body.codeStart;

    while (p < body.end) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // END
        if (op === 0x0b)
            break;

        // BLOCK / LOOP / IF
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            p = readBlockType(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // ELSE
        if (op === 0x05)
            continue;

        // BR / BR_IF
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // BR_TABLE
        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
                p = readULEB(p).next;

            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // RETURN
        if (op === 0x0f)
            continue;

        // CALL
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            if (TARGET_FUNCS.has(r.value)) {
                totalCalls++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log("DIRECT CALL TO BULK FUNCTION");
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log(`CALLER FUNC = ${body.ordinal}`);
                console.log(`CALL PC     = ${hex(pc)}`);
                console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${r.value}`);
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
            }

            continue;
        }

        // CALL_INDIRECT
        if (op === 0x11) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // LOCAL GET/SET/TEE
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // GLOBAL GET/SET
        if (
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // I32 CONST
        if (op === 0x41) {
            p = readSLEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // I64 CONST
        if (op === 0x42) {
            p = readSLEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // Memory loads
        if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x35
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // Memory stores
        if (
            op >= 0x36 &&
            op <= 0x3e
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        // memory.size / memory.grow
        if (
            op === 0x3f ||
            op === 0x40
        ) {
            p += 1;
            continue;
        }

        // Most numeric instructions have no immediates.
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DIRECT CALLS FOUND = ${totalCalls}`);

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION BODIES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const body of code.bodies) {
    if (!TARGET_FUNCS.has(body.ordinal))
        continue;

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${body.ordinal} | BODY ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorConstants.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const funcs = [6464, 6611, 6613, 6615, 6618, 7467, 7579];

const selectors = new Set([4788, 4789, 4790, 4791]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR CONSTANT / TABLE-INDEX TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of funcs) {
  console.log(`\nFUNC ${f}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  /*
   * Search raw function-code area for:
   *   i32.const selector
   *   i32.const 4788..4791
   *
   * This is intentionally conservative:
   * we are looking for literal selector values near
   * calls to FUNC 113.
   */

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 5; p++) {
    if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

    const x = uleb(p + 1);

    if (selectors.has(x.value)) {
      console.log(
        `CONST SELECTOR ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)}) @ ${hex(p)}`
      );
      hits++;
    }
  }

  if (!hits) {
    console.log("NO LITERAL SELECTOR CONSTANTS FOUND");
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TARGET MEMORY VALUES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [addr, name] of [
  [0x19534c, "DISPATCH_A"],
  [0x195350, "DISPATCH_B"],
  [0x195354, "DISPATCH_C"],
  [0x195358, "DISPATCH_D"],
]) {
  console.log(
    `${name} ${hex(addr)} = 0x${wasm.readUInt32LE(addr).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorDefs.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
  { f: 6611, call: 0x22c948, local: 6 },
  { f: 6611, call: 0x22c9ef, local: 2 },
  { f: 6613, call: 0x22cb34, local: 2 },
  { f: 6615, call: 0x22cc55, local: 1 },
  { f: 6615, call: 0x22cc5e, local: 1 },
  { f: 6618, call: 0x22d083, local: 1 },
  { f: 6464, call: 0x22838e, local: 11 },
];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, n = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    n++;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { v: v >>> 0, n };
}

function skip(p: number): number {
  const op = wasm[p];

  if ([0x20,0x21,0x22,0x0c,0x0d,0x10,0x41].includes(op))
    return 1 + uleb(p + 1).n;

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    const b = uleb(p + 1 + a.n);
    return 1 + a.n + b.n;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) return 3;

  return 1;
}

function findBodies() {
  const bodies: { f:number, start:number, end:number }[] = [];

  // Find all function-body starts using known code section structure.
  // Code section starts at 0x5ae, body data starts after count.
  let p = 0x5ae;

  const sectionId = wasm[p];
  if (sectionId !== 3) throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

  const secSize = uleb(p + 1);
  let q = p + 1 + secSize.n + secSize.v;

  // Function section gives type indices; skip it.
  const count = uleb(q);
  q += count.n;

  // Code section search.
  for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length - 5; i++) {
    if (wasm[i] === 10) {
      const s = uleb(i + 1);
      const start = i + 1 + s.n;
      const c = uleb(start);
      if (c.v === 9398) {
        p = start + c.n;
        for (let f = 0; f < 9398; f++) {
          const bs = uleb(p);
          const bodyStart = p + bs.n;
          bodies.push({
            f: f + 83,
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyStart + bs.v
          });
          p = bodyStart + bs.v;
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT SELECTOR LOCAL DEFINITIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
  const body = bodies.find(x => x.f === t.f);
  if (!body) continue;

  console.log(`\nFUNC ${t.f}  CALL113 @ 0x${t.call.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`TARGET LOCAL = ${t.local}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < Math.min(body.end, t.call)) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);

      if (x.v === t.local) {
        console.log(`\nDEF @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);

        const s = Math.max(body.start, p - 20);
        let z = s;

        while (z < Math.min(body.end, p + 25)) {
          const o = wasm[z];

          if (o === 0x20) {
            const a = uleb(z + 1);
            console.log(`  0x${z.toString(16)} local.get ${a.v}`);
            z += 1 + a.n;
          } else if (o === 0x41) {
            const a = uleb(z + 1);
            console.log(
              `  0x${z.toString(16)} i32.const ${a.v} (0x${a.v.toString(16)})`
            );
            z += 1 + a.n;
          } else if (o === 0x21 || o === 0x22) {
            const a = uleb(z + 1);
            console.log(
              `  0x${z.toString(16)} ${o === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee"} ${a.v}`
            );
            z += 1 + a.n;
          } else if (o >= 0x28 && o <= 0x3e) {
            const a = uleb(z + 1);
            const b = uleb(z + 1 + a.n);
            console.log(
              `  0x${z.toString(16)} memop 0x${o.toString(16)} offset=${b.v}`
            );
            z += 1 + a.n + b.n;
          } else {
            const names: Record<number,string> = {
              0x6a:"i32.add",
              0x6b:"i32.sub",
              0x6c:"i32.mul",
              0x71:"i32.and",
              0x72:"i32.or",
              0x73:"i32.xor",
              0x74:"i32.shl",
              0x75:"i32.shr_s",
              0x76:"i32.shr_u"
            };
            console.log(`  0x${z.toString(16)} ${names[o] ?? `opcode 0x${o.toString(16)}`}`);
            z++;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    p += skip(p);
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorInFunctions.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([6464, 6611, 6613, 6615, 6618, 7467, 7579]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Parse WASM sections and obtain exact function body ranges.
function getCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function getDefinedFunctionBodies() {
  const code = getCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: { func: number; start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  // Defined function #0 corresponds to global function index 83.
  const FIRST_DEFINED = 83;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + s.value;

    const func = FIRST_DEFINED + i;

    if (TARGETS.has(func)) {
      bodies.push({
        func,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR TRACE INSIDE EXACT FUNCTION BODIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const bodies = getDefinedFunctionBodies();

for (const f of bodies) {
  console.log(`\nFUNC ${f.func}`);
  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(f.start)} .. ${hex(f.end)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = f.start; p < f.end; p++) {

    // i32.const
    if (wasm[p] === 0x41) {
      const x = readULEB(p + 1);

      if (
        x.value === 4788 ||
        x.value === 4789 ||
        x.value === 4790 ||
        x.value === 4791
      ) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(p)}  i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`
        );
        hits++;
      }
    }

    // call
    if (wasm[p] === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p + 1);

      if (x.value === 113) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(p)}  CALL 113`
        );
        hits++;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!hits) {
    console.log("NO TARGET SELECTOR / CALL113");
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorLoads.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x001226d;
const END   = 0x001295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 - LOAD / SELECTOR DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",
  0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
  0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
  0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
};

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
  const op = wasm[p];

  if (!(op in names)) continue;

  let q = p + 1;

  // memarg alignment
  let shift = 0;
  while (q < END) {
    const b = wasm[q++];
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    if (++shift > 5) break;
  }

  // memarg offset
  let offset = 0;
  shift = 0;

  while (q < END) {
    const b = wasm[q++];
    offset |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) break;
  }

  const from = Math.max(START, p - 12);
  const to = Math.min(END, q + 12);

  const raw = [...wasm.subarray(from, to)]
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log("");
  console.log(`OFFSET : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`OPCODE : 0x${op.toString(16)} ${names[op]}`);
  console.log(`ALIGN  : ${shift}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET : 0x${(offset >>> 0).toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RAW    : ${raw}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
